Cardamom Almond Syrup (Pax & Beneficia Style)

Ingredients
  • 25 green cardamom pods
  • 1 cup (240 milliliters) water
  • 3/4 cup (150 grams) granulated sugar
  • 1/4 cup (50 grams) packed light brown sugar
  • 1 teaspoon almond extract
  • 1/2 teaspoon vanilla extract
Instructions
  1. In a mortar and pestle or on a cutting board with the side of a knife, lightly crush the cardamom pods until the seeds are exposed.
  2. Place a medium saucepan over medium heat.
  3. Once hot, add the crushed cardamom and toast, stirring often, until fragrant, 1-2 minutes.
  4. Carefully pour in the water, then stir in the granulated sugar and brown sugar.
  5. Bring the mixture to a simmer and cook, stirring occasionally, until the liquid slightly thickens and all the sugar has dissolved, about 10-15 minutes.
  6. Remove the pan from heat and stir in the almond and vanilla extracts.
  7. Cover the saucepan and set aside at room temperature for 30 minutes to allow the flavors to blend.
  8. Strain the syrup through a fine mesh sieve into a jar. Cool to room temperature, then refrigerate for up to 2 weeks. The flavors are even better after a day or two.

Crispy Hot Honey Feta Chicken

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 1/3 cup / 110g honey
  • 1 1/2 tsp hot sauce (Frank's or Cholula)
  • 3/4 tsp chilli flakes
  • 2 x 250g/9oz chicken breasts
  • 2 tsp paprika
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1/2 tsp each: onion powder, garlic powder, dried oregano, black pepper
  • 1/4 tsp cayenne pepper
  • 35g / 1/4 cup plain flour
  • 2 eggs, beaten
  • 65g / 1 cup panko breadcrumbs
  • Veg oil, as needed (6-8 tbsp)
  • 200g / 7oz feta, crumbled
Instructions
  1. Combine the honey, hot sauce and chilli flakes in a bowl and warm in the microwave (20 seconds or so). Stir then leave to cool. You can also do this on the stove in a small pot (bring to a simmer then remove and cool).
  2. Horizontally slice the chicken breasts right through the centre to create 4 even-sized breasts. In a small pot or bowl combine the paprika, salt, onion powder, garlic powder, oregano, cayenne and black pepper.
  3. Line up 3 large shallow dishes and place a large tray at the end. First bowl: flour + half the seasoning. Second: beaten egg. Third: panko with the rest of the seasoning. Dredge each chicken piece through flour, into egg, then breadcrumbs, and place on the tray, coating thoroughly at each stage.
  4. Add enough oil to coat the base of a large pan over medium-high heat. Once a breadcrumb rapidly sizzles, add the chicken (work in 2 batches if needed, topping up oil between). Fry 3-4 minutes per side until deep golden and crispy outside, white and piping hot through the centre. Rest on a wire rack.
  5. Add the crumbled feta, covering as much of the chicken as possible, then place under a high grill until the feta starts turning golden (buttery soft, slightly crisp on top).
  6. Serve with your preferred amount of hot honey drizzled over. Tightly store leftover honey in the fridge.

Lemon Garlic Salmon Pasta

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 8 oz uncooked pasta (rigatoni works well)
  • 1 lb fresh salmon
  • Salt & pepper, to taste
  • 2 tbsp butter
  • 3 cloves garlic, minced
  • 1/2 tsp Italian seasoning
  • 1/4 cup dry white wine
  • Juice and zest of 1/2 medium lemon (about 1 tbsp juice)
  • 1 cup heavy/whipping cream
  • 1/2 tsp cornstarch
  • 1/2 cup freshly grated parmesan cheese, plus extra for serving
  • 2 cups (packed) fresh baby spinach
Instructions
  1. Boil a salted pot of water and cook the pasta al dente according to package directions.
  2. Meanwhile, season the salmon with salt & pepper.
  3. Add the butter to a skillet over medium-high heat. Once hot, add the salmon and cook 2 minutes/side, then transfer to a plate (do not fully cook).
  4. Reduce heat to medium. Add garlic, Italian seasoning, wine, and lemon juice/zest to the skillet and let bubble about 30 seconds.
  5. Stir in the cream.
  6. In a small bowl, mix the cornstarch with 1 teaspoon cold water and stir it into the skillet.
  7. Once the sauce starts bubbling, add the salmon and parmesan. Break the fish into bite-size pieces with your spoon. Cook a few minutes until salmon is cooked through and sauce thickens.
  8. Stir in the spinach and let it wilt. Add hot pasta water if the sauce gets too thick.
  9. Drain the pasta, add to the skillet, toss with the sauce. Season with more salt & pepper as needed. Serve with extra parmesan.

Orange Blossom Syrup (bergamot + vanilla)

Serves ~8 oz
Ingredients
  • 1 1/2 cups granulated sugar
  • 3/4 cup water
  • 1 tablespoon lemon juice (optional)
  • 1-2 tsp orange blossom water (or bergamot extract + vanilla to taste)
Instructions
  1. In a small heavy saucepan, combine the sugar, water and lemon juice (if using) and bring to a boil over medium-high heat.
  2. Reduce to low heat and simmer for 5 minutes.
  3. Remove from heat and stir in the orange blossom water (and vanilla/bergamot, to taste).
  4. Pour the hot syrup into a heatproof container and let it cool completely. Store in an airtight jar; keeps ~2-3 weeks refrigerated. Use in cold brew, iced tea, lemonade, or a gin & tonic.

Quick Korean Beef Rice Bowl (Weeknight Bibimbap)

Ingredients
  • 500 g (1 lb) beef mince (ground beef), any fat % you like
  • 1 tbsp canola oil or other plain-flavoured oil, plus 2 tsp for wilting spinach
  • 1 tbsp onion, finely grated (~1/4 onion) (bulgogi sauce)
  • 3 tbsp red apple, finely grated, skin on (~1/2 apple) (bulgogi sauce)
  • 3 garlic cloves, finely grated (bulgogi sauce)
  • 1 tsp ginger, finely grated (bulgogi sauce)
  • 2 1/2 tbsp soy sauce, all-purpose or light (bulgogi sauce)
  • 1 tbsp sesame oil (bulgogi sauce)
  • 1 tbsp mirin, omit for no alcohol (bulgogi sauce)
  • 1 tbsp brown sugar (bulgogi sauce)
  • 1/8 tsp black pepper (bulgogi sauce)
  • 2 tbsp soy sauce (rice bowl drizzle sauce)
  • 1 tbsp sesame oil (rice bowl drizzle sauce)
  • 2 tsp brown sugar (rice bowl drizzle sauce)
  • 2 tsp rice vinegar or apple cider vinegar (rice bowl drizzle sauce)
  • White rice (medium or short grain if you can)
  • 4 large handfuls baby spinach or similar chopped leafy greens (cabbage, kale)
  • 1 large carrot, peeled and julienned or grated
  • 1 avocado, mashed
  • 1/4 cup pickled ginger, pink if you can for colour
  • 1 1/2 cups baby cos/romaine lettuce, torn into bite-size pieces
  • 1 tsp white sesame seeds
  • 1 green onion, finely sliced
Instructions
  1. Mix the Bulgogi sauce ingredients in one bowl, and the rice bowl drizzle sauce ingredients in a separate bowl.
  2. Wilt the spinach: heat 2 teaspoons of oil in a large non-stick pan over high heat. Add half the spinach and toss with tongs until semi-wilted (~30 seconds), then add the rest and toss until just wilted (~1 minute). Remove into a bowl.
  3. Cook the bulgogi beef: add 1 tablespoon of oil to the same pan, still on high heat. Add the beef and cook, breaking it up as you go, until you no longer see raw beef. Pour in the Bulgogi sauce and let it simmer rapidly, stirring every now and then, until the sauce is mostly evaporated. Then stir regularly until the beef gets a bit of caramelisation but is still moist from the sauce.
  4. Assemble: spoon the beef over steamed rice, and place the wilted spinach and carrot alongside. Top with pickled ginger and a dollop of mashed avocado. Tuck the lettuce down the side, sprinkle with sesame seeds and green onion, and serve with the drizzle sauce on the side to pour over everything before jumbling it all up and digging in.

Single-Serve Lemon Chicken Orzo Soup

Ingredients
  • ~1 cup shredded chicken (I used rotisserie)
  • ¼ leek (chopped)
  • 2 cloves garlic (sliced)
  • 2 baby potatoes (chopped)
  • ¼ cup peas
  • 2 tbsp orzo
  • Lemon zest + juice
  • Dill
  • 2 tbsp White wine
  • 2 cups Chicken broth
  • Olive oil
  • Salt & pepper
Instructions
  1. In a pot over medium heat, add a splash of olive oil and then the chopped leek, garlic and potatoes. Sautée for about 4-5 minutes, stirring occasionally. You want to get a slight golden colour but not burn anything here. Season with salt & pepper.
  2. Add in the orzo, stir for another minute and lightly toast, then deglaze with the white wine and add in the broth. Bring to a slight bubble and let simmer over low-medium for about 10 minutes as the potato and orzo become tender. If using fresh peas, add these in during the last 4-5 minutes. If using frozen peas, add these in the last 2 minutes.
  3. Once the potatoes are tender and the orzo is cooked, stir in the shredded chicken and then finish with lemon juice to taste.
  4. Garnish with lemon zest, fresh dill and an optional drizzle of olive oil.

Tiramisu Overnight Oats

Ingredients
  • 1 cup oats (quick oats or old fashioned oats)
  • 3 tbsp unsweetened cocoa powder + more to decorate
  • 1½ tbsp chia seeds
  • Pinch of salt
  • 3 tbsp espresso or brewed coffee (equiv. 2 espresso shots), cooled
  • 1¼ cups unsweetened almond milk (or other non-dairy milk, like soy)
  • 2½ tbsp pure maple syrup
  • 1¼ tsp vanilla extract
  • 1 cup coconut yogurt (or yogurt of choice)
Instructions
  1. In a large bowl, mix together oats, cocoa powder, chia seeds and salt.
  2. Add espresso (or other coffee), milk, 2 tbsp maple syrup and 1 tsp vanilla (reserve remaining maple syrup and vanilla for later). Mix together thoroughly.
  3. Pour into airtight container and refrigerate 4-8 hours.
  4. To make mascarpone, add coconut yogurt and remaining maple syrup and vanilla extract to a small bowl. Stir to combine.
  5. Remove overnight oats from fridge, and divide half the mixture between 2 glass or mason jars. Follow this with a layer of 'mascarpone', then repeat once more (so you have two layers of each in both jars).
  6. Sift over cocoa powder, and decorate with coffee beans if desired.

Xà Lách Bò Xào (Vietnamese Sautéed Beef Salad)

Ingredients
  • 1 lb Ribeye, Thinly Sliced
  • 1 tbsp Oil
  • 1/2 White Onion
  • 1 Bundle Green Leaf Lettuce
  • 1 Cucumber
  • 5 oz Cherry Tomatoes
  • Marinade:
  • 1 tbsp Oyster Sauce
  • 1 tbsp Dark Soy Sauce
  • 1/2 tbsp Sugar
  • 1/2 tsp MSG
  • 1 tbsp Oil
  • 1/4 tsp Black Pepper
  • Dressing:
  • 1 tbsp Fish Sauce
  • 1/2 tbsp Sugar
  • 2 tbsp Water
  • 2 tbsp White Vinegar
  • 1/2 tbsp Minced Garlic
  • 1 Thai Chili, Chopped
  • 2 tbsp Oil
Instructions
  1. Marinate the beef: Start by thinly slicing the ribeye against the grain into thin slices. (Pro tip: freeze your beef for about an hour beforehand. This firms up the meat, making it much easier to slice.) Once sliced, marinate the beef with oyster sauce, dark soy sauce, sugar, MSG, black pepper, and oil. Let the beef marinate in the fridge, covered, for 10-15 minutes.
  2. Prepwork: Wash the lettuce thoroughly, then dry it completely using a salad spinner or clean towel. Chop the leaves roughly into bite-sized pieces. (A little bit bigger than average salad pieces, as it will wilt down later.)
  3. Cut the cucumber in half lengthwise, then slice on a slight diagonal to create angled half-moon shapes. Slice the cherry tomatoes in half.
  4. Cut the onion in half, and then slice along the grain into thin strips.
  5. Make the fish sauce vinaigrette: In a small bowl, combine the fish sauce, sugar, water, white vinegar, minced garlic, and chopped Thai chili. Slowly drizzle in the oil while whisking continuously until the mixture becomes fully emulsified.
  6. Cook the beef: Heat a pan over high heat and add a bit of oil. Once the pan is hot, sear the marinated beef in batches if needed to avoid overcrowding. Let it sit undisturbed for about a minute to develop a nice caramelized crust.
  7. Then, add in the sliced onions and toss everything together until the beef is just cooked through and the onions have softened slightly.
  8. Put it all together and serve: In a large mixing bowl, add the chopped lettuce, cucumbers, cherry tomatoes, and fish sauce vinaigrette. Add the hot beef and onions directly over the salad, then gently toss everything together and serve immediately while the beef is still warm. Enjoy!

Air Fryer Chicken Tikka with Spiced Rice

Ingredients
  • 400 g boneless skinless chicken thighs (serves 2)
  • Greek yoghurt (for marinade and raita)
  • Tikka masala paste
  • Microwave rice (or cooked basmati rice)
  • 2 lemons
  • 2 red onions
  • Vine tomatoes (for the tomato salad)
  • 1 green chilli
  • Nigella seeds
  • Olive oil
  • 1 cucumber (for the raita)
  • Fresh mint (for the raita and garnish)
  • Salt and black pepper
Instructions
  1. Finely slice the red onions. Add half of the sliced onions to a bowl with the juice of 1/4 of the lemons and season with salt. Leave to quickly pickle while you prep the rest of the dish. Cut 1/3 of the remaining lemon into wedges and set aside for garnish.
  2. Slice the chicken thighs into three pieces each. Add to a bowl with the juice of half the remaining lemon, the yoghurt and the tikka paste. Season generously with salt and pepper, then mix well. Cover and set aside in the fridge for at least 30 minutes.
  3. Meanwhile, make the raita: halve the cucumber lengthways, scoop out the seeds and finely dice it. Finely chop the mint. Add the yoghurt to a bowl and squeeze in the juice of the remaining lemon. Add the cucumber and mint, season with salt and pepper, and set aside.
  4. Add the rest of the onions to the air fryer, drizzle with a glug of olive oil and top with the chicken pieces, then add another small drizzle of oil. Set the air fryer to 200°C and cook for 14-16 minutes until charred and the chicken juices run clear.
  5. Finely slice the green chilli and cut the tomato into wedges. Add the tomatoes to a bowl along with the nigella seeds, then season with salt and pepper.
  6. Cook the rice according to package instructions.
  7. Remove the chicken from the air fryer, then toss the cooked onions with the rice. Season with salt and pepper.
  8. Divide the rice between two plates, add the tomatoes, then top with the tikka chicken and pickled onions. Scatter with the green chilli, lemon wedges and more mint, if you like.

Arrachera Carne Asada Tacos

Ingredients
  • 2 lb skirt steak (arrachera)
  • Juice of 2 limes
  • Juice of 1 orange
  • 3 cloves garlic, minced
  • 1/2 cup fresh cilantro, chopped
  • 1/3 cup olive oil
  • 6 oz light beer
  • 1 tsp kosher salt (for marinade)
  • Kosher salt to taste (for seasoning before grilling)
  • Corn tortillas
  • Quesillo (Oaxaca cheese), for searing
  • Diced onion, cilantro, and lime juice, for serving
  • Salsa verde tatemada (see CasitaMade.com/post/salsa-verde-tatemada)
Instructions
  1. Marinate: Combine the lime juice, orange juice, garlic, cilantro, olive oil, beer, and salt in a bowl or Ziploc bag. Add the steak and coat evenly. Marinate 2–4 hours in the refrigerator, up to 6 hours.
  2. Grill: Preheat the grill to high heat. Immediately before grilling, season the steak with kosher salt. Remove from the marinade and pat lightly to remove excess moisture. Grill 2–4 minutes per side until medium-rare to medium.
  3. Slice: Rest 5–10 minutes, then slice thinly against the grain.
  4. Serve: Serve with warm corn tortillas (optionally with seared quesillo), grilled onions, salsa tatemada, and lime wedges.

Cardamom Vanilla Latte Syrup

Ingredients
  • 1 cup (200g) granulated sugar
  • 1 cup (237ml) water
  • 20-25 green cardamom pods (or 2 teaspoons ground cardamom, see notes)
  • 1 teaspoon (5ml) pure vanilla extract (optional)
  • pinch of salt (optional)
Instructions
  1. If using whole cardamom pods, crush the pods in a mortar and pestle or with the flat side of a knife. They should be open with the seeds inside visible.
  2. Bring the water and cardamom to a boil in a small saucepan over medium-high heat.
  3. Once boiling, remove from heat and let sit for 15-20 minutes to allow the cardamom to steep into the hot water.
  4. Add the sugar and bring back up to medium heat, stirring until the sugar dissolves completely.
  5. Remove from heat and add the vanilla and salt (if using). Let the syrup cool completely, then strain through a fine-mesh sieve into an airtight container or bottle. Store in the fridge for up to 2 weeks; it can also be frozen in ice cube trays.

Chicken Shawarma Sheet Pan Dinner

Ingredients
  • 3 pounds boneless, skinless chicken thighs, sliced into 1/2-inch strips
  • 2 tablespoons plain Greek yogurt
  • 3 tablespoons olive oil
  • 5-6 cloves garlic, pressed or finely minced
  • 2 teaspoons kosher salt
  • 1/2 teaspoon black pepper
  • 1 1/2 teaspoons ground cumin
  • 1 1/2 teaspoons smoked paprika (regular paprika in a pinch)
  • 1 1/2 teaspoons turmeric
  • 1 teaspoon ground coriander
  • 1/2 teaspoon ground cinnamon
  • 1 cup plain Greek yogurt, at least 2% fat (yogurt sauce)
  • 2 tablespoons mayonnaise (yogurt sauce)
  • 1 clove garlic, grated with a microplane (yogurt sauce)
  • 2 tablespoons lemon juice (yogurt sauce)
  • 1/2 teaspoon kosher salt, more to taste (yogurt sauce)
  • 1/2 teaspoon dried dill, or about 1 teaspoon fresh minced (yogurt sauce)
  • 2 tablespoons fresh lemon juice (honey lemon drizzle)
  • 1 tablespoon honey (honey lemon drizzle)
Instructions
  1. Pat the chicken completely dry and trim off any excess fat (kitchen shears work great for this). Slice the thighs into 1/2-inch strips and place in a large mixing bowl.
  2. Add the 2 tablespoons yogurt, olive oil, garlic, salt, pepper, cumin, paprika, turmeric, coriander and cinnamon to the bowl with the chicken. Toss well to coat.
  3. Cover the bowl and place in the fridge. Marinate for at least 30 minutes, but preferably 1 hour or longer.
  4. While the chicken marinates, whisk together the yogurt sauce: combine 1 cup yogurt, mayonnaise, garlic, lemon juice, salt and dill. Add a tiny bit of water until it reaches a good drizzling consistency, add more salt to taste if needed, and store in the fridge.
  5. Preheat the oven to 425°F. Use convection mode if you have it.
  6. Line a rimmed sheet pan with parchment if desired. Spread the marinated chicken evenly in a single layer, avoiding overcrowding.
  7. Roast for 25-35 minutes, stirring halfway through, until the chicken is browned and cooked through. If needed, broil briefly at the end for extra color and browning.
  8. Place the honey in a small bowl and warm for 10 seconds in the microwave. Whisk in the lemon juice. When the chicken is done, pour the honey-lemon mixture over it and toss.
  9. Serve the chicken in warm pitas or naan with toppings, over a salad, or in rice bowls, with the yogurt sauce drizzled on top.

Chili Garlic Chicken Fried Rice

Ingredients
  • 1360g (48oz) chicken thighs, sliced in half
  • 60g (4 Tbsp) chili garlic sauce
  • 30g (2 Tbsp) soy sauce
  • 60g (3 Tbsp) honey
  • 1 Tbsp rice vinegar
  • 1 Tbsp minced ginger
  • Salt & pepper to taste
  • 150g (⅔ cup) 0% skyr or 0% Greek yogurt
  • 45g (3 Tbsp) light mayo
  • 35g (2 Tbsp) gochujang
  • 30g (2 Tbsp) sriracha
  • Splash of rice vinegar
  • 1400g (7 cups) day-old cooked rice (600g {3 cups} uncooked weight)
  • 60g (4 Tbsp) soy sauce
  • 30g (2 Tbsp) fish sauce
  • 60g (3 Tbsp) honey
  • 30g (2 Tbsp) rice vinegar
  • 30g (2 Tbsp) sesame oil
  • 600g (4 cups) mixed frozen veggies of choice
  • Chopped green onions
  • 30g (2 Tbsp) sesame oil (post-cook mix)
  • 400g (1 ⅔ cups) egg whites
  • 6 eggs
Instructions
  1. If using day-old rice, cook the rice the day before and let it rest in the fridge until making this recipe.
  2. Slice the chicken thighs in half and add them to the slow cooker. Pour in chili garlic sauce, soy sauce, honey, rice vinegar, minced ginger, salt, and pepper. Mix to coat evenly, cover, and cook on high for 2–3 hrs or on low for 3–4 hrs until tender. Use tongs to break up the chicken when done.
  3. Spread day-old (or pre-cooked) rice on a large sheet pan. In a bowl, mix soy sauce, fish sauce, honey, rice vinegar, and sesame oil, then pour over the rice and toss to coat.
  4. Preheat the oven to 475°F, then broil the rice for 10–25 min, checking every 5 min, until crispy and browned. Remove from the oven, scrape the edges, add frozen veggies on top, and broil for another 10–15 min until the veggies are cooked. Transfer rice and veggies to the slow cooker and mix with the cooked chicken. Add egg whites and eggs to the same pan and broil for 3–4 min or until set. Chop eggs and add to the slow cooker with remaining sesame oil and chopped green onions. Stir well.
  5. For the spicy mayo, mix 0% skyr or Greek yogurt, light mayo, gochujang, sriracha, rice vinegar, salt, and pepper, adding water to reach your desired consistency. Drizzle or mix into the fried rice.
  6. Divide into 10 servings, allow to cool, and store leftovers in freezer-safe containers.

Coconut Soaked Lime Cake

Ingredients
  • 1 3/4 cups (225g) all-purpose flour, spooned and leveled
  • 1 tbsp cornstarch
  • 3/4 tsp baking soda
  • 1/2 tsp kosher salt
  • 1 1/4 cup (250g) granulated sugar
  • Zest of 6 limes, divided
  • 1/2 cup (112g) unsalted butter, room temp
  • 3 tbsp coconut oil (refined or unrefined), room temp
  • 3 large eggs
  • 1 tsp vanilla extract
  • 1/4 cup (60g) lime juice (about 3 to 4 of the zest limes)
  • 13.6 oz can full fat coconut milk, divided
  • 14 oz can sweetened condensed milk, divided
  • 1/2 tsp coconut extract
  • 2 cups (480g) heavy whipping cream, cold
  • 1/2 tsp coconut extract
  • Sweetened coconut flakes, for topping
Instructions
  1. Preheat the oven to 350F and grease a 9×13 baking dish.
  2. In a medium bowl, whisk together the flour, cornstarch, baking soda, and salt. Set aside.
  3. In a large mixing bowl using a hand or stand mixer with the paddle attachment, add the sugar and zest of 3 limes. Using your fingers, rub the zest into the sugar to release even more flavor.
  4. Now add the butter and coconut oil and mix until creamy and well combined, about 1 minute. Mix in the eggs and vanilla until completely blended, about 30 seconds.
  5. Dump in the dry ingredients and mix. About halfway through, pour in the lime juice and 1 cup (240g) coconut milk and continue to mix until smooth and combined. At the very end, switch to a silicone spatula to mix by hand to ensure everything is evenly mixed.
  6. Spread the thin batter in the prepared pan and bake for 25 to 30 minutes or until evenly puffed and the center springs back when lightly tapped.
  7. While the cake bakes, combine the coconut soak ingredients in a pourable container: remaining coconut milk from the can (a little over 1/2 cup), 1/2 cup (120g) sweetened condensed milk, zest of 2 limes, coconut extract, and 2 tablespoons of water. Use a fork to beat until smooth and fluid.
  8. When the cake is fresh from the oven, use a skewer or fork to prick holes all throughout the cake. Pour the soak evenly over the cake and wait a couple minutes for it to disappear and sink in. Transfer to the refrigerator uncovered for about 1 hour to cool completely before topping (if you plan to keep the cake in the refrigerator for longer, cover with plastic wrap after it has completely cooled).
  9. Using a hand or stand mixer with the whisk attachment, whip the heavy cream on high speed just until it ever-so-slightly starts to thicken. Then pour in the rest of the can of sweetened condensed milk along with the coconut extract and whip until it reaches soft peaks.
  10. Spread the smooth whipped cream over the cooled cake and top with coconut flakes and zest of the remaining 1 lime.
  11. When slicing and serving the cake, the soak can make the bottom a little bit sticky so I suggest using a small spatula to lift out the slices. Enjoy!

Creamy Cajun Chicken Orzo

Ingredients
  • 1.5 lbs boneless, skinless chicken thighs
  • 1 tbsp cajun seasoning
  • salt & pepper (to taste)
  • 1 tbsp olive oil (or avocado oil)
  • ½ jumbo yellow onion (diced (about 1 cup))
  • 1 green bell pepper (diced)
  • 3 cloves garlic (minced)
  • 1 tbsp tomato paste ((optional))
  • 1 tsp cajun seasoning
  • salt & pepper (to taste)
  • 1 cup dry orzo
  • 2 cups chicken broth (or chicken bone broth for extra protein)
  • ½ cup heavy cream (*)
  • 2 oz fresh spinach
  • juice from ½ lemon
Instructions
  1. Pat chicken thighs dry and then season both sides with cajun seasoning, salt & pepper. Bring a large pan to medium high heat. Once hot, add olive oil. Then, add seasoned chicken. Sear for 4-5 minutes on each side. Remove chicken from the pan and set aside.
  2. Reduce heat to medium. If there is plenty of excess oil remaining in the pan, just use that. If not, add a bit more olive oil. Then, add onion, bell pepper, salt & pepper. Sauté, stirring occasionally, for about 3 minutes.
  3. Add tomato paste and garlic, stir, and let cook for 2 more minutes. Then add cajun seasoning and stir to combine.
  4. Next, add orzo. Let it toast, stirring occasionally, for about 3 minutes. Then, deglaze the pan with chicken broth, scraping up any browned bits at the bottom of the pan.
  5. Nestle the chicken in the orzo, making sure it's mostly submerged. If not, add a bit more broth. Then, reduce the heat to low and let it simmer for about 15 minutes, or until the chicken is done through and the orzo is tender. I like to flip my chicken halfway through.
  6. Turn off the heat. Temporarily remove the chicken from the pan. Stir in the heavy cream, spinach and lemon juice. Taste and adjust salt, pepper & cajun seasoning as needed.
  7. Once the spinach has wilted slightly, add back in the chicken, top with parsley if desired, and enjoy!

Crockpot Red Beans

Ingredients
  • 1 lb. dried red beans
  • 1 lb. Andouille sausage
  • 4 cloves garlic (minced)
  • 1 large yellow onion (chopped)
  • 1 green bell pepper (chopped)
  • ½ teaspoon red pepper flakes (or less, if feeding kids)
  • 1 teaspoon kosher salt
  • 32 oz. unsalted chicken broth
  • Cooked white rice (for serving)
Instructions
  1. Sort the beans. Remove any broken ones, as well as any plant bits or rocks. Give them a rinse.
  2. Place the beans in the basin of the slow cooker.
  3. Chop the onion and bell pepper. Mince the garlic. Sprinkle them on top of the red beans in the slow cooker.
  4. Season with the red pepper flakes and kosher salt.
  5. Slice the (smoked) Andouille sausage*, and place on top of the other ingredients in the slow cooker.
  6. Give the ingredients a stir to combine them, then pour in the chicken stock.
  7. Cover, and cook on HIGH for 5-6 hours (on LOW for 9-10 hours, though please check it about halfway through and then at the 7-8-hour mark to ensure that it does not run out of liquid and burn), or until the red beans are extremely soft.
  8. Serve warm over rice!

Cơm Gà Hội An (Vietnamese Chicken & Rice)

Ingredients
  • 1 whole free-range chicken (or 1 kg bone-in thighs)
  • 1 thumb-sized piece of ginger, smashed
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 3 cups jasmine rice
  • 1 tbsp turmeric powder
  • 2 tbsp chicken fat or neutral oil
  • 3 1/4 cups chicken broth (from poaching)
  • 3 pandan leaves, tied in a knot
  • 2 thin slices ginger
  • 1 onion, thinly sliced
  • 1 bunch mixed herbs (Vietnamese coriander, Thai basil, cilantro, spring onions)
  • Juice of 1 lime
  • Salt and freshly ground black pepper
  • 2 tbsp fish sauce
  • 1 tbsp lime juice
  • 1 tsp sugar
  • 1 clove garlic, minced
  • 1 small chili, sliced
Instructions
  1. Place the chicken in a pot with water, ginger, and salt. Simmer gently for 30–40 minutes. Remove and let cool. Shred the meat and set aside. Reserve the broth.
  2. Rinse the rice. In a pan, sauté the turmeric in chicken fat, add the rice, and stir well. Transfer to a rice cooker, add the chicken broth, pandan leaves, and ginger. Cook until fluffy.
  3. Toss the shredded chicken with the onion, herbs, lime juice, salt, and pepper.
  4. Mix the fish sauce, lime juice, sugar, garlic, and chili until the sugar dissolves.
  5. Scoop the turmeric rice onto plates, top with the chicken salad, and serve with the dipping sauce and a side bowl of broth.

Hasselback Teriyaki Tofu Steaks

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 1 block firm tofu, drained
  • Teriyaki sauce, to taste
  • Light soy sauce, to taste
  • Maple syrup, to taste
  • 1 lime, juice + zest
  • Fresh ginger, grated
  • Pinch of salt (optional)
Instructions
  1. Press the tofu for 10–15 minutes to remove excess water.
  2. Slice the block in half so you're left with two large rectangle 'steaks'.
  3. Meanwhile, combine the teriyaki sauce, light soy sauce, maple syrup, lime juice, lime zest and ginger in a bowl. Depending on the brand of teriyaki sauce, season with a small pinch of salt. Set aside.
  4. Cut each steak hasselback style: place chopsticks on either side and cut thin vertical slices without cutting all the way through, creating a fan shape.
  5. Brush the sauce over the tofu, letting it soak into the cuts, then cook (pan-fry or air fry at 400°F) until golden and crisp, basting with more sauce.
  6. Serve hot with rice and a squeeze of lime.

45m📌 Personal

Homemade Ginger Beer Soda

Ingredients
  • 2 cups (500 ml) filtered water (for ginger bug)
  • 6 tsp shredded or chopped fresh ginger, skin on (for ginger bug)
  • 4 tsp organic unrefined sugar (for ginger bug)
  • 2 tsp chopped ginger + 2 tsp sugar, for daily feeding of the bug
  • 2 litres (8 cups) filtered water
  • 180 g (about 3/4 cup) unrefined sugar
  • 54 g (about 1/4 cup) grated fresh ginger
  • Juice of 2 lemons (optional)
  • 1/2 cup strained ginger bug (starter)
Instructions
  1. Make the ginger bug: put room-temperature filtered water, sugar and diced ginger in a clean jar. Firmly attach the lid, give it a good shake, and place it in a warm spot (70-80°F) for 24 hours.
  2. Feed the ginger bug the next day by adding 2 tsp chopped ginger and 2 tsp sugar, then shaking again. Repeat this feeding once a day for 3-5 days; the bug is ready when you see little bubbles forming.
  3. Make the ginger beer: put the 2 litres of water, 180 g unrefined sugar and 54 g grated ginger in a pan. Bring to a boil, then simmer for about 10 minutes.
  4. Take off the heat and let it sit until it reaches room temperature. Strain through a fine-mesh colander, then stir in the juice of 2 lemons and 1/2 cup of the strained ginger bug.
  5. Mix until combined and pour into sterilised flip-top bottles, leaving about 2 inches of headroom.
  6. Let the bottles sit at room temperature with the tops locked for 3-6 days (about 2-3 days to carbonate), and burp the bottles once a day to release pressure.
  7. Once the soda is fizzy to your liking, move it to the fridge to slow fermentation and serve chilled.

Honey & Soy Chicken with Coconut Gochujang Broth

Ingredients
  • For the Broth:
  • 1 tbsp olive oil, for sautéing
  • 2 cans (13.5 oz each) coconut milk, unsweetened
  • 2 cups chicken stock
  • 4 tbsp white miso paste
  • 2 tbsp rice vinegar
  • 3 tbsp gochujang
  • 1 medium white onion, sliced
  • 2 shallots, sliced
  • 6 garlic cloves, diced
  • 1-inch piece of ginger, grated
  • 4 tbsp soy sauce
  • 3 tbsp mirin
  • 2 tbsp brown sugar
  • 4–6 baby bok choy, halved lengthwise
  • Chili oil, for drizzling
  • For the Chicken Marinade:
  • 4 chicken breasts (if large breasts, slice in half)
  • 5 tbsp honey
  • 6 tbsp soy sauce
  • 4 tsp chili crunch
  • 4 garlic cloves, grated
  • 2 tsp fresh ginger, grated
  • For the Bowl:
  • 4 cups cooked jasmine rice (about 1 cup uncooked per person)
  • 6 green onions, thinly sliced
  • 2 tbsp toasted sesame seeds
  • 1/2 cup crispy fried onions
Instructions
  1. Marinate the Chicken: In a bowl, whisk together honey, soy sauce, chili crunch, garlic, and ginger.
  2. Add chicken breasts and coat well. Marinate at least 30 minutes, or up to overnight in the fridge.
  3. Make the Broth: In a large pot, heat the oil over medium. Add onion, shallots, garlic, and ginger. Sauté 5–6 minutes until softened.
  4. Stir in miso paste and gochujang, cooking 1 minute.
  5. Pour in coconut milk, stock, soy sauce, rice vinegar, brown sugar and mirin. Simmer for 15 minutes. Cook the chicken in the meantime.
  6. Cook the Chicken: Heat a skillet or grill pan over medium-high heat.
  7. Cook chicken for 5–6 minutes per side, until golden and cooked through (165°F internal temp).
  8. Let rest 5 minutes, then slice. Add your bok choy to the broth and simmer another 3–4 minutes until tender but bright green.
  9. Assemble the Bowls: Divide jasmine rice among 4 bowls.
  10. Ladle over broth and add bok choy.
  11. Top with sliced honey soy chicken.
  12. Finish with green onions, fried onions, sesame seeds, and a drizzle of chili oil.

Lemon Yogurt Chicken

Serves 4-6
Ingredients
  • 8 chicken thighs (bone-in, skin-on)
  • 3 tbsp olive oil
  • 2 cups plain yogurt (Balkan, Bulgarian, or Greek)
  • 1 tbsp salt
  • 1 tsp black pepper
  • 2 tbsp dried oregano
  • 2 lemons, juiced (squeezed pieces added to dish too)
  • 6 cloves garlic
Instructions
  1. Add the chicken to a baking dish with the olive oil and yogurt.
  2. Season with salt, pepper, and oregano.
  3. Add the juice of 2 lemons, then add the squeezed lemon pieces to the dish as well.
  4. Add 6 cloves of garlic to the baking dish.
  5. With clean hands, massage the mixture well to evenly distribute the spices.
  6. Marinate in the fridge for at least 1 hour, or up to overnight. (It will still be delicious but not as tender if you skip this.)
  7. Preheat the oven to 400°F (200°C).
  8. Bake, uncovered, for 45 minutes to 1 hour depending on thigh size. Fully cooked when a meat thermometer in the thickest part registers 165°F (74°C).
  9. To crisp the skin further, broil on top for 1–2 minutes, watching closely.

45m📌 Personal

Pan-Roasted Chicken Thighs with Herby Rice & Pickled Salad

Ingredients
  • 6 skin-on chicken thighs
  • 250g basmati rice
  • 1/2 cucumber
  • 1/2 red onion
  • 1 red chilli
  • 2 tbsp white wine vinegar
  • 1 tbsp sugar
  • 2 shallots
  • 15g fresh parsley
  • 15g fresh dill
  • 15g fresh mint
  • 2 lemons
  • Salt
Instructions
  1. Ask your butcher to debone the thighs, or do it yourself by using a small, sharp knife to slice lengthways either side of the bone and then carefully work your way around each end.
  2. Season the thighs all over with salt and leave to come up to room temperature. Place the thighs skin-side down in a heavy-based frying pan and then turn the heat to low-medium. Once they begin to sizzle, cook the thighs for about 15 mins, skin-side down. As they come up to temperature, the fat will render from under the skin and it will turn golden brown and crispy.
  3. Meanwhile, get the rice on. There are lots of ways to cook basmati rice, but the favourite is to add a 2:1 ratio of water to (well rinsed) rice, bring it to the boil, turn the heat to a low simmer, cover with a lid and cook for 10 mins. Turn the heat off and leave to steam for a few minutes.
  4. Meanwhile, finely slice the cucumber, red onion and chilli and add to a bowl with the vinegar and sugar. Stir well and set aside.
  5. Finely dice the shallots and finely chop the herbs. Set aside.
  6. By now the chicken should have been cooking for about 15 mins and the rice for about 12 mins (including steaming time). Turn the rice off and leave it to steam. Flip the chicken thighs to cook the top – they will be almost cooked through, so this will only take about 2 mins.
  7. Remove the thighs and set them aside on a plate to rest for a few minutes. Meanwhile, add the shallots to the chicken fat in the pan and cook for 2 mins until soft. Tip the shallots, any remaining chicken fat, three-quarters of the herbs and the juice of 1 lemon into the rice. Mix well.
  8. Plate up a mound of rice, slice the chicken thighs into nice thick slices and lay them on top, then finish with a pile of the pickled salad. Top with the remaining fresh herbs and a wedge of lemon.

45m📌 Personal

Quinoa Salad with Halloumi & Coconut Cilantro Dressing

Ingredients
  • 1 cup uncooked quinoa
  • 1 packed cup cilantro, 2.5 ounces / 70 grams
  • 1 large jalapeño, 1.5 ounces / 42 grams
  • 1 (13.5 ounce) can full-fat unsweetened coconut milk
  • 3 tablespoons freshly squeezed lemon juice, about 1 lemon
  • 1 teaspoon ground cumin
  • 1 teaspoon Diamond Crystal kosher salt
  • 1 large summer squash, 12 ounces / 340 grams
  • 3 medium ears of corn, yields 12 ounces / 350 grams of corn kernels
  • 1 large sweet bell pepper, 8 ounces / 226 grams
  • 1 pint cherry tomatoes, 8 ounces / 226 grams
  • 8 ounces halloumi cheese
  • Extra-virgin olive oil
Instructions
  1. Begin by cooking your quinoa according to package instructions (a rice cooker with a 2:1 ratio of water to quinoa works well, but any method works). When it's finished, set it to the side to cool to room temperature.
  2. While the quinoa is cooking, add 3/4 cup of cilantro to a blender, reserving the rest. Add 1 jalapeño, one can of coconut milk, 3 tablespoons of lemon juice, one teaspoon of cumin, and one teaspoon of salt, and blend until it is a smooth, light green dressing. Taste and season with salt as preferred. Set this aside.
  3. Thinly slice the summer squash and add it to a large bowl. Cut the corn kernels from the ears of corn and set them in the same bowl.
  4. Thinly slice the sweet bell pepper and quarter the cherry tomatoes. Set those aside.
  5. Cut the halloumi into bite-sized cubes.
  6. Set a pan over medium heat and add a tablespoon of olive oil, just enough to coat the surface. Add the halloumi and let it cook, untouched, until it is golden and sizzling on one side, about 3-4 minutes. Use a spatula to flip the halloumi and let it continue to cook, browning on all sides, another 3-4 minutes.
  7. When the halloumi has browned, add in the corn and squash. Season with a few large pinches of salt and cook, stirring often, for 7-9 minutes, or until the squash has softened and the corn is taking on a bit of color.
  8. Add the cooked vegetables and halloumi to a large bowl with the quinoa. Let this cool to room temperature for 10-15 minutes (or else the other veg risks getting soggy).
  9. When the quinoa salad has cooled, add the pepper and tomatoes, plus the reserved cilantro. Mix everything together.
  10. Top each serving with a healthy amount of the dressing (nearly 1/2 cup per bowl). If saving for meal prep, store the salad and the dressing in separate containers. Serve and enjoy!

Rice Cooker Teriyaki Salmon & Broccoli Bowl

Ingredients
  • 1/3 cup teriyaki sauce (homemade recipe below, or store-bought)
  • 2 (4 oz) salmon fillets, skin on
  • 3 cups broccoli florets, chopped
  • 1/2 tablespoon avocado or melted coconut oil
  • 1 cup cooked white or brown rice
  • 1 cup shredded red cabbage
  • 1/2 cup shredded carrots
  • 1/2 bunch green onions, chopped
  • 1/2 avocado, quartered and sliced
  • 1 lime, quartered
  • Sesame seeds or gomasio, optional
  • 1 tablespoon avocado oil or sesame oil (teriyaki sauce)
  • 1/4 cup low sodium tamari or low sodium soy sauce (teriyaki sauce)
  • 1/2 tablespoon maple syrup or honey (teriyaki sauce)
  • 2 cloves garlic, minced (teriyaki sauce)
  • 1 teaspoon apple cider vinegar or rice vinegar (teriyaki sauce)
  • 1/2 teaspoon fresh ginger, grated or minced (teriyaki sauce)
Instructions
  1. Add all teriyaki sauce ingredients to a small bowl and whisk together until combined.
  2. In a shallow baking dish, pour 1/4 cup of the teriyaki sauce over the salmon fillets and use a spoon to make sure each piece is coated. Marinate in the refrigerator for at least 30 minutes, up to 4 hours. Meanwhile, toss the chopped broccoli with oil and a bit of sea salt.
  3. Preheat oven to 425°F and lightly grease a baking sheet or line it with parchment paper.
  4. Place the marinated salmon fillets skin-side down on one half of the prepared baking sheet and add the broccoli to the other half. Bake until the salmon is cooked through and flakes easily and the broccoli is roasted, about 12-18 minutes.
  5. Cook or reheat the rice while the salmon and broccoli are baking.
  6. Grab two bowls, add rice, and top each with a salmon fillet, cabbage, carrots, green onions, avocado, a squeeze of lime, and a drizzle of the remaining teriyaki sauce. Sprinkle with sesame seeds, if using, and serve immediately.

Slow-Roasted Beef Stew

Ingredients
  • 2 lbs beef cut into 1 1/2" pieces (chuck or round roasts)
  • 1-2 Tbsp cajun seasoning
  • 2-3 Tbsp flour
  • 2-4 Tbsp oil (as needed)
  • 1 onion (sliced)
  • 2 stalks celery (sliced)
  • 3-4 cloves garlic (minced)
  • 3/4 cup red wine
  • 3 Tbsp tomato paste
  • 6 cups beef broth (divided)
  • 1 Tbsp Worcestershire sauce
  • 1 Tbsp soy sauce
  • 2 bay leaves
  • 2 sprigs rosemary (or oregano/thyme)
  • 1 Tbsp paprika
  • 3 carrots (diced)
  • 3 potatoes (diced)
  • 2 parsnips (diced (optional))
  • salt and pepper (to taste)
  • 1 cup frozen peas (optional)
  • 2 Tbsp parsley (chopped)
Instructions
  1. Preheat oven to 350°F (180°C).
  2. Season beef cubes on all sides with Cajun seasoning or salt and pepper. Dredge pieces in flour, shaking off excess.
  3. Heat a Dutch oven or heavy pot over medium high heat for 1 minute. Add 2 Tbsp oil and heat for 1 minute.
  4. Brown beef on all sides in hot pan. For best results, do this in two or three batches. Remove finished beef before adding more beef and oil as needed. Adjust heat as needed to prevent burning.
  5. Remove all beef from pan. Reduce heat to medium. If needed, add more oil then sauté onions and celery until soft but not brown, about 2 minutes. Add garlic and sauté for 1 minute.
  6. Add red wine to deglaze pan. Stir in tomato paste. Stir to get all those tasty caramelized brown bits from the pan. Add 4 cups broth, Worcestershire, soy sauce, bay leaves, rosemary (or oregano or thyme) and paprika.
  7. Return beef and any juices that have collected to pan. Mix well. Cover and cook in oven for 1 1/2 to 2 hours to get beautiful tender meat.
  8. Add potatoes, carrots and parsnips (if using) to stew. Add remaining 2 cups broth. Cover, return to oven and bake for 1 1/2 to 2 hours.
  9. Remove from oven, taste and adjust seasoning as desired. Add frozen peas or other frozen or leftover veggies and let rest for 10 minutes before garnishing and serving. (The stew will be so hot the frozen veggies will heat through in no time).

15 Minute Beef & Broccoli

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • Makes 4 portions
  • 1 lb beef flank steak or sirloin .5kg
  • 5 g or 1t soy sauce
  • 5 g or 1t shao-hsing chinese cooking wine sub with dry sherry or white wine
  • 10 g or 2 1/4t sesame oil
  • 2 g or .5t sugar
  • 2 g or 3/4t corn starch
  • 2 g or .5t baking soda
  • 350 g broccolini or broccoli cut into small pieces 12oz
  • ¼ large white onion sliced
  • 10 g 3-5 cloves garlic grated
  • 10 g or 1-1.5Tbsp ginger grated
  • Stir fry sauce see recipe below
  • Cornstarch slurry 3g/1teas corn starch mixed with 30-40g water/couple of Tbsp
  • High smokepoint oil canola, peanut, etc
  • 10 g or 2.5t sugar
  • 30 g or 1.75Tbsp soy sauce
  • 45 g or 2.5Tbsp oyster sauce sub with hoisin
  • 30 g or 1.75Tbsp shao-hsing cooking wine sub with dry sherry or white wine
Instructions
  1. Cut steak with the grain into 3 2.5”/6-7cm wide strips. Turn each strip 90 degrees and slice thinly on a bias. Pieces should be approx 1”x ⅛”x2” (2.5x.3x5cm).
  2. Place sliced meat in a colander and rinse well with water.
  3. Place rinsed and drained meat in a bowl and add soy sauce, cooking wine, sesame oil, sugar, corn starch, and baking soda. Mix well to coat.
  4. NOTE: I use a seasoned 12” carbon steel wok which is recommended for the high heat of this recipe, but if you don’t have one, use a large cast iron or 12-14” nonstick at a lower temp (450F/230C).
  5. Place pan over high heat to preheat to at least 550F/285C (mine was over 600F). Add a squeeze of oil around the outside of the wok. Add half of the marinated beef. Stir and toss to coat with the oil, moving it around/tossing often. Do this for about 60-90 sec or until browned and cooked about 80-90% through.
  6. Transfer beef to a bowl, add another squeeze of oil followed by second half of raw/marinated beef to wok. Cook, stirring and tossing frequently for 60-90 sec.
  7. Transfer to cooked beef bowl. Return wok to high heat. Add splash of water and wet towel with tongs to remove any stuck-on beef gunk from pan. Add squirt of oil followed by half of broccolini and half of onions. Stir and toss to cook for 60-90sec or until charred around the edges and are tender but still snappy.
  8. Transfer to bowl with cooked beef. Return wok to high heat, add squeeze of oil and the rest of the broccolini and onions, cook for 60-90 sec. Add garlic and ginger, moving/stirring constantly. Add previously stirfried beef and veg. Toss to combine for about 15-20 sec.
  9. Add stirfry sauce (recipe above) around inside edges of wok. Add 2-3 spoons of cornstarch mixture. Toss and stir together to combine. Serve with rice.

15 Minute Carbonara

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 225 g very thick cut bacon diced into small batons
  • olive oil
  • 340 g spaghetti
  • 50 g aged parmesan feather shredded
  • 50 g pecorino romano feather shredded
  • 1 egg
  • 3 egg yolks
  • 2 g ground black pepper
  • 500 mL reserved pasta water
Instructions
  1. Fill a 12" sauté pan with water and bring it to a boil.
  2. Salt the water and then add spaghetti. Simmer on high for about 8 minutes. After 8 minutes, the noodles should have just a bit of crunch in the middle.
  3. While the noodles are cooking, heat a large dutch oven or large, heavy bottomed pot over medium heat.
  4. Add a bit of olive oil and the cubed bacon into the dutch oven. Cook and render the bacon down for about 8 minutes, stirring occasionally. The bacon should be well rendered and golden.
  5. In a separate bowl, mix together cheeses and eggs. Remove the bacon from the pot and set aside. Reduce heat to medium low, adding any bacon grease that comes with it back into the pot followed by black pepper.
  6. Fry black pepper and stir with wooden spoon, scraping up any bacon fond. At this point, ladle in about 300mL/1 ½ cup pasta water into bacon pot and continue to deglaze up bacon fond.
  7. Transfer spaghetti into bacon pot (remembering to save additional pasta water to adjust sauce later).
  8. Stir 100mL/½ cup of pasta water together with the egg and cheese mixture. Add 100mL/½ cup of additional pasta water into the spaghetti pot and stir.
  9. Simmer pasta in the bacon pot for about 90 seconds. Taste the pasta, it should be al dente, if not continue cooking until it is.
  10. Remove from heat and stir in the rendered bacon.
  11. Add egg/cheese mixture and stir very well until cheese melts. If needed, return pot to low heat, stirring constantly until cheese is melted and sauce is velvety and creamy. Don’t take it too far or sauce will become gloppy.
  12. Top with a pinch of aged parmesan and black pepper and serve.

25 Minute Stir Fry

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 400 g water
  • 300 g rice
  • pinch of salt
  • 280 g chicken breast cut into thin bite sized pieces
  • 10-12 oyster mushrooms sub 2 or 3 sliced portabellas
  • 6 baby bok choys quartered
  • 6-7 scallions tops removed and chopped into ½”/1cm pieces
  • knob of ginger 1" grated
  • 5 cloves garlic grated/minced
  • high smoke point oil canola, avocado, light olive
  • kosher salt
  • 25 g soy sauce
  • 5 g sesame oil
  • 25 g oyster sauce
  • 25 g mirin
  • 50 g chicken stock
  • 5 g corn starch
Instructions
  1. Add to rice, water and a pinch of salt to a rice cooker and cook.
  2. Fluff the rice with a fork when it is done cooking.
  3. Whisk together until combined.
  4. Preheat wok over med high to high heat. Add high smokepoint oil to the wok followed by chicken and a pinch of salt. Allow chicken to cook for 60-90 seconds before moving chicken. Loosen chicken from wok with wooden spoon and cook until it's 65-70% done. It will cook more later. Transfer chicken to a bowl.
  5. Wipe out wok with wet paper towel then return wok to burner. Add oil then mushrooms and a pinch of salt. Stir fry for 60-90 seconds. When they’re turning brown and and have lost a lot of their water. Add into the bowl with cooked chicken.
  6. Return wok to heat and add just a bit more oil. Add bok choy and a pinch of salt. Toss and stir fry for 1-2 minutes until leaves are wilted but bottoms are still a bit crunchy. Add bok choy to chicken/mushroom bowl.
  7. Return wok to heat, add a bit more oil than scallions, garlic, and ginger. Toss and stirfry quickly until scallions begin to char. Keep this moving to prevent burning the garlic. Add stir fry sauce by swirling it around inside edges of the wok.
  8. Add the chicken, mushrooms, and bok choy back into the wok with the the sauce and scallions. Stir fry to combine and warm through. If sauce is too thick, add a bit more chicken stock to thin a bit.
  9. Serve over rice.

30 Minute Chili

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2.5 pounds 80/20 ground beef 1200g
  • 250 g white onion, small diced 1 large
  • 20 g garlic 5-6 cloves, pressed
  • 2 14.5 oz cans diced tomatoes 411g, drained
  • 1 can 425g tomato sauce
  • 1 28 oz an fire-roasted tomatoes 800g
  • 2 smoked chipotle peppers in adobo
  • 50 g tomato paste 3T
  • Olive oil
  • 60 g chili powder 1/2c
  • 30 g paprika 1/4c
  • 10 g ground cumin 1 1/2T
  • 1-3 g Chili flakes 1t
  • 20 g hot sauce (i like frank's) 4t
  • 30 g brown sugar 2T
  • 30 g Worcestershire sauce 2T
  • 15 g cider vinegar 1T
  • 30 g beef bouillon paste (i like better than bouillon) 1 1/2T
  • 10 g salt 2t
  • 4 16 oz cans kidney beans 450g, i like bush's or 365 brand
  • Water or beef stock as needed
Instructions
  1. Place ground beef on a sheet tray, flatten into a sheet, and broil on high for 8-10 minutes (8″/20cm) below broiler until browned on top. Allow to cool while you prepare the chili.
  2. While the beef is broiling, dice the onion and press the garlic. Drain the liquid from the diced tomatoes.
  3. Use an immersion blender to combine fire-roasted tomatoes and chipotle peppers in adobo sauce. Blend until smooth.
  4. In a large pot over medium heat, add a long drizzle of olive oil, onion, garlic, and a pinch of salt. Sweat the onion until softened, 5 min.
  5. Add chili powder, paprika, ground cumin, and chili flakes. Stir and toast the spices for 20-30 seconds.
  6. Add the blended tomato mixture, tomato sauce, drained diced tomatoes, and tomato paste. Stir and scrape the bottom of the pot.
  7. Add hot sauce, brown sugar, Worcestershire sauce, cider vinegar, beef bouillon paste, and salt. Stir to combine and simmer for 5 minutes.
  8. Once beef is cooled, break it down into small crumbles and add it into the simmering chili along with drained kidney beans and simmer for a minimum of 10 minutes.
  9. If the chili is too thick, add a bit of water or stock to achieve the desired consistency. Simmer for an additional 2 minutes.
  10. Taste the chili and adjust seasoning if needed with additional salt, apple cider vinegar, brown sugar, and hot sauce to taste. Stir and taste in between adding each ingredient.
  11. I like to top mine with diced onion, sour cream, shredded cheddar, and oyster crackers, but you do you.

30 Minute Gyro Taco

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 100 g onion rough chopped
  • 10 g garlic
  • 450 g 90/10 ground beef
  • 450 g 85/15 ground lamb
  • 50 g panko breadcrumbs
  • 2 g dried oregano
  • 5 g coriander ground
  • 3 g black pepper ground
  • 1-2 g chile flake
  • 7 g cumin ground
  • 10 g salt
  • 150 g cucumber partially skinned, seeds removed, medium diced
  • 150 g tomato medium diced
  • 100 g red onion medium diced
  • 15 g parsley chopped
  • 15 g lemon juice or juice of ½
  • 20 g olive oil
  • Salt large pinch
  • 200 g plain yogurt
  • 75 g mayo
  • 20 g lemon juice or juice of ½
  • 1 clove garlic minced
  • 2 g salt
  • 3 g fresh dill chopped
  • Fluffy flour tortillas or good pitas if you can find them
  • Greek style feta cheese crumbled
  • Pepperoncinis
  • Shredded lettuce
Instructions
  1. Add onion and garlic to a food processor and process until minced.
  2. Add meats, breadcrumbs, herbs and spices. Process until just combined and meat is broken down. Texture should be sticky.
  3. Measure meat mixture into 6 balls (about 175g each) and shape each ball into an oblong/oval shaped patty.
  4. .Preheat grill on high. Inside temp should reach 450-500F/230-260C. (alternately you can cook these under a medium broiler if you don’t have access to a grill but keep a close eye on them to be sure they don’t burn)
  5. Clean and oil grill grates. Spray or brush oil on meat patties then place them on the grill to cook for 4-5min, lid closed.
  6. After 4-5min, flip to side 2, lower lid and grill for 3-4 more minutes.
  7. When done, internal temp should be at 150F/63-65C. Allow meat to rest for 5 min.
  8. Slice meat into pieces similar to the size of gyro meat.
  9. Combine all ingredients in a bowl and allow to marinate.
  10. Combine all ingredients except dill into a high sided container and blend with immersion blender until smooth. Stir in dill.
  11. Toast tortilla or pita on both slides and hold in a tortilla warmer or small container with a lid until you’re ready to build the “gyros”.
  12. Add meat to tortilla/pita, top with a generous drizzle of the lemony yogurt sauce, crumbled feta, pepperoncinis, and a large spoonful of the cucumber tomato salad.

30 Minute Pad Woon Sen (Thai Stir Fry Noodles)

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 200 g mung bean vermicelli noodles 7-8oz
  • 500 g boneless skinless chicken thighs
  • 200 g cabbage cut into thin strips, 1/4 head
  • 100 g snow peas sliced into strips
  • 100 g red bell pepper cut into thin strips
  • 100 g carrot shredded
  • 15 g garlic minced, 3-5 cloves
  • 100 g white onion thinly sliced
  • 2 eggs beaten
  • neutral oil light olive, avocado, canola, etc
  • kosher salt
  • fresh cilantro
  • scallions thinly sliced
  • sesame seeds
  • sriracha or sambal
  • 50 g soy sauce
  • 20 g fish sauce
  • 25 g brown sugar
  • 100 g oyster sauce
  • 150 g water
Instructions
  1. Soak noodles in warm water until soft (i soak the brand im using for 15-20mins). Cut noodles into 6-9″ (15-22cm) if they’re super long.
  2. Prep the chicken by removing any excess fat then cut into thin strips.
  3. Cut and prep all veg, beat eggs, and mix stir fry sauce.
  4. Preheat large saute pan or wok over med-high. Add a long glug/drizzle of oil to pan and add chicken. Add a pinch of salt. Cook for 2-3 minutes or until browned and nearly cooked. Transfer chicken to a bowl.
  5. Return pan to med-high. Add eggs and scramble. When eggs are 90% cooked, add to bowl with chicken.
  6. Return pan to heat, add a drizzle of oil followed by bell peppers, snow peas, cabbage and a pinch of salt toss and stir to cook. When veggies begin to soften (about 2-3 min), add carrots. Cook for an additional 1-2 minutes until veg has taken on some caramelization. Transfer veggies to bowl with eggs and chicken.
  7. Return pan to med-high heat, add a squeeze of oil. Add sliced onion. Cook for about 60 seconds. When beginning to brown add garlic, stirring constantly for about 20 seconds. Add in soaked vermicelli noodles and stir. Add stir fry sauce, stir to coat and bring to a simmer and cook until noodles are fully softened. Add veggies/chicken into pan, ½ at a time, stirring and tossing to combine.
  8. Plate up and garnish with cilantro, sliced scallions, sesame seeds, and sriracha or sambal.

5 Ingredient Mexican: Enchiladas

Serves 8
Ingredients
  • 400 g tomato sauce canned
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 5 g granulated sugar
  • 10 g white distilled vinegar
  • 40 g chipotle in adobo
  • 50 g chili powder
  • 400 g water
  • 400-450 g rotisserie chicken meat
  • 200 g shredded Mexican blend cheese plus more for topping
  • corn tortillas
  • optional: cilantro
Instructions
  1. In blender, combine tomato sauce, salt, sugar, vinegar, chipotle in adobo, chili powder, and water. Blend until smooth.
  2. Pick the chicken meat off the carcass, chop it into bite-sized pieces, and mix it with 200g/2 cups of shredded cheese and 200g/1 cup of enchilada sauce. Heat tortillas in a non stick or cast-iron pan. Keep tortillas warm until ready to build the enchiladas. & brush each side with enchilada sauce.
  3. To build the enchiladas, brush each side of the tortillas with the enchilada sauce before filling each one with about ½ cup of the chicken mix and roll them up and place them in an oven safe pan, I am using a large sauce pan for these ones.
  4. Lather some additional sauce over the enchiladas and broil them on high for about 2 minutes. remove the pan from the oven and top with a little more sauce and a generous handful of cheese.
  5. Broil for another 2 minutes, remove them from the oven and let them sit for 2-5 minutes. top with cilantro or any other toppings you might want.

5 Ingredient Mexican: Mushroom Quesadilla

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 250 g white onion diced
  • 450 g mushrooms diced
  • 75 g pickled jalapenos diced
  • olive oil
  • kosher salt
  • large flour tortillas
  • Chihuahua cheese
  • optional: hot sauce lime juice
Instructions
  1. Saute the mushrooms with a large pinch of salt in olive oil over medium heat for 10-15 mins until mushrooms are golden brown. Add onions & salt. Cook for 5-7 minutes until translucent and softened. Stir in pickled jalapeños.
  2. On the flour tortillas, layer cheese, mushroom mix, cheese. Fold the tortilla in half and cook in oil, in a large sauté pan over medium heat on each side until both sides are golden brown and the cheese is melted. This should take roughly 2 minutes per side.

5 Ingredient Mexican: Tostada

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 850 g canned black beans 2 15oz cans
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 10 g onion powder
  • 10 g ground cumin
  • 5 g garlic powder
  • olive oil
  • apple cider vinegar or lime juice
  • 4 tostada shells
  • avocado cubed
  • cherry tomatoes sliced
  • feta cheese crumbled
  • taco sauce or hot sauce
Instructions
  1. Preheat your oven to 350°F/175℃
  2. In food processor, combine 1 drained can of black beans, 1 undrained can of black beans, salt, onion powder, cumin, and garlic powder.
  3. Process until smooth.
  4. Heat olive oil in large non-stick over medium-high. Add bean puree to hot oil and fry for 10 minutes, stirring frequently.
  5. Off heat, add dash of apple cider vinegar or lime juice.
  6. Heat up the tostada shells in the preheated oven for 5 minutes.
  7. Build the tostadas in such an order: tostada, bean puree, avocado, tomatoes, feta, then hot sauce.

Alfredo E Pepe

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 50 g unsalted butter
  • 20 g AP flour
  • 500 g half & half
  • 8 g kosher salt
  • 8 g coarse black pepper
  • 75 g grated pecorino
  • 75 g grated parmesan
  • 450 g fettuccine
  • olive oil
  • more cheese + pepper for garnish
Instructions
  1. In a dutch oven or wide bottom sauce pan over medium heat, melt the butter. when butter is fully melted and bubbling slightly, whisk in the flour and cook for another minute or just until there is no scent of raw flour. Be sure not to brown the butter or flour.
  2. Whisk in half & half slowly to ensure no clumps are formed. Bring the sauce to a low simmer. When the sauce has thickened, remove it from the heat and stir in pecorino, parmesan, salt and pepper. Whisk everything together and set aside while you cook your pasta.
  3. Bring a pot of water to a boil and add in a strong pinch of salt. Cook the fettuccine for 12-13 minutes, or until pasta is desired doneness. Reserve 1 cup of pasta water and strain off the noodles.
  4. Stir the pasta and a splash of pasta water into the cheese sauce. When everything is fully incorporated, taste the pasta and adjust seasoning and consistency as needed with more salt, pepper, cheese and pasta water.
  5. Serve each portion with more cheese, pepper, and a nice drizzle of olive oil.

Bacon and Peas Pasta

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 225 g pasta shells medium size
  • 225 g thick cut bacon diced into squares
  • 100 g white onion small diced
  • salt
  • 50 g unsalted butter
  • 125 g frozen peas
  • 50 g grated parm plus extra for topping
  • black pepper
Instructions
  1. Bring a large pot of water to a boil, salt well, and cook pasta according to package instructions. Reserve 250mL/1c pasta water then drain. Return empty pasta pot to medium heat and add diced bacon. Cook, stirring often, until fat has rendered and bacon is brown and becoming crisp, 7-8min. Remove bacon and the bulk of fat from the pan and drain bacon on towel.
  2. Return pot to heat and add olive oil, diced onions, and a pinch of salt, scraping as the onions cook. When onions have softened, add reserved pasta water, drained pasta, and butter. Stir and toss until emulsified.
  3. Add rendered bacon, peas, and grated parmesan. Remove from the heat. Stir and toss gently until parmesan is melted.
  4. Serve and top with a pinch of grated parmesan and black pepper.

Basil Pesto Pasta

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 100 g parmesan cubed
  • 100 g raw pine nuts
  • 125 g olive oil
  • 100 g fresh basil
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 450 g spaghetti
  • more parm + pine nuts for garnish
Instructions
  1. In a food processor, pulse the parmesan cubes until it has turned into a course grind.
  2. Add in nuts, oil, basil and salt. Blend for 25- 30 seconds until it has a nice saucy consistency but is not smooth. The sauce should still have some texture to it.
  3. Bring a pot of water to a boil and add in a strong pinch of salt. cook the spaghetti for 9 minutes or longer if you prefer a softer bite. Reserve a cup of pasta water and strain the noodles.
  4. Return the pasta to the pot you cooked it in along with the pesto and a splash of pasta water. Gently stir the pasta and pesto together until they have formed a slightly creamy pasta. adjust seasoning and consistency as needed with salt, parmesan and pasta water.
  5. Serve each portion with additional parmesan and pine nuts

Brown Butter Sage Pasta

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 1 leg of butternut squash cut into ¼”/.5cm triangles
  • olive oil
  • 50 g pine nuts
  • 225 g campanelli pasta
  • 5-6 leaves sage thinly sliced
  • 100 g unsalted butter
  • 50 g grated parmesan plus extra for topping
  • lemon
  • chili flake
Instructions
  1. Remove stem & bottom bulge of squash. Peel and cut the rest of the squash into ¼"/.5cm triangles. Spread squash onto parchment lined half sheet tray. Toss with olive oil, salt, and pepper. Roast for 10 minutes at 450°F/232°C. You should have enough squash to nearly fill a half sheet tray.
  2. Toast pine nuts on a small sheet tray in the oven (450°F/232°C), 5-6 minutes.
  3. Bring a large pot of water to a boil. Add salt and pasta. Cook to al dente. Reserve 250ml/1 cup pasta water. Drain & set aside pasta while you build the sauce.
  4. Return empty pasta pot to medium heat. Add 50g butter and sliced sage. Fry sage for 90 seconds until butter has browned. Add reserved pasta water and drained pasta. Reduce heat to low. Stir and toss the pasta while shaking the pan to emulsify the sauce. After about a minute the texture should be silky.
  5. Off heat, add half the toasted nuts, all of the roasted squash, and 50g of parmesan. Stir to gently melt the cheese. Plate up and top with a squeeze of lemon juice, a pinch of parm, chili flakes, pine nuts, and a drizzle of olive oil.

Budget Pad Woon Sen

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 400 g thin rice noodles
  • neutral oil
  • 4 large eggs
  • 150 g white onion sliced, 1 medium
  • 225 g snow peas sliced
  • 225 g red bell pepper julienned, 2 large
  • 20 g garlic minced, 5-6 cloves
  • sriracha for serving
  • lime for serving
  • 150 g water
  • 50 g soy sauce
  • 25 g fish sauce
  • 40 g brown sugar
  • 125 g oyster sauce
Instructions
  1. Soak rice noodles in hot water for 15 minutes, then cut in half and drain.
  2. In a 12" nonstick pan over medium-high heat , add oil, crack in eggs, add salt, and scramble until eggs are fully cooked. Transfer the eggs to a bowl and set aside.
  3. Return the pan to heat. In 2 batches add oil, snow peas, peppers, onions, and a pinch of salt. Cook until vegetables are just tender then set aside with the eggs.
  4. When stir frying the second batch of vegetables, add in the garlic while cooking.
  5. After the vegetables are cooked, wipe the pan clean and return it to the heat. Add in the stir-fry sauce and bring it to a boil. Stir in the soaked noodles, submerge them in the liquid and cook for 30 seconds.
  6. Add the cooked vegetables and eggs back into the pan. Toss together until well combined.
  7. Serve in bowls, optionally with sriracha and/ or lime.

Chicken Aloo Vindaloo

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 450 g chicken thighs cut to 1 ½" dice
  • kosher salt
  • 300 g Yukon gold potatoes 1″ dice
  • 250 g large onion grated
  • 60 mL oil or ghee
  • 20 g garlic paste
  • 20 g ginger paste
  • 50 g tomato paste
  • 10 g garam masala
  • 5 g cumin
  • 10 g curry powder
  • 10 g paprika
  • 2 g chile flakes optional
  • 750 g chicken stock
  • 5 g kosher salt
Instructions
  1. Salt the chicken and set it aside while you prepare the rest of the ingredients.
  2. In a dutch oven, sauté the grated onion over medium heat in oil for about 5 minutes until the moisture is cooked out and it has browned.
  3. Stir in the garlic, ginger, and tomato paste. Cook for an additional 2 minutes.
  4. Add in the spices and cook for 1 more minute.
  5. Stir in the stock and bring the pot to a boil. Add in the chicken and potatoes. Simmer uncovered for 15-18 minutes, stirring often.
  6. Adjust the consistency with stock if needed.
  7. Serve over basmati rice with yogurt & cilantro.

⚡ ≤40 min35m

Chicken Piccata

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 2 large chicken breasts boneless skinless
  • 1 pinch salt
  • 30 g all-purpose flour for dredging
  • 30 g olive oil
  • 25 g garlic (6-8 cloves) finely minced
  • 30 g capers drained
  • 3 g all-purpose flour for sauce
  • 200 g white wine dry, preferably Chardonnay
  • 100 g chicken stock
  • 10 g lemon juice
  • 75 g unsalted butter cold, cut into tablespoon-sized cubes
  • 5 g flat-leaf parsley chopped
  • ⅙ lemon Lemon zest
  • 1 pinch of salt to finish
Instructions
  1. Slice each breast in half horizontally so you have two thin pieces, or 4 in total.
  2. Place sliced breasts into a freezer bag and pound to a ¼" thick using the bottom of a sauce pan.
  3. Season pounded chicken with salt and pepper on both sides, and let dry brine/cure for 5–10 minutes.
  4. Next dredge each cutlet in AP flour, shaking off excess. Let them sit for 5 minutes then dredge again right before cooking.
  5. Heat about 3 tablespoons of oil (neutral or olive oil) in a large sauté pan over medium high. Once the oil shimmers, lay the cutlets in and cook undisturbed for 3 minutes until the first side is the color of golden toasted bread with a nice crust. During the fry on the first side make sure to press the cutlet into the pan for 15-20 seconds to ensure maximum surface area contact with the hot pan.
  6. Flip and cook the second side for 45 seconds to 1 minute. Transfer the chicken to a plate.
  7. Wipe out the pan and return to medium-low heat, then add the dried off capers and let them sizzle for about 1 minute or until they are softer and taking on a little bit of color.
  8. Stir in minced garlic and a little more oil and cook gently for about 90 seconds over medium low heat until it's fragrant and just starting to take on color. Keep the garlic moving constantly to prevent burning. If the garlic is starting brown, add tiny splash of water to cool pan and introduce steam.
  9. Sprinkle in the 3g of AP flour and stir to combine.
  10. Pour in white wine and scrape up anything stuck to the pan. Let it reduce over medium heat until nearly dry or when running your spatula through leaves a wide trail.
  11. Add the chicken stock and reduce by about 50% or until it's slightly thickened, around 2–3 minutes.
  12. Once stock is starting to thicken slightly, remove from heat then add the cold cubed butter a few pieces at a time, and the lemon juice, whisking constantly until each addition is melted and incorporated before adding more. The sauce should become glossy, creamy, and smooth. If it looks greasy or broken, add a small splash of water, pull it off the heat completely, and whisk in a bit more cold butter.
  13. Stir in the chopped parsley and lemon zest. Taste for salt and adjust. Spoon the sauce generously over the chicken cutlets. Serve right away. It's best served freshly cooked.
  14. Garnish with more parsley and lemon zest grated on a microplane.
Full recipe →

Chicken Teriyaki

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 2 large bone-in, skin-on chicken thighs
  • 2 g kosher salt
  • 5 g oil
  • 60 g soy sauce
  • 60 g mirin
  • 60 g sake
  • 45 g sugar
Instructions
  1. Use a sharp knife to debone the chicken thighs. Make two slices along either side of the bone to expose it then carefully cut along the bone to remove it while keeping the knife in contact with the bone to avoid losing excess meat. Remove any cartilage from the larger end of the thigh, being careful not to pierce the skin.
  2. Leave all excess skin intact as it will shrink significantly during cooking.
  3. Score the flesh side of each thigh with ½” deep cuts spaced 1 to 1.5 inches apart in a crosshatch pattern.
  4. Lightly salt the flesh side of the thighs.
  5. Do not salt the skin side to keep it dry for better crisping.
  6. Pat the skin dry with a towel before cooking
  7. Heat a nonstick pan over medium-low (4/10) heat.
  8. Lightly oil the pan and place the chicken skin-side down.
  9. Immediately press down on the chicken with a heavy saucepan (~3 lbs) to prevent the skin from shrinking excessively. Allow allow the thighs to cook undisturbed under the heavy pot for 5–6 minutes
  10. Remove the pot and continue cooking for another 4-5 minutes
  11. Check the skin, it should be deep golden brown, not just lightly browned.
  12. Flip the thighs and cook for 2 minutes over low heat to finish cooking through.
  13. The internal temperature should reach 170–180°F (77–82°C).
  14. Let the chicken rest for 5 minutes on a wire rack.
  15. Wipe excess fat from the pan, leaving behind any browned bits.
  16. Add soy sauce, sake, mirin, and sugar to the pan.
  17. Stir to dissolve the sugar, then bring to a simmer.
  18. Continue cooking over high to reduce the sauce until it becomes slightly thick and sticky. It should leave a visible trail when a spatula is dragged through it.
  19. Avoid over-reducing; the sauce should be glossy and cling to the chicken without being overly thick and gloppy
  20. Brush the sauce onto the rested chicken, coating it evenly.
  21. Slice the chicken into 5–7 pieces, cutting skin-side down to prevent tearing the crispy skin.
  22. Serve over steamed medium-grain rice and top with sesame seeds
Full recipe →

Chicken Thigh Khao Soi

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 600 g boneless skinless chicken thighs
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 10 g curry powder
  • 10 g turmeric ground
  • 5 g paprika
  • 3 g coriander ground
  • 30 g garlic paste sub finely minced or grated
  • 30 g ginger paste sub finely minced or grated
  • 15 g lemongrass paste
  • 1 lime zested
  • 1 T coconut oil
  • 500 g chicken stock
  • 1200 mL coconut milk
  • 30 g fish sauce
  • 25 g brown sugar
  • 20–25 g sambal
  • 175 g dry egg noodles
  • raw red onion thinly sliced
  • fried wonton strips
  • pickled mustard greens sub sauerkraut
Instructions
  1. Salt the chicken thighs with 5g of salt and set aside until ready to cook.
  2. In a mixing bowl, combine spices, pastes & lime zest, this will be your curry paste.
  3. In a dutch oven or heavy bottomed pot, fry the paste in oil over medium-high heat for 2–3minutes.
  4. Stir in stock, coconut milk, fish sauce, sugar, sambal and chicken. Simmer over medium heat for 10 minutes and add in the noodles. Cook for an additional 8-10 minutes
  5. Remove the chicken thighs from the soup. Shred the thighs and cut them into spoon-sized pieces, return them to the pot.
  6. Thin the soup with a little extra chicken stock if needed.
  7. Serve with fried wonton strips, shaved red onion, cilantro & pickled mustard greens or sauerkraut.

Chicken Thigh Orange Chicken

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 450 g chicken thighs cut to 1" cubes
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 250 g broccoli cut to 1½" florets
  • 125 g chicken stock
  • 50 g soy sauce
  • 75 g mirin
  • 50 g sugar
  • 10 g sesame oil
  • 15 g garlic paste sub grated or finely minced
  • 15 g ginger paste sub grated or finely minced
  • 1 orange zested
  • 75-85 g orange juice
  • 10 g cornstarch
  • 25 g rice vinegar
Instructions
  1. Salt the chicken with 5g of salt. Set the chicken aside and let cure until it is time to cook.
  2. Combine the sauce ingredients in a mixing bowl and stir to combine. Set aside.
  3. Heat a wok over medium-high heat and add in about 2 tbsp of oil. Toss in the broccoli along with a pinch of salt and stir-fry for about 2 minutes. Remove the broccoli from the wok and set aside.
  4. If needed, wipe out any debris from the wok and return to medium-high heat. Add the chicken to the wok and spread it out into a single layer. let the chicken sit undisturbed for 2-3 minutes, until the edges are beginning to brown. Stir and break up the chicken and stir-fry for an additional 3 minutes.
  5. Stir in the scallions and cook for 1 minutes.
  6. Pour the sauce around the edges of the chicken in the wok. Bring to a boil until thick/sticky. This will take about 2-3 minutes.
  7. Add the broccoli back into the wok and toss to coat.
  8. Serve over rice and garnish with sesame seeds.

Easy Beer Battered Fish Sandwich

Serves 10
Ingredients
  • 1400 g fresh Atlantic cod 140g/5oz per piece
  • kosher salt
  • 150 g AP flour
  • 150 g rice flour
  • 2 g baking soda
  • 2 g kosher salt
  • 350 mL light beer
  • high smoke point oil enough to fill fry pot about 1.5″/4cm
  • 10 brioche buns
  • butterleaf lettuce
  • tartar sauce recipe below
  • 175 g mayonnaise
  • juice of ½ lemon
  • 30 g bread and butter pickles or sweet pickles
  • 20 g capers chopped
  • 30 g 1/2c shallot minced and rinsed
  • 1 clove garlic minced and rinsed
  • 40 g dijon mustard
  • 10 g fresh dill chopped
  • 10 g fresh parsley chopped
Instructions
  1. Season both sides of cod generously with salt. Set aside for 10minutes.
  2. To make the beer batter, add 100g AP flour, 100g rice flour, baking soda and salt to a bowl and mix.
  3. Whisk in most of the beer (all but about an ounce). Consistency should be pretty thin. About like melted ice cream. Refrigerate batter for about 10 minutes while you do the next two steps.
  4. Add 1.5"/4cm oil to large heavy bottomed pot and heat over medium until temp reaches 375°F/190°C.
  5. For the dredge, mix together 50g AP flour and 50g rice flour.
  6. Dry the fish VERY well then dredge fish in the flour mixture.
  7. Remove batter from fridge and check consistency. If it’s a lot thicker than melted ice cream, add additional beer to thin.
  8. Place pieces of fish in cooled batter to cover on all sides, then allow excess batter to drain off.
  9. Drop pieces of fish into pre-heated oil very slowly and place down away from you. Cook for 2 ½ minutes, flipping over halfway through. Don't fry more than about 3 pieces of fish at a time to avoid cooling oil.
  10. Remove from oil and place on a paper towel or wire rack to drain.
  11. To serve, toast brioche* bun. Place a few pieces of butterleaf lettuce down on bottom bun, top with fish, and a generous amount of tartar sauce and top with bun.
  12. Stir ingredients to combine.
  13. BEEF ON WECK (The Absolute KING of Roast Beef Sandwiches)
  14. FRIED CHICKEN SANDWICH RECIPE (with Potato Bun and Buttermilk Ranch)

Fried Rice

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 300 g jasmine rice rinsed well
  • 270 g water
  • 4 large eggs
  • salt
  • 75–100 g scallions sliced, whites + greens separated
  • high smoke point oil
  • 50 g soy sauce
  • 25 g oyster sauce
Instructions
  1. Rinse rice until water runs mostly clear. Cook with 270 g water (90% hydration).
  2. Turn cooked rice out onto a tray, gently spread, and chill 20–30 min until cold.
  3. Heat up about 2 Tbsp of oil until slightly smoking. Add in eggs and allot to puff up for about 15 seconds. Flip the eggs and break down slightly and cook for about another 10 seconds. Set aside.
  4. Heat up another 2 Tbsp of oil and add in scallion greens and cook for about 30 seconds and add in all of your rice.Push the rice around a little until the onions are incorporated and press the rice out into a single layer in the pan and let it cook for 30 seconds. Toss and repeat once more. Add in the eggs and stir and toss until incorporated
  5. Push the rice into the center of the wok, into a single mound. Pour the soy sauce mixture around the edge of the rice and toss until incorporated. Stir in remaining green onion. Season to taste if needed

Green Goddess Egg Salad

Serves 8
Ingredients
  • Dressing makes two times what is needed for the egg salad.
  • 450 g mayo
  • 60 g spinach
  • 15 g fresh dill
  • 5 g fresh tarragon
  • 50 g fresh parsley
  • 10 g of garlic
  • 12 g salt for dressing
  • 20 g honey
  • 24 eggs
  • 2 avocados look for aovs that are mostly ripe, but not super soft
  • 8 g salt for egg salad
  • Juice of 1 lemon
  • 15-20 g chives
  • Olive oil
  • Fresh croissants
  • Arugula dressed with olive oil and a squeeze of lemon juice
Instructions
  1. Add mayo, spinach, dill, tarragon, parsley, garlic, salt and honey to a food processor and spin for 30 seconds or until ingredients are broken down and the sauce is mostly smooth.
  2. Transfer half (about 300g) to a storage container (for green goddess dressing throughout the week) and the other half into a bowl to finish the egg salad.
  3. Press eggs through a wire rack to “dice”
  4. Finely mince chives and add to egg bowl
  5. Medium dice the flesh from two avocados (about 300g) and add to egg bowl.
  6. To that, add 8g salt, juice from 1 lemon, and gently fold to combine.
  7. Add 3-4 spoonfuls onto a nice flaky croissant and top with dressed arugula

Italian Sausage and Rapini Pasta

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 150 g rapini chopped, about ½ a large bunch
  • 225 g orecchiette pasta
  • olive oil
  • 225 g Italian sausage
  • salt and pepper
  • 115 g butter
  • 40 g grated parmesan cheese
  • red chili flakes
Instructions
  1. Chop the rapini into 1" pieces, then blanch in a large pot of well salted boiling water for 30 seconds. Use a spider strainer to scoop into a paper towel lined bowl and set aside.
  2. Cook the orecchiette pasta in the same water you blanched the rapini in until desired doneness. Reserve 1 cup of pasta water and strain the pasta. Toss in a little bit of oil and set aside.
  3. In a large non-stick pan over medium-high, heat olive oil and cook the Italian sausage until browned and well crumbled. Add in the rapini and sauté for another minute.
  4. Over medium low heat, pour in the reserved pasta water and add drained, cooked orecchiette to the pan. Stir in butter and toss until melted and emulsified.
  5. Kill the heat and add in grated parmesan. Toss to combine.
  6. Plate up and garnish with a sprinkle of additional parmesan, some cracked black pepper, and a sprinkle of red chili flakes.
