Italian Wedding & Minestrone Mashup Soup

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 250 g white onion small diced, 1 large
  • 250 g carrots carrots diced, 4-5 medium
  • 75 g kale kale stems removed, leaves sliced into 1″ pieces, 1 bunch
  • 20 g garlic pressed, 5-6 cloves
  • 225 g hot Italian sausage rolled into small balls, 25-30 balls
  • Olive oil
  • 75 g better than bouillon chicken or any chicken bouillon
  • 1400 g box chicken stock
  • 600 g water
  • 100 g elbow macaroni
  • 850 g canned white beans semi-drained
  • salt
  • black pepper for garnish
  • grated parmesan for garnish
Instructions
  1. This soup comes together quickly, so start by cutting the vegetables and rolling out your meatballs.
  2. Heat a large stock pot over medium heat. Add in olive oil, onions, carrots, garlic, and a pinch of salt. Sweat until slightly softened, about 5-6 minutes.
  3. Stir in chicken bouillon, chicken stock, water and bring it all to a simmer.
  4. Add the elbow macaroni, chopped kale and sausage meatballs and cook until the pasta is tender. (if making ahead of time, cook until the meatballs are done)
  5. Pour in the semi-drained white beans. Taste and adjust for salt if needed.
  6. Serve the soup with a sprinkle of grated parmesan, black pepper, and a drizzle of olive oil.

Keema Curry

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 30 g olive oil
  • 225 g white onion small diced
  • 150 g medium-large russet potato peeled and diced ½ inch
  • 25 g garlic minced
  • 450 g 85/15 ground beef
  • 10 g kosher salt divided, plus more to taste
  • 5 g curry powder
  • 3 g garam masala
  • 5 g cumin
  • 25 g tomato paste
  • 400 g full-fat coconut milk
  • 225 g water
  • 150 g frozen peas
  • 150 g baby spinach chopped, 1 bag
  • Steamed white rice for serving
Instructions
  1. In a large (12”) nonstick pan over medium-high heat, add about 1 Tbsp of olive oil, diced onion, diced potato, and a pinch of salt. Sauté for 3–4 minutes, until the onions start to caramelize and the potatoes begin to soften.
  2. Add garlic, stir, and cook for another 2 minutes. Transfer the vegetables to a bowl and set aside.
  3. Return the pan to heat and add another Tbsp of olive oil, ground beef, and a large pinch of salt. Brown the beef, breaking it down well as it cooks.
  4. Add curry powder, garam masala, cumin, and tomato paste. Stir and fry the spices for 20–30 seconds until fragrant.
  5. Return the cooked potatoes and onions to the pan with the beef. Stir to combine, then add coconut milk and water. Reduce heat to medium, bring to a simmer, and cook for about 10 minutes, until the sauce reduces slightly and the potatoes are tender. When properly reduced, the sauce should lleave a clean trail when you drag your spatula through it.
  6. Season with a pinch of salt, then add peas and spinach. Stir and cook for 2–3 minutes, until the spinach wilts and peas are warmed through.
  7. Adjust consistency with a splash of stock or coconut milk if needed.
  8. Serve hot over steamed white rice.

Linguine and Clams

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 300 g linguine
  • olive oil
  • 30 g garlic sliced, 6-8 cloves
  • 2 g chili flakes
  • 300 g dry white wine
  • 370 g cooked clams canned, 185g/6½ oz per can
  • fresh parsley chopped
  • 115 g butter Salt 1 stick
Instructions
  1. Cook linguine in a large pot of well salted boiling water until al dente, about 7-8 minutes. Reserve some pasta water and drain the linguine.
  2. In a large non-stick pan, heat olive oil and sauté sliced garlic over medium heat for 2 minutes until softened. Add chili flakes and fry for another minute.
  3. Add wine and simmer until reduced by half.
  4. Strain the clam juice from the cans of cooked clams into the pan (reserve the clam meat for later). Simmer for 2 minutes to reduce liquid by half.
  5. Add the cooked linguine to the pan along with reserved clams, chopped parsley, and butter. Toss well to coat the pasta in the sauce.
  6. Serve and top with additional parsley and a drizzle of olive oil.

Mapo Tofu

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 400 soft tofu
  • 15 g grated ginger
  • 30 g garlic minced or pressed, 6 cloves
  • 75 g scallions light green and white parts only, thinly sliced, reserve greens for garnish
  • 10 g Thai chiles thinly sliced, or reduce for less heat
  • 2 g Szechuan peppercorns ground, or sub black pepper
  • 75 g neutral oil e.g., avocado or peanut
  • 125 g 80/20 ground beef or pork
  • 40 g toban djan fermented bean & chili paste
  • 15 g gochugaru chile powder or sub paprika, added later
  • 30 g Shaoxing wine or mirin, white wine, or water
  • 300 g chicken stock
  • 10 g soy sauce
  • 3 g salt
  • 5 g sugar
  • 1 g MSG optional
  • 5 g sesame oil
  • 5 g cornstarch
  • 20 g water
Instructions
  1. Slice tofu into 6 planks, then cube into ¾–1″ pieces. Leave cubes undisturbed on the cutting board to prevent breakage.
  2. Bring 2–3 quarts water to a boil in a wok or pot. Salt generously. Gently slide tofu into the water using a bench scraper. Simmer 2 minutes. Remove with a slotted spoon and transfer to a paper towel–lined plate to dry.
  3. Prep the aromatics: grate ginger, press garlic, slice scallions (light parts only), and chiles. Grind Sichuan peppercorns in a mortar and pestle or crush with a pan.
  4. Heat dry wok over high heat. Add oil and ground beef. Break up and brown for ~2 minutes.
  5. Add toban djan. Stir-fry 30 seconds.
  6. Add gochugaru and ground Sichuan peppercorns. Stir-fry 1 minute.
  7. Add scallions, chiles, garlic, and ginger. Stir-fry ~90 seconds until softened.
  8. Deglaze with Shaoxing wine, scraping up browned bits. Reduce ~30 seconds. Add stock, soy sauce, salt, sugar, MSG, and sesame oil. Bring to a boil.
  9. Gently fold in tofu. Simmer 2 minutes.
  10. Stir cornstarch and water together into a slurry. Add to wok and stir gently until sauce thickens to a stirrable, saucy consistency. Add more stock if too thick.
  11. Serve over rice and garnish with scallion greens

Pasta alla Vodka (aka the king of pantry pastas)

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 20 g garlic minced/pressed
  • 75 g shallots minced
  • 30-40 g calabrian chile chopped
  • olive oil
  • kosher salt
  • 2-3 g chile flake
  • 170 g tomato paste 1 small can
  • 100 mL vodka sub in white wine if you don’t have vodka on hand
  • 350 g heavy cream if you don’t have cream on hand, use evaporated milk + additional butter and parmesan
  • 60-100 g pasta water or hot water
  • 1 lb fusilli or pasta of choice
  • 75 g unsalted butter
  • 50-75 g aged parmesan grated
Instructions
  1. Preheat large, heavy-bottomed pot over medium. Add 40g or ¼ cup olive oil to the heavy bottomed pot, followed by shallots, garlic, and a pinch of salt. Stir and sweat for 1-2 minutes.
  2. When translucent, add chile flake and calabrian chiles. Stir to bloom for about a minute. Then stir in the tomato paste and cook, stirring frequently, for about 2 minutes to bloom that and cook off any raw flavor.
  3. Deglaze with vodka and cook for about 1 minute to cook off most of the alcohol.
  4. Once thickened and vodka has mostly evaporated, stir in the cream and remove from heat and set aside while you cook your noodles. Add in a few ounces of hot water just to make sure the sauce doesn't get too think while it sits.
  5. Bring large pot of salted water to a boil and cook the fusilli noodles until al dente, or to your desired doneness. Somewhere around 8-10 minutes.
  6. When done, reserve 1-2 cups of pasta water and strain off the noodles.
  7. Over medium low heat, stir in the drained pasta along with butter and ½ cup or 120g of pasta water with the vodka sauce. Gently stir to melt butter and combine.
  8. Once butter is melted in, add an additional ½ cup or 120g of pasta cooking liquid followed by more parmesan.
  9. Remove from heat and stir to combine. If needed, adjust sauce consistency by adding more pasta water to thin it out or more cheese and/or butter to thicken sauce.
  10. Taste for seasoning and add salt if needed. Plate up and garnish with a sprinkle of parmesan, black pepper, a sprinkle of chile flakes, a drizzle of olive oil, and fresh basil if you have it.

Pasta Vodka

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 40 g olive oil
  • 75 g shallots minced
  • 20 g garlic minced
  • 30–40 g Calabrian chiles chopped
  • 2–3 g chile flakes
  • 170 g tomato paste
  • 100 g vodka
  • 350 g heavy cream
  • 450 g fusilli
  • 1 cup pasta water
  • 75 g unsalted butter
  • 50–75 g grated parmesan
  • kosher salt
  • black pepper for garnish
  • grates parmesan for garnish
Instructions
  1. Bring a large pot of salted water to a boil for the pasta.
  2. In a heavy, wide bottom sauce pan or dutch oven over medium heat, add oil, shallots, garlic and a pinch of salt. Sweat for 5 minutes.
  3. Stir in chiles, chile flake and a little more oil if needed. Cook for another 2 minutes and stir in tomato paste. Bloom for 2 minutes until the color deepens.
  4. Deglaze with vodka and reduce for 30 seconds until it has become a thick paste.
  5. Stir in the cream and bring the sauce to a low simmer. Cook for an additional 3-5 minutes until the sauce has slightly thickened. Set aside while you cook your noodles.
  6. Cook the fusilli for 11 minutes or until desired doneness. Reserve 1 cup of pasta water and strain off the noodles.
  7. Add the pasta into the vodka sauce and stir in butter and parmesan.
  8. Taste the pasta and adjust seasoning and consistency as needed with salt, pasta water and cheese.
  9. Serve portions topped with additional parmesan and black pepper.

Quick & Easy Indian Dinner

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 1 kg boneless skinless chicken thighs
  • kosher salt
  • neutral oil i’m using light olive oil
  • 1 medium white onion cut into chunks
  • 5-7 cm knob of ginger cut into coins 2-3 inch
  • 5-7 garlic cloves
  • 15 g curry powder
  • 5 g chili powder
  • 5 g cumin
  • 5 g turmeric
  • 15 g garam masala
  • 1-2 g cayenne
  • 25 g tomato paste
  • 300 g water
  • 400 g tomato puree
  • Sugar
  • 225 g baby spinach
  • 50-75 g frozen peas
  • 30-40 g heavy cream
Instructions
  1. Preheat large heavy bottomed pot over high heat.
  2. Season chicken on both sides with salt.
  3. Add a couple of glugs of oil to preheated pot followed by chicken. Allow to sear for 3-4 minutes on the first side. Flip and continue to cook for 2-3 minutes on side 2.
  4. While the chicken cooks, add onion, ginger, and garlic to a food processor. Process until broken down into paste.
  5. Once chicken is cooked, remove it from pot and place on plate/tray.
  6. After chicken is removed, add paste to chicken pot followed by pinch of salt. Stir and scrape up chicken bits with wooden spoon. Continue to cook for about 2 minutes. Add curry spices and tomato paste. Stir to combine and allow to bloom for 30 seconds. Stir in water and tomato puree. Cover and cook for 3-4 minutes, stirring at least once during that time.
  7. After chicken rests, cut into bite sized pieces.
  8. Add chopped chicken and its juices back into the pot. Stir and bring to a simmer. Add pinch of salt and pinch sugar.
  9. Stir in spinach, peas, and cream, cover and cook for about 1 minute over medium heat.
  10. Taste for seasoning and adjust if needed. Serve over rice.

Quick Green Curry

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 680 g filet black cod/sablefish bones removed, cubed into bitesize chunks, or tilapia
  • kosher salt
  • 200 g Japanese eggplant ½ of the skin removed and cubed
  • 200 g green beans cut into large bitesize pieces
  • coconut oil
  • 200-230 g green curry paste all of the paste from the recipe below
  • 400 mL full fat coconut milk canned
  • 20 g brown sugar
  • 30 g fish sauce
  • 30 g ginger rough chopped, 1 2-3" knob
  • 30 g fresh lemongrass about 1/2 – ⅓ of a stalk, roughly chopped
  • 30 g shallot peeled & rough chopped
  • 30 g garlic minced
  • 30 g jalapeno rough chopped
  • 30 g green bell pepper or more jalapeno here if you like a lot of spice, rough chopped
  • zest of 2 limes
  • 3 g cumin ground
  • 3 g coriander ground
  • 2 g turmeric ground
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 3 g white pepper, or black ground
  • 30 g cilantro fresh
Instructions
  1. Season the cubed cod with a pinch of salt and toss to combine.
  2. Preheat a large heavy bottomed pan over medium-high heat. Add a spoonful of coconut oil followed by the green curry paste. Stir and cook for 60-90 seconds, scraping bottom of the pot with a wooden spoon.
  3. Add in coconut milk. Stir and bring to a simmer, cook for 2-3 minutes. If desired, pulse mixture with an immersion blender to further puree paste. This step is optional.
  4. Stir in the brown sugar, fish sauce, green beans and eggplant. Stir and bring to a simmer. Cover and cook for 3 minutes.
  5. When the vegetables are about 60-70% cooked, add fish and gently stir to submerge. Cover and reduce the heat to low. Poach for another 3 minutes.
  6. Serve over rice, top with a squeeze of lime juice and garnish with thai basil.

Rapini Anchovy Pasta

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 1 ½ bunches rapini/broccoli rabe
  • 30 g garlic sliced, 8 large
  • 56 g anchovies canned, rinsed
  • 225 g orecchiette
  • olive oil
  • 50 g panko bread crumbs
  • chili flake
  • 50 g unsalted butter
  • 50 g parmesan grated, plus extra for topping
Instructions
  1. Remove stems and cut the rest of the bundles of rapini into 2-3" (5-6cm) pieces. Wash. Rinse anchovies and chop.
  2. Bring a large pot of water to a boil. Add a handful of salt and the pasta. Cook according to package instructions. When the pasta has 1 minute left to cook, add in chopped rapini in with the pasta.
  3. Reserve 250mL/1cup water, then drain the pasta/rapini and set aside. Return pasta pot to medium heat and add about 25g/2tbsp of olive oil. When the oil is hot add 50g panko and fry, stirring constantly for about 1 minute until golden brown. Transfer breadcrumbs to a paper towel to drain.
  4. Add another long squeeze (25g/2tbsp) of olive oil followed by sliced garlic. Fry over medium heat for about a minute then add a pinch of chili flake and chopped anchovies. Stir and cook for about 2 minutes.
  5. Add reserved pasta water, drained pasta and rapini followed by 50g of butter and reduce the heat to low. Stir, toss, and shake pan to create an emulsion, resulting in a creamy-silky sauce. Remove from heat and add grated parmesan.
  6. Serve and top with a generous amount of toasted breadcrumbs, a pinch of parmesan, and a drizzle of olive oil.

Ravioli With Marsala Cream Sauce

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 25 g olive oil
  • 125 g yellow onion small dice
  • 20 g garlic minced
  • salt
  • 175 g mushrooms small dice
  • 2 g fresh thyme minced
  • 125 g marsala wine if non-alcoholic: 100g beef stock + 10g Worcestershire + pinch sugar
  • 200 g heavy cream
  • 450 g frozen cheese ravioli
  • grated parmesan
Instructions
  1. In a Dutch oven or wide bottom sauce pan over medium high heat, add in olive oil, onion, garlic and a pinch of salt. Proceed to sweat the onions and garlic for about 5 minutes or until all the moisture has been cooked out and the onions are translucent and soft.
  2. Add in the mushrooms, a pinch of salt and a little more oil if needed. Cook the mushrooms for about 5 minutes, stirring constantly until they have released their liquid and have shrunk slightly.
  3. Stir in thyme and cook for another 6-7 minutes until the mushrooms have lightly browned.
  4. Deglaze the pan with the marsala. Reduce until it is nearly dry.
  5. Add in cream and a splash water. Simmer for 5 minutes until the sauce coats the back of a spoon.
  6. Bring a pot of water to a boil and add in a strong pinch of salt. Cook the ravioli in the boiling water for 3-4 minutes, or until they are all floating. Using a slotted spoon or spider, remove the ravioli from the water and begin tossing them in the sauce gently. You may need to add a splash of pasta water to adjust the consistency of the sauce. The sauce should be saucy but not watery.
  7. Taste the sauce and pasta and adjust the seasoning and consistency if necessary.
  8. Serve up the ravioli and top with more parmesan.

Sheet Pan Fajitas

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 650 g boneless skinless chicken thighs
  • 1 large red onion julienned
  • 450 g bell peppers 1 large red, yellow, and orange bell
  • 100 g poblano 1 large
  • 12 g kosher salt
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 10 g garlic powder
  • 10 g onion powder
  • 10 g ground cumin
  • 10 g chili powder
  • 1 g red pepper flakes
  • 1 g dried oregano
  • juice of 1 lime
  • 25 g olive oil
  • 100 g sour cream
  • 150 g mayo
  • 25 g jalapeno
  • 1 garlic clove
  • 25 g cilantro 1 bunch
  • zest of most of a lime
  • Juice of 1 lime
  • 3 g kosher salt
  • queso fresco grated, sub crumbled feta
  • puffy flour tortillas heated
  • cilantro jalapeno crema
Instructions
  1. Preheat your oven and sheet pan on 500°F/260°C.
  2. Cut chicken into strips that are 1/2-1/4″ wide (1cm or so). Add to a bowl.
  3. Cut onion in half, pole to pole, then slice into 1/4″ pieces Slice of the sides and bottom of the bell peppers so you're left with large planks that leave behind most of the ribs and seeds. Remove any white pith left behind on the peppers then slice the pepper planks into about a ½" or 1½cm. Add to the bowl with chicken and onions.
  4. Slice off the outer planks of the poblano, leaving behind the core and seeds and slice into the same size as the bell peppers. Add to bowl.
  5. Add salt pepper, spices/herbs, lime, and olive oil into bowl and mix to coat.
  6. Spread meat and veg onto a flat layer on your now preheated sheet tray – you should hear a sizzle. Return tray to 500℉/260°C oven and cook for 15 minutes, gently stirring halfway through.
  7. Remove sheet tray from oven, and stir chicken and veg once more, turn the oven broiler on high and load sheet tray under the broiler to take on some nice char for about 3 minutes, stir, then return to broiler for another 2-3 minutes, keeping an eye on things to be sure it doesn’t burn. At this point, the chicken and veg should all have some nice browning and charred bits, kind of like you’d get on a grill.
  8. Blend ingredients in a blender until combined and smooth.

Spaghetti Chow Mein

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 225 g thin spaghetti
  • kosher salt
  • 225 g button mushrooms halved and sliced
  • 150 g snow peas cut into bite sized pieces
  • 125 g carrot julienned/shredded
  • 150 g red bell pepper thinly sliced
  • 4-6 scallions root half only, chunky sliced, save and thinly slice the greens for garnish
  • 25 g ginger grated
  • 15 g garlic grated
  • 75-100 g bean sprouts
  • 20 g high smoke point oil avocado, peanut, canola, light olive, etc
  • 75 g soy sauce
  • 75 g mirin
  • 35 g sesame oil
  • 25 g oyster sauce or vegetarian hoisin
  • sesame seeds for garnish
  • scallions greens from above
Instructions
  1. Bring a large pot of water to a boil. Season with a generous pinch of salt
  2. Boil pasta the pasta until al dente, for most thin spaghetti this will take 6 minutes. When the pasta is done cooking, strain and toss the noodles in a high smoke point oil.
  3. Preheat large skillet or wok over med-high heat then add long squeeze of high smoke point oil, mushrooms and a pinch salt. Cook for about 3 min or until they've lost their moisture and are taking on a golden color. When the mushrooms are done cooking, transfer them to a bowl and set them aside.
  4. Return the pan to high heat and add another squeeze of oil, snow peas, bell pepper, and a pinch of salt. Cook and stir for 60-90 seconds or until beginning to char, but still a bit crunchy. Transfer the vegetables to the small bowl as the mushrooms.
  5. Return the pan to high heat and add another long squeeze of oil followed by the spaghetti noodles. Stir fry for 2-3 min until well dehydrated on the outside. Once beginning to take on color, add ginger, garlic, scallions, and shredded carrot. Toss and stir fry until raw edge has cooked off of the aromatics.
  6. Add back in the mushrooms, peas, bell peppers, raw bean sprouts, and stir fry sauce. Stir and toss until sauce reduces to a glaze and everything is combined, this will take about 1 minute.
  7. Garnish each portion of chow mien with sesame seeds and scallions.
  8. Stir ingredients to combine.

Speed Scratch Chicken Soup

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • olive oil
  • 600 g frozen precut mirepoix mix carrot, onion, celery, 2 10oz bags
  • 3 g garlic powder
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 1-2 g dried oregano
  • 75 g chicken better than bouillon
  • 1800 g chicken stock
  • 150 g orzo sub broken ramen noodles or frozen rice for a gluten free option
  • 150 g swiss chard leaves chopped, or other leafy green of choice, 3-4 large leaves
  • 450 g rotisserie chicken meat or baked chicken thighs, chopped
  • 2-3 g turmeric
  • 150 g frozen peas
Instructions
  1. Add a generous squeeze of olive oil into a large preheated heavy bottomed pot over high heat followed by mirepoix.
  2. Stir and cook for 1-2 minutess until thawed and starting to soften.
  3. Add garlic powder, pepper, and oregano (feel free to eyeball it). Stir and fry for about a minute until fragrant.
  4. Stir in chicken bouillon paste then chicken stock.
  5. Add orzo (if subbing orzo with frozen rice wait to add until later). Bring soup to a simmer.
  6. About 4-5 minutes from when orzo/pasta is done cooking, stir chard/greens. Reduce heat to medium/med-low and cook until greens are tender and orzo is cooked to al dente.
  7. Stir in turmeric, frozen peas, and chopped chicken just to heat through. If using frozen or pre-cooked rice, add it during this step.
  8. Taste for seasoning and adjust with salt if needed. I usually add a large pinch of salt or 2 at this step. Be sure to stir salt in well to dissolve before adding more.
  9. Garnish with black pepper, a drizzle of olive oil, a squeeze of lemon juice, and fresh dill if you have it.
  10. Salt boneless, skinless thighs and lay out on a sheet tray. Bake at 450°F/230°C for 13-15 minutes.

Weeknight Banh Mi

Serves 4
Ingredients

  • 150 g carrot shredded

  • 100 g white vinegar
  • 40 g sugar
  • 4 g salt
  • 900 g ground Pork
  • 10 g salt
  • 25 g sugar
  • 5 g black pepper
  • 10 g corn starch
  • 60 g fish sauce
  • 30 g garlic grated/minced, 5-6 cloves
  • 30 g ginger grated
  • 60 g shallot finely minced
  • 20 g fresh basil
  • 120 g water
  • 45 g fish sauce
  • 25 g granulated sugar
  • 25 g lime juice 1 whole
  • 10 g garlic minced, 2-3 cloves
  • 10 g sambal
  • 1 english cucumber partially peeled and cut into thin planks
  • 1 jalapeno thinly sliced
  • cilantro rough chopped
  • store-bought baguettes/hoagies
  • mayonnaise duke’s or homemade
Instructions

  3. Preheat oven to 475°F/245°C
  4. In a bowl combine all ingredients and mix until well incorporated.

1 Hour Grandma Pizza

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 210 g beer warmed to 80-90°F
  • 7 g instant yeast
  • 15 g olive oil
  • 350 g all-purpose flour
  • 5 g salt
  • 8 g sugar
  • 150-170 g tomato paste 1 small can
  • 150 g water
  • 10 g sugar
  • 2 g salt
  • 1 g black pepper
  • ½ g dried basil
  • ½ g dried oregano
  • 1 g onion powder
  • 1 g garlic powder
  • 10 g olive oil
  • 450 g ground pork 80/20 fat
  • 7 g salt
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 2 g ground fennel seed
  • 3-5 g chili flakes
  • 10 g minced garlic 2 cloves
  • 5 g red wine vinegar
  • olive oil for sheet pan
  • 10-12 thin slices of full-fat aged mozzarella cheese
  • 10-12 thin slices of provolone cheese
  • grated Parmesan cheese
  • crumbled sausage recipe above
  • sauce recipe above
  • olive oil for sheet pan
  • 10-12 thin slices of full-fat aged mozzarella cheese
  • 10-12 thin slices of provolone cheese
  • grated Parmesan cheese
  • 225 g baby spinach
  • 20-25 garlic slices soaked briefly in olive oil
  • 30-40 pieces of partially rendered thick-cut bacon
  • 50 mL 50/50 mix of olive oil and lemon juice for drizzling
Instructions
  1. Stir yeast into to warmed (not hot) beer. Add olive oil and whisk.
  2. In a separate large bowl, combine the flour, salt, and sugar. Stir to combine.
  3. Pour the wet ingredients into the dry ingredients. Stir until the dough comes together and absorbs the liquid.
  4. Once the dough becomes shaggy and hard to stir, wet your hands and knead the dough in the bowl until it forms a cohesive ball.
  5. Transfer the dough to a floured countertop or cutting board and knead for about 2 minutes, until smooth and elastic. It should stretch without tearing easily.
  6. Place the dough in a bowl, cover it, and let it rise for 20-30 minutes while you prepare the toppings and sauce.
  7. In a bowl, combine the tomato paste, sugar, salt, black pepper, basil, oregano, onion powder, garlic powder, and olive oil.
  8. In a large bowl, mix together ground pork with salt, black pepper, fennel seed, chili flakes, minced garlic, and vinegar.
  9. Spread onto a baking sheet and bake at 350°F / 175°C for 12-15 minutes until almost cooked through.
  10. Once cool, crumble the sausage by hand
  11. Preheat your oven to 550°F / 285°C (or the hottest your oven will go) with a pizza steel or stone inside.
  12. Liberally oil a half-sheet pan with about 3 tablespoons of olive oil, spreading it up the sides.
  13. On a floured surface, roll out the dough to about 80% of the sheet pan’s size. Place it in the pan, stretching it to the edges.
  14. Poke any bubbles with a fork, then dock the dough 40-50 times to prevent bubbling.
  15. Spoon the sauce onto the pizza in two wide strips, leaving some sections cheesier and others saucier.
  16. Sprinkle crumbled sausage across the top and finish with a generous layer of grated Parmesan cheese.
  17. Bake for 13-15 minutes until the crust is golden and crisp.
  18. Prep the dough in the sheet pan as listed above.
  19. Layer the mozzarella and provolone slices evenly over the dough.
  20. Mound the spinach evenly across the dough
  21. Top with garlic slices and partially rendered bacon pieces.
  22. Sprinkle with grated Parmesan cheese.
  23. Bake at 550°F/285C for 15 minutes or until the spinach has wilted and the bacon is crispy.
  24. Drizzle with a mix of olive oil and lemon juice, then finish with an extra sprinkle of Parmesan before serving

1 Hour Pan Pizza

Serves 1
Ingredients
  • 180 g beer warmed to 90-95F/32-35C
  • 10 g instant yeast
  • 5 g granulated sugar
  • 15 g olive oil
  • 9 g kosher salt
  • 280 g bread flour
  • 800 g crushed tomatoes canned
  • 100 g tomato paste
  • 20 g granulated sugar
  • 12 g kosher salt
  • 2 g onion powder
  • 2 g garlic powder
  • 1 g dried oregano
  • 1 g dried basil
  • pinch chile flakes
  • 250 g full fat mozzarella shredded
  • pepperoni
  • 150 g beer warmed to 90-95F/32-35C C
  • 8 g instant yeast
  • 4 g granulated sugar
  • 13 g olive oil
  • 8 g kosher salt
  • 240 g bread flour
Instructions
  1. Warm beer in saucepot over low heat. Transfer to bowl and add yeast. Mix in sugar, olive oil, and salt. Add flour and stir to combine with a spoon before switching to a soaking wet hand to finish squeezing and mixing the dough until it forms a cohesive ball.
  2. Cover and ferment at room temp for 15min.
  3. Perform a strength building fold. Cover and ferment at room temp for another 15min.
  4. Transfer dough into a well oiled/sprayed 12″ cast iron (or split between two 8″ square pans). With oiled fingers, press dough out to fill the shape of the pan. Cover and allow to rest for 10min.
  5. Preheat oven to 550F/285C with baking stone or steel. Use finger tips to press dough out into the pan the rest of the way.
  6. Combine all ingredients and pulse with an immersion blender until smooth, about 30 sec.
  7. Spread 4-5 generous spoonfuls of sauce over the dough followed by 1/2lb or 250g of shredded mozzarella, then pepperonis (and any other toppings you'd like).
  8. Allow to proof on the counter for 10-15min then bake on top of baking steel for 16-18min.

  • Olive oil

  • 4 large red bell peppers
  • 2 white onions
  • 1 red onion
  • 4-5 garlic cloves

  • 200 g white distilled vinegar 1c
  • 40 g sugar 1/4c
  • 4 g salt 1t
  • Red onions sliced
  • Flour tortillas
  • Ranchero sauce mayo & salsa

  • Cucumbers

  • pepper

  • Black beans

  • Korean BBQ sauce

  2. Place chicken in pot, cover with chicken stock.
  3. Season w salt & black pepper. Bring to boil, reduce heat to med-low, cover, cook 45-55 min.
  4. Transfer cooked chicken & 1/2c of the broth into container. Shred & store in fridge until ready to use.
  5. Cook rice, water, & a large pinch of salt in a rice cooker.

  16. Heat beef, rice, pepper/onion mix, & beans in microwave. Add shredded cheese, shredded lettuce, pickled red onion, & salsa or ranchero sauce.
  17. Heat beef in nonstick, add 1/2c korean bbq sauce to glaze. Add to bowl with warm rice & rabe. Top w shredded carrot, sliced cucumbers, kimchi, bibimbap sauce, & sunny side up egg.
  18. Add 2-3 Tbsp chili crisp to large nonstick, add chopped rabe & 1c ground beef mix. Toss & warm. Add handful of spaghetti, 1/4c water, 1T peanut butter & 1T soy sauce.
  19. Heat nonstick pan w olive oil, add 1-2Tbsp chopped calabrian chiles, 1Tbsp tom paste, 1/2c finely chopped onion/pepper mix, 3-4T water & 2-3T butter. Swirl to melt & emulsify. Add lrg handful spaghetti. Toss. Off heat add generous amount of parm. Top with parm & black pepper to serve.

30 Minute Khao Soi

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 30 g ginger grated
  • 15 g lemongrass grated
  • 25 g garlic grated or minced, 6-7 cloves
  • zest of 1 lime
  • 50 g red onion minced, 1/4 of a red onion
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 12 g turmeric ground
  • 10 g curry powder
  • 3 g coriander ground
  • 5 g paprika
  • 1-2 fresno chile minced, optional
  • 700 g boneless skinless chicken thighs
  • kosher salt
  • 40 g coconut oil
  • 800 mL coconut milk 2 cans, 14oz/414mL each
  • 1 kg chicken stock Full tutorial in this video: https://youtu.be/oLwuUJcFJvY, 4c
  • high smoke point frying oil canola, vegetable, coconut
  • Chinese fresh egg noodles sub regular fresh egg noodles, ramen noodles, or rice noodles
  • 30 g fish sauce
  • 25 g brown sugar
  • 20-25 g Sambal chili sauce
  • red onion thinly sliced, and rinsed under cold water
  • cilantro
  • lime wedge
  • crunchy fried noodles see below
  • pickled mustard greens If you can’t find, look for a mellow sauerkraut or sour deli pickle. Essentially you want something tangy and crunchy
Instructions
  1. Stir ingredients to combine into a paste
  2. Salt chicken on both sides. And set aside for a few minutes while you make the curry paste.
  3. Preheat large dutch oven or pot over med high then add coconut oil. When melted, add curry paste (recipe above) and pinch of salt. Stir and cook for 60-90 seconds until aromatic and beginning to glaze pot.
  4. Stir in coconut milk and chicken stock to combine and bring to a simmer. Once simmering, carefully lay in chicken thighs. Once simmering again, reduce heat to low to cook for 15-20 minutes or until thighs are fully cooked and tender.
  5. Once tender and breaking apart easily, remove chicken from broth. Add fish sauce, brown sugar, and sambal to soup and stir to combine.
  6. Cut chicken into bite sized pieces and return them to the broth.
  7. Taste for seasoning and adjust if needed with fish sauce and/or chili sauce.
  8. For the noodles in the soup, bring water to a boil, salt liberally, and boil until al dente. If using fresh noodles, this should take 2-3 minutes depending on the noodle.
  9. For the crunchy fried noodle topping, add about 1"/2.5cm of oil to a medium sauce pot and heat over medium high. Once oil reaches 350°F/175°C, drop in a small bundle of the fresh egg noodles to fry for about 15-20 seconds per side or until puffed up and taking on some color.
  10. Once done, transfer to towel to drain excess oil. If subbing in packaged ramen, no need to fry. These can be crunched on top of the soup straight from the package.

5 Ingredient Mexican: Arroz Con Pollo

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 5 lb bone-in 2.5kg, skin-on chicken thighs
  • 25 g olive oil 2T
  • 20 g coarse salt 4t
  • 5 g black pepper 2t
  • 5 g paprika 2t
  • 5 g ground cumin 2t
  • 5 g turmeric 2t
  • 10 g onion powder 1T
  • 5 g garlic powder 1t
  • 400 g jasmine rice, rinsed 2 1/4c
  • 5 g cumin 2t
  • 5 g garlic powder 1t
  • 2 g turmeric 3/4t
  • 2 g onion powder 1/2t
  • 7 g coarse salt 1 1/2t
  • 500 g chunky salsa 1 3/4c
  • 650 g water 2 3/4c
  • 150 g frozen peas 1+c
  • Chopped cilantro
Instructions
  1. Mix chicken thighs with olive oil, salt, pepper, paprika, cumin, turmeric, onion powder, garlic powder.
  2. Roast chicken in 450°F/230c oven on a sheet tray with wire rack sprayed with pan spray, 30-35 minutes until skin is crispy.
  3. In large deep pot, add olive oil, then saute rinsed jasmine rice 3-4 minsuntil translucent.
  4. Add cumin, garlic powder, turmeric, onion powder, and salt. Fry for another minute.
  5. Stir in salsa. Cook for 1-2min until cooked down.
  6. Add water, bring to simmer. Add peas, cover with lid, bake in oven with chicken for 15 mins. Remove from oven & rest, lid-on for 10 mins.
  7. Fold cilantro into rice.

5 Ingredient Mexican: Slow Cooker Al Pastor

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 1.5 kg boneless pork shoulder cut into 6-8 chunks
  • 30 g coarse salt
  • 5 g cumin
  • 5 g oregano
  • 10 g paprika
  • 30 g chili powder
  • 2 bay leaves optional
  • 550 g canned pineapple 2 20oz cans
  • white onion small diced and rinsed
  • fresh cilantro chopped
  • corn tortillas
  • hot sauce and/or squeeze of lime
Instructions
  1. Place your pork chunks in a slow cooker with salt, cumin, oregano, paprika, chili powder, bay leaves, & juice from 2 cans of pineapple. Slow cook on low for 7-8 hours until tender.
  2. Pull apart and shred half of the cooked pork, discard any really fatty parts. Chop the pork on a cutting board, not too much though, be sure to leave the pieces bite sized and not much smaller. (freeze other half for later).
  3. Cut the canned pineapple into small bite sized pieces.
  4. Caramelize about a cup of pineapple pieces for 2-3 min in olive oil over med-high. Add in chopped pork and fry until crispy around edges, about 3-5 minutes.
  5. Pour in a cup of the pork cooking liquid and cook until reduced.
  6. Assemble the taco on a corn tortilla and top with diced white onion, cilantro and your favorite hot sauce.

AMAZING Tikka Masala Home

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 10 g cumin
  • 10 g paprika
  • 10 g curry powder
  • 10 g garam masala
  • 3 g chile flake
  • 1 kg boneless skinless chicken thighs about 12
  • half of the tikka masala spice blend
  • olive oil
  • 200 g plain Greek yogurt
  • 20 g kosher salt
  • 20 g lemon juice about ½ a lemon
  • 20 g garlic grated,4 cloves
  • 20 g ginger grated
  • 1 large whole onion peeled, grated
  • 20 g garlic grated, 4 cloves
  • 20 g ginger grated
  • 30 g serrano chile diced, 1 small chile
  • 30 g ghee or olive/coconut oil
  • strong pinch of salt
  • other half of tikka masala spice blend
  • 800 g crushed tomatoes canned
  • 3 g kosher salt
  • 5-7 g sugar
  • 250 g heavy cream
Instructions
  1. Prepare the tikka masala spice blend by combining cumin, paprika, curry powder, garam masala, and chile flake in a bowl. Stir together and divide in half.
  2. Place chicken thighs in a medium bowl. Add half of the tikka masala spice blend, a drizzle of olive oil, Greek yogurt, salt, lemon juice, garlic, and ginger. Mix well to coat. Marinate in the fridge for 15 minutes, but ideally 1 hour
  3. Grate onion, garlic, and ginger. Dice the serrano Chile.
  4. In a heavy-bottomed pot, melt ghee or oil over medium heat. Add grated onion, garlic, ginger, and diced serrano. Sprinkle with a strong pinch of salt and sauté until fragrant and softened, about 2-3 minutes
  5. Add the remaining half of the tikka masala spice blend and toast the spices for about a minute. Stir in the crushed tomatoes and scrape up any browned bits from the bottom of the pot.
  6. Turn the heat to low and simmer for about 2 minutes until slightly thickened. Stir in salt, sugar, and heavy cream.
  7. Preheat the grill to its highest setting. Oil the grates well to prevent sticking. Grill the marinated chicken thighs for 5-6 minutes. Flip chicken over with a spatula and continue to cook and char for 2-3 minutes on the second side.
  8. Chop the grilled chicken into bite-sized pieces. Stir the chopped chicken into the masala gravy. If the gravy is too thick, add a splash of water to achieve the desired consistency.

65m

Baja Fried Fish Tacos Recipe

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 200 g little gem or romaine lettuce shaved thin
  • 200 g tomato small diced
  • 10 g cilantro chopped
  • 10 g jalapeno small diced
  • 2 g salt
  • ½ lime juiced
  • 200 g mayonnaise
  • 60 g sambal or sriracha
  • 2 lbs fresh Atlantic cod or haddock
  • salt
  • neutral oil canola, avocado, etc
  • 20 flour tortillas small
  • 25 g all-purpose flour
  • 25 g white rice flour
  • 75 g all-purpose flour
  • 75 g white rice flour
  • 2 g salt
  • 2 g baking powder
  • 12 oz beer basic lager or pilsner
  • 1 red onion peeled and sliced thin
  • 200 g white distilled vinegar
  • 200 g water
  • 40 g sugar
  • 4 g salt
Instructions
  1. If you're making your own pickled red onions, bring all ingredients aside from onions to a boil, pour over onions, and let cool to room temperature. Best made the day before.
  2. Cut fish into 16–20 evenly thick planks that are approximately 1.5 ounces each, 4 inches long and 1–1½ inches wide. Season all sides lightly with salt and refrigerate uncovered.
  3. Whisk together 75 grams all-purpose flour, 75 grams rice flour, 2 grams salt and 2 grams baking powder. Slowly whisk in most of the beer until smooth and about the consistency of thin pancake batter. Add more beer if needed, then refrigerate until ready to fry.
  4. Cut the lettuce into short, thin strips. Small-dice the tomato, keeping its juice and seed pulp. Chop the cilantro and finely dice the jalapeño, if using. Keep everything separate for now. You'll combine later.
  5. Stir together the mayonnaise and sambal.
  6. To prep for frying, fill a large, heavy-bottomed pot no more than halfway with neutral oil. Set up a thermometer, a wire rack over a sheet tray, a spider and a fork next to the fry pot. Heat the oil to 375°F (190℃). Mix 25 grams all-purpose flour and 25 grams rice flour on a tray for the dredge.
  7. Remove the fish and batter from the refrigerator. Lightly dredge all the fish, shaking off any visible excess.
  8. Coat each piece of fish in batter, letting excess drip off. Using a fork to support the fish from below, slowly lower one end into the oil away from your body. Hold it near the surface for a second so the batter begins to set, then release into the oil.
  9. Fry about six pieces at a time without letting them touch. Keep the oil temperature between 350℉ (176℃) and 365℉ (185℃) and fry for 3-5 minutes until deeply golden and crisp. When cooked, fish should be opaque, flake easily and reach 145℉ (63℃) in the thickest part. Carefully lift fish with spider or slotted spoon and drain on a wire rack. Allow oil to return to 375°F (190℃) before frying the next batch.
  10. While second batch fries, make the salad slaw by combining lettuce, tomato and its juices, cilantro jalapeno, lime juice, and 2 g salt.
  11. Warm the tortillas to heat through in a nonstick pan. Spread each with a thin layer of spicy mayo, then add the fried fish, salad, slaw, and pickled red onions. Serve immediately.

420m

Banh Mi with Crispy Baguette

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 1500 g pork belly skin removed, cut in half
  • 380 g warm water
  • 12 g instant yeast
  • 22 g sugar
  • 525 g bread flour
  • 12 g salt
  • 30 g lime juice
  • 30 g brown sugar
  • 30 g fish sauce
  • 30 g sambal chili paste
  • 150 g diakon shredded
  • 150 g carrot shredded
  • 200 g water
  • 100 g rice vinegar
  • 100 g white vinegar
  • 40 g sugar
  • 4 g salt
  • mayonnaise
  • mint leaves
  • cilantro
  • cucumber cut into long thin strips
  • fresh jalapenos thinly sliced
Instructions
  1. Sprinkle generously on both sides with salt.
  2. Roast on wire rack in 300F/150C oven for 3 hours.
  3. Allow to cool then slice against the grain into about ¾”/2cm slabs.
  4. Add ingredients to a stand mixer with dough hook attachment. Mix on high for 6-8 minutes until dough has formed a ball and clears sides of the bowl. Alternatively you can knead by hand until dough comes together and is no longer a shaggy mess.
  5. Transfer to a bowl and round it into a ball by rotating it and tucking up and under itself.
  6. Cover and allow to ferment at room temp for 60 min. The dough should have risen a lot.
  7. Punch it down to release some gas, then do a strength building fold by grabbing a section on the side of the dough, pulling it up until you meet resistance, then folding it over to the opposite side of the dough ball. Rotate 45 degrees and repeat 4-5 times around the bowl.
  8. Next flip the dough over and tuck and round into a ball as you did before. Cover, and let ferment at room temp for another 60 minutes. The dough should have risen a lot again.
  9. Flour dough and work surface and divide the dough into 6 equal pieces (about 150g each). Preshape each piece into a ball.
  10. Once preshaped into balls, cover them with a damp towel to relax for 15-20 minutes at room temperature.
  11. Shape baguettes by pressing the dough out into a square. then roll it into itself to make a cigar shaped roll. Using your thumbs and pinky fingers on each side of the dough, push them back and forth to create tension on the top of the dough. Push the palms of your hands down on the edges as you are doing this to taper off the edges and place each loaf into a baguette pan. If you don't have a baguette pan, check out my beginner baguette video on YouTube here https://youtu.be/5FRGt0CSo to see how to bake these using a sheet tray.
  12. Cover shaped baguettes with damp towel and proof for 45 min.
  13. Preheat oven to 400°F/205°C with baking stone/steel on top rack and cast iron skillet on bottom rack. When baguettes are proofed and ready to bake, they should indent when you poke them, then slowly bounce back.
  14. Score diagonally across the top of each baguette with a bread lame or sharp knife. Spray top of loaves liberally with water then load into preheated oven on top of baking stone/steel. Pour boiling water into cast iron skillet on bottom rack and close oven door quickly to bake for 30-35mins.
  15. Loaves are done when toasty and golden brown. Brush with melted butter
  16. Bring water, vinegars, sugar, & salt to a boil. Pour over the mix of shredded carrot and daikon.
  17. Combine ingredients and stir until fully incorporated.
  18. To prep the pork belly, sear 3/4in/2cm slabs in a saute pan over medium/med-high until just browned, crisp on both sides, and warmed through (about 1 minute per side)
  19. Slice baguette open on one side, creating a sandwich pocket.
  20. Shingle in 3-4pcs of pork belly then place in 425F/220C oven to refresh and crisp bread for 2-3min.
  21. Spread on mayo then add cucumber, jalapenos, a good amount of the carrot-daikon pickle, fresh herbs, and a generous spoonful or 3 of the sandwich juice.

BBQ Chicken Pizza

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 400 g warm water 86F/30C
  • 6 g instant yeast
  • 20 g sugar
  • 20 g olive oil
  • 680 g ap flour OR bread flour you’re looking for at least 11.7% protein if using all purpose
  • 16 g salt
  • 450 g chicken breast filets
  • 10 g salt
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 3 g garlic powder
  • 10 g olive oil
  • 10 g brown sugar
  • 3 g liquid smoke
  • 400 g ketchup
  • 75 g worcestershire
  • 60 g apple cider vinegar
  • 40 g hot sauce
  • 5 g garlic powder
  • 5 g onion powder
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 5 g chili powder
  • 150 g molasses
  • 150 g brown sugar
  • 200 g pineapple juice
  • smoked gruyere shredded, sub with gouda
  • full fat aged mozzarella shredded
  • 10-12 slices thick cut bacon rendered and cut into large squares
  • sliced red onion
  • chopped cilantro
  • grilled chicken recipe above
  • BBQ sauce recipe above
Instructions
  1. Mix ingredients in a stand mixer with the dough hook on medium speed for about 3 minutes or until the dough has formed into a mass of dough. At this point, flip out onto a work surface and knead by hand for 4-5 more minutes.
  2. When the dough is no longer sticky and you can pull a piece without it shearing or tearing, it's ready to shape into a round. Roll the dough onto your counter. To build surface tension, cup the side of your hand against the table and gently pull the dough toward you. You’ll see a smooth, tight skin form on top. Place in a bowl, cover, and allow to sit at room temperature for 1 hour.
  3. After the hour, the dough should have risen by 30-50%. At this point, cut the dough into 4 equal (275g) pieces. De-gas each piece with your fingertips then shape them into balls using the same rounding technique as used in the previous step.
  4. Moved the shaped dough balls onto a lightly oiled sheet tray (or into containers with lids. I use these), wrap tightly and refrigerate for 8 hours to several days.
  5. Before you’re ready to make pizza, remove the dough balls from fridge and allow them to come to room temperature before just before shaping.
  6. stretch your dough into a pizza shape and then load onto pizza peel before adding sauce and toppings.
  7. Place chicken breasts into a freezer bag and use a heavy bottomed pot or mallet to pound into an even thickness. Add salt, pepper, garlic powder, olive oil, brown sugar, and liquid smoke to the bag. Rub ingredients onto chicken to combine and coat, then marinate in the fridge for 30-40 minutes.
  8. For best results, grill chicken over high heat for 4-5min, flip to side 2 and cook for additional 30 seconds. Cooking in a hot oven or in saute pan will also work.
  9. When done, the internal temperature of the chicken should read 150F/65C.
  10. Once rested, dice chicken into bitesize squares.
  11. Add ingredients to a sauce pot and bring to a boil then lower the heat to medium low to reduce sauce by about half (should take about 20 min). Be sure it’s thick!
  12. Again, before you’re ready to make pizza, remove the dough balls from fridge and allow them to come to room temperature before just before shaping.
  13. Stretch the doughs out into a pizza shape, then load onto pizza peel before adding sauce and toppings.
  14. Start with a few spoonfuls of the bbq sauce to create a thin layer
  15. Next add shredded mozzarella and gruyere
  16. 12 or so slices of red onion
  17. 12-20 pieces of rendered bacon
  18. 12-20 pieces of diced grilled chicken.
  19. Stretch pizza dough to 12″/30cm then load into 550F/285C oven onto preheated pizza stone/steel to baked for 5-7 min.
  20. Rotate halfway through cooking if needed.
  21. Top with additional bbq sauce and chopped cilantro.

Beef and Broccoli

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 450 g flank steak sliced ¼ in thick, 1 lb
  • 350 g broccoli florets cut to 1–1½ in, 3½ c
  • 100 g onion thinly sliced, ¾ c
  • high smoke point oil
  • 10 g soy sauce 2 t
  • 5 g sesame oil 1 t
  • 5 g shaoxing wine or mirin 1 t
  • 2 g sugar ½ t
  • 2 g cornstarch ½ t
  • 2 g baking soda ½ t
  • 30 g soy sauce 2 T
  • 30 g beef stock or water 2 T
  • 30 g shaoxing wine or mirin 2 T
  • 50 g oyster sauce 3½ T
  • 10 g sugar 2½ t, use ~5 g if using mirin
  • 5–7 g cornstarch 1½–2 t
  • 10 g garlic minced, 2 t
  • 10 g fresh ginger grated, 1 T
Instructions
  1. Cut each flank piece of flank steak into 1 ½ inch x 1 inch x ⅛ inch pieces and marinate in soy, shaoxing, sesame oil, sugar, cornstarch, and baking soda.
  2. In a seasoned wok, sear half of the beef off on one side until dark brown, flip and cook for another 10 seconds and set aside. Repeat the process with the rest of the beef.
  3. In the wok saute off half of the broccoli and onions until the veg is tender but still snappy. Repeat with the rest of the broccoli and onions. Add in the garlic and ginger for the last 30 seconds of cooking.
  4. Into the wok add in all of the beef and broccoli and stir fry for about 15-20 seconds until everything is mixed together. Add in stir fry sauce, mix and bring to a boil. Add in cornstarch slurry and toss for another 20 seconds. Remove from the heat and serve with rice.

Beef Pad Kra Pao

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • THAI-INSPIRED GROUND BEEF PAD KRA PAO
  • 200 g green beans, chopped into 1" pieces ½lb
  • 100 g shallots or red onion, chopped 3 medium
  • 30 g oil, divided 2T
  • 450 g 85/15 ground beef 1lb
  • 2 g salt, divided 2-finger pinch
  • 15 g ginger, grated 1T
  • 15 g garlic, grated or pressed 1T
  • 150 g baby spinach, chopped 5oz
  • 50 g oyster sauce (or 30g/1½T hoisin) 2½T
  • 25 g fish sauce 1T
  • 25 g soy sauce 1T
  • 50 g beef or chicken stock (or water) 3T
  • 25 g brown sugar 2T
  • 15–20 g ½c loosely packed basil, chiffonade
Instructions
  1. Heat wok over high, add oil. Stir-fry green beans in 1T oil + pinch salt for 1½–2 min until charred. Remove beans, set aside.
  2. Add 1T oil to wok over high. Add beef + pinch salt. Cook 5 min until crumbled to pea size.
  3. Add shallots. Stir-fry 2–3 min. Add splash of water if sticking.
  4. Add garlic & ginger. Stir 1 min till fragrant.
  5. Add spinach + beans. Stir 1 min til spinach melts.
  6. Stir together oyster, fish, soy sauces, stock, and sugar then add around wok edge. Simmer 30–60 sec to glaze.
  7. Kill heat. Stir in basil. Serve w/ rice & crispy fried egg.

Beef Stew

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 5 lb beef chuck 2.5kg
  • Olive oil
  • Salt
  • 200 g or about 2c pearl onions peeled
  • 400 g or 3.4-4c carrots cut into big chunky pieces
  • 300 g or 4.5-5c mushrooms cut into large pieces
  • 115 g unsalted butter 1stick
  • 125 g or 3/4C + 3Tbsp AP flour gluten free works well too
  • 1800 g or 1.8L/qt good beef stock OR see my flavorful beef stock dupe below 450g/1pint dark stout beer (a flavorful GF beer works too)
  • 500 g/4c small golden potatoes partially peeled and cut into very large chunks
  • 2-3 bay leaves
  • 2-3 sprigs of thyme
  • 150 g or 1 heaping cup frozen peas
  • fresh cracked black pepper
  • 4 packets powdered gelatin
  • 1800 g or 2 boxes store bought beef stock
  • 30 g better than bouillon
  • 30 g tomato paste
  • 30 g worcestershire
  • 28 g powdered gelatin 4 packets
Instructions
  1. Preheat oven to 500F/260C.
  2. Remove band of fat in the center of the pieces of chuck. Reference video @0:36.
  3. Cut the rest of the meat into cubes. You should be left with about 3.5lb/1.5kg of meat.
  4. Add meat cubes to a bowl and drizzle with generous amount of olive oil and 2 large pinches of salt. Toss to coat and spread beef onto a half sheet tray and load into 500F/260C oven to sear for about 20-25mins until well browned.
  5. Preheat a large heavy-bottomed pot over medium high. Add a generous glug of olive oil followed by mushrooms, carrots, onions, and pinch of salt.
  6. Sweat the veg to par-cook for 3-5mins until its beginning to soften and caramelize.
  7. Transfer veg into a bowl and set aside.
  8. Return pot to stove over medium heat and add stick/115g butter.
  9. When butter is melted, add 100g or 3/4c of flour. Stir to cook, scraping up brown bits from bottom of the pot. Fry for 60-90 seconds until smooth and taking on color.
  10. Add beef stock and beer. Bring to a simmer, stirring and scraping frequently.
  11. Once simmering, add in beef.
  12. Add a splash of water to the beef cooking tray, scraping up beef fond. Add into pot with beef. Stir to combine.
  13. Load pot, uncovered, into 300F/150C oven and cook for 2 hours, stirring halfway through.
  14. Add potatoes into the bowl with the par-cooked vegetables and mix with 25g/3Tbsp flour. Toss to combine. (NOTE: if your stew is relatively thick at this point, omit tossing the veg in the flour or add less flour here).
  15. Remove stew pot from oven to carefully add potatoes/veggies into stew and gently stir. Add in bay and thyme.
  16. Return stew pot to oven to cook for 1 more hour.
  17. At this point, check the beef and potato for doneness. Beef should be tender and shreddable, but not shredded. Potato should be fork tender, but not mushy.
  18. Ladle off excess fat from top of stew (or allow to cool slightly, refridgerate, then scrape off fat from top once cooled). Stir peas into hot stew.
  19. Taste for seasoning and add salt if needed.
  20. Ladle into bowl, top with fresh cracked black pepper, and serve with a piece of rustic sourdough bread.
  21. Stir ingredients to combine and dissolve gelatin.
Full recipe →

Beef Stroganoff

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2 lbs thin cut sirloin 1kg
  • 50 g or 3Tbsp soy sauce for marinade
  • 35 g or 2Tbsp worcestershire 15g for beef marinade, 20g for sauce
  • 200 g white onion 1 medium diced
  • 450 g crimini or button mushrooms 1lb , sliced
  • 4-5 large garlic cloves minced/pressed
  • High smokepoint oil canola, grapeseed, avocado, etc
  • Salt 175g/1 glass dry white wine
  • 25 g or 1.5Tbsp tomato paste
  • 15 g or 2Tbsp ap flour or substitute with GF flour
  • 400 g or 1 2/3c beef broth either homemade bone broth or store bought with 3 packets of powdered gelatin stirred in
  • 25 g 1 2/3Tbsp dijon mustard
  • 3 g or 1.5tsp black pepper
  • 450 g Campanelle pasta 1lb, bronze die cut if you can find it
  • 65 g stick unsalted butter .5, cut into chunks
  • 75 g or 1/3c heavy cream
Instructions
  1. “Jaccard” sirloins by poking many times on both sides with a fork (or use a jaccard tenderizer if you have one)
  2. Place steaks into a bag or container with soy sauce and 15g of worcestershire, making sure to coat well and refrigerate for 15-20min.
  3. Dry beef well with towels before searing.
  4. Add a long glug of oil to pre heated heavy bottomed pot (over high heat) and carefully lay in steaks, making sure to press down on each for several seconds. Cook steaks on side 1 for about 90 seconds or until nicely browned. Flip to side 2 and cook for another 2-3 minutes.
  5. Transfer steaks to a plate to rest. Temp should read about 128-130F/53-55C.
  6. Add another squeeze of oil into the steak pot. Add mushrooms and pinch of salt, using a wooden spoon to scrape up beef fond. Saute for 4-5 minutes or until mushrooms have released some moisture, and most of the beef fond is scraped up.
  7. Add chopped onion and another pinch of salt. Stir and continue to saute for 3-4min, stirring every minute and continuing to scrape up fond on the bottom of the pan.
  8. Add garlic and cook for 10-15 seconds, then add wine. Stir to deglaze bottom of the pan. After about a minute or when wine has reduced, add tomato paste and flour. Stir. After about a minute of frying, flour and tomato paste should have glazed the bottom of the pot.
  9. Add beef broth. Stir to work up glaze from bottom of the pot and bring to a simmer. Stir in mustard, worcestershire, and black pepper. Reduce heat to low and simmer for 10 minutes, covered.
  10. When ready, the sauce should be thickened and glossy. Stir in heavy cream.
  11. Add pasta to a pot of boiling, well salted water. Stir and cook per package instructions (8-9min).
  12. When cooked to al denta, drain pasta, leaving about 1/4c of pasta water in the pot with the noodles. Add butter to the noodles and stir to emulsify as it melts.
  13. Slice steaks against the grain then cut in half (or into bite sized pieces) and add steak + any juices on the plate into the reduced mushroom wine sauce and stir.
  14. Cover and cook over low heat for about a minute. Taste for seasoning and salt if needed.
  15. Top buttered noodles with beef stroganoff sauce and garnish with chopped dill and parsley.

Birria Tacos

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 3 lbs /1.5kg beef chuck roast cut into 2-3 inch chunks
  • 20 g coarse kosher salt 1 T + 1 t
  • 45 g oil 3 T
  • 30 g guajillo chiles ~10
  • 30 g ancho chiles ~6
  • 675 g water 3 c
  • 175 g onion rough chopped, ~1 medium
  • 25 g garlic 6-7 cloves
  • 875 g store-bought beef stock divided, 3½ c, 675g + 200g
  • 20 g tomato paste 1 T
  • 1 large chipotle chile in adobo
  • 35 g achiote annatto paste 2 T
  • 2 g ground cumin 1 t
  • 2 g ground coriander 1 t
  • 2 g ground cinnamon 1 t
  • 3 g ground black pepper 1½ t
  • ⅛ t ground cloves
  • 1 g dried oregano ½ t
  • 20 g apple cider vinegar 1½ T
  • 10 g salt 2 t
  • 20 g agave syrup 1 T
  • 200 g braising adobo sauce 3/4c
  • 300 g beef stock 1 1/4c
  • 3-5 g Better Than Bouillon beef base ½-1 t
Instructions
  1. Corn tortillas
  2. Mexican melting cheese, Oaxaca, Chihuahua, or Monterey Jack
  3. Cut beef chuck roast into 2-3 inch chunks. Combine beef with 20g salt and 45g oil, tossing to coat evenly. Rest 20 minutes.
  4. Remove stems and seeds from dried chiles, tearing into chunks. Toast in a pot over medium-low heat for 2-3 minutes until fragrant. Add 675g water, onion, and garlic to the pot. Bring to a low simmer. Cover and simmer gently for 10 minutes.
  5. Drain cooking water, then transfer the softened mixture to a blender. Add 675g beef stock, tomato paste, chipotle chiles, achiote paste, cumin, coriander, cinnamon, pepper, cloves, oregano, apple cider vinegar, salt, and agave to the blender. Blend until smooth.
  6. Broil beef chunks on high for 15 minutes until well browned. Deglaze pan with remaining 200g beef stock; scrape up fond and add to pot along with beef and adobo puree.
  7. Bring to a simmer on the stovetop then cover, and braise in a 300°F/150C oven for 2 hours. Check beef tenderness. It should have some give, but not be falling apart. If ready, rest 30 minutes covered. Shred gently by hand into chunks.
  8. For consommé, mix 200g braising adobo with 300g beef stock and bouillon. Heat to dissolve. Brush corn tortillas lightly with braising adobo sauce on both sides.
  9. In a nonstick pan over medium-low heat, fry tortillas in oil. Top half of each tortilla with cheese and 3oz/100g shredded beef. Fold over and fry 4-5 minutes per side over med-low until crispy and browned.
  10. Rest briefly on a wire rack. Serve quesabirria tacos with hot consommé for dipping.

Braised Beef Short Ribs

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 5-6 lb bone-in beef short ribs 2.5kg
  • Neutral oil for searing
  • Salt
  • 1000 g yellow onions, medium diced 3-4 large
  • 20-25 g garlic, pressed 6-7 cloves
  • Olive oil
  • 50 g Worcestershire sauce 3T
  • 50 g tomato paste 3T
  • 1 container of demi-glace 42.5g – sub with 25 grams beef bouillon paste
  • 2 cans about 900 grams Guinness Stout (substitute with red wine or 1 can of cola for gluten-free or alcohol-free options)
  • 1100 g store-bought beef stock mL
  • 4 packets 28g powdered gelatin
  • 4-5 fresh thyme sprigs
  • 3 bay leaves
  • 300 g polenta 1 3/4c
  • 1200 g whole milk 5c
  • 7 g salt 1 1/2t
  • 115 g unsalted butter 1 stick
  • 100 g grated Parmesan cheese 1c
Instructions
  1. Preheat the oven to 500°F (260°C).
  2. Rub each beef short rib with neutral oil and season liberally with salt. Allow the meat to cure for 20 minutes.
  3. Place the seasoned short ribs on a sheet tray and oven-sear for 10-15 minutes until browned.
  4. In a large, heavy-bottomed pot, heat olive oil over medium heat. Add diced onions, pressed garlic, and a pinch of salt. Stir occasionally and caramelize the onions for about 20 minutes, deglaze the pot with a splash of water every 5-6 minutes to incorporate the browned onion fond.
  5. When the onions are softened and starting to take on color, add Worcestershire. Stir and cook for about 30 seconds.
  6. Add tomato paste, stir and cook for about a minute.
  7. Add demi-glace, beer, beef stock, powdered gelatin, thyme sprigs, and bay leaves. Bring to a simmer.
  8. Place oven-seared short ribs into the pot, leaving the lid slightly ajar. Braise in a 275°F (135°C) oven for 3 to 3.5 hours.
  9. Cool the braised beef for 90 minutes, then refrigerate to cool completely. Once cooled, skim off coagulated beef fat from the top of the pot.
  10. Bring the pot to a low simmer to melt the gelatin.
  11. Once the gelatinous stock has become fully liquid, remove the short ribs from the liquid then ladle off any excessive fat from the top of the liquid. Continue to reduce the braising liquid by 75% over medium-high heat, about 20-30 minutes. note: This is a good time to make the polenta.
  12. Once the beef liquid is reduced, place the short ribs back into the reduced braising liquid to reheat for about 5 minutes over low heat while basting the short ribs.
  13. Toast polenta in a nonstick saucepan for 3 minutes over medium high heat.
  14. Add milk and salt, whisking until thickened (3-4 minutes). Reduce heat to low and cook for an additional 10 minutes, stirring occasionally.
  15. Stir in butter and grated Parmesan cheese until melted.

Braised Brisket & Noodle Kugel

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • Whole trimmed brisket about 8lb/3.5kg
  • Olive oil
  • 1 kg 3-4 large onions sliced
  • Salt
  • 1800 g beef stock 32oz, 2 boxes
  • 30 g or 1.5Tbsp Better bouillon beef base
  • 30 g or 1.75Tbsp worcestershire
  • 10 g or 1.5Tbsp paprika
  • 5 g or 3/4Tbsp garlic powder
  • 3 g or 1.5tsp black pepper
  • 1-2 g or 1-2tsp dried thyme
  • 200 g or 3/4c canned tomato sauce
  • 15 g or 1Tbsp anchovies finely minced
  • 200 g or 3/4c ketchup
  • 50 g or 3Tbsp A1 or other steak sauce of choice
  • Bloomed gelatin 4 packets of gelatine stirred with 40-50g water
  • 1 lb egg noodles .5kg, i’m using no yolks
  • 450 g or 1 3/4c sour cream
  • 450 g or 2c cottage cheese
  • 6 eggs
  • 12 g or 2tsp salt
  • 1-2 g or 1-2tsp dried thyme
  • 2 g or 3/4tsp black pepper
  • 150 g or 1 1/3c gruyere shredded
  • 113 g/1 stick butter
  • 200 g or 1large onion thinly sliced
  • 15 g or 3 garlic cloves minced
  • Buttered breadcrumbs 120g/2 1/8c panko breadcrumbs mixed with 30g/2-2.5Tbsp melted butter
Instructions
  1. Spread oil on each side of brisket then season both sides generously with salt. “Sear” on a rimmed sheet tray in 550F/287C oven for 10-15 minutes. Flip and continue to cook on the second side for 10-15 minutes. When properly seared, the outside should appear nicely browned.
  2. Add a long squeeze of olive oil into a large fry pan (i’m using a 12” cast iron) over high. Add sliced onions. Cook for 10-12 min over high heat (no salt added) until roasty and slightly caramelized.
  3. Add the roasted onions to a roasting pan and place the seared brisket on top. Add a bit of water to the sheet tray you cooked the brisket on and scrape up any brown bits. Add the resulting liquid into the roasting pan with the brisket and onions.
  4. To make the cooking liquid, whisk together beef stock, better than bouillon, worcestershire, paprika, garlic powder, pepper, thyme, tomato sauce, anchovies, ketchup, and A1. Pour the liquid over the beef in the roasting pan. Cover beef with a piece of parchment, and tightly wrap the entire pan with foil. Bake at 300F/150C for 4 hours.
  5. After 4 hours, remove foil and continue to cook for an additional 1-2 hours to reduce liquid and brown the beef. When done, beef should be tender and almost shreddable, but not falling apart. Allow meat to cool slightly, then refrigerate for 12-24 hours.
  6. Once cooled, remove excess fat from the top of the roasting pan. Remove brisket from cooking liquid and transfer roasting pan to the stove over a medium-high burner to reduce liquid by 2-3 times. Add bloomed gelatin if desired and continue to reduce for another 30 minutes or so to continue to thicken. Taste for seasoning and adjust with salt if needed.
  7. Slice brisket and shingle into roasting pan spooning the sauce on top. Cover with foil and hold in very low oven until ready to serve.
  8. Boil pasta in well salted water to package instructions.
  9. Spin sour cream, cottage cheese, eggs, salt, thyme, pepper, and gruyere in a food processor until cheeses are broken down and mixture is well combined. It will be quite loose.
  10. Melt butter in a fry pan over medium heat. Add onions, garlic, and a pinch of salt. Stir and sweat the onions for 5-8 minutes. Onions should soften, but not take on color. Transfer onions to a bowl.
  11. Add noodles to the onion bowl along with the sour cream/egg custard. Stir to combine and load into a well buttered 9”x13”/22x32cm baking dish. Top evenly with buttered bread crumbs then bake at 375F/190C for 50-60min.
  12. Allow to set before serving.

Broccoli Cheddar Soup

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 75 g olive oil
  • 350 g onion small dice
  • 125 g carrot small dice
  • 125 g fennel small dice
  • 350 g potatoes medium dice
  • 20 g garlic minced
  • 400 g broccoli cut down to bite size pieces
  • salt
  • 50 g flour
  • 1500 g chicken stock
  • 250 g cream
  • 225 g cheddar shredded
  • black pepper
Instructions
  1. Preheat large pot over med-high heat. Add oil. Add vegetables and a pinch of salt. Stir. Sweat veg for 6-8 mins. Once beginning to soften, add flour. Stir to coat veg and cook for a couple of minutes, just to cook off the raw edge, before adding in chicken stock and cream. Stir, reduce heat to med-low and continue to cook for 10min.
  2. When veggies are tender, but not mushy, reduce heat to low. Scoop about 3-4c of soup into a tall sided sauce pot and blend with immersion blender (or blend with standard blender). Add blended soup back into the original soup pot and stir. Stir in cheese. Taste for seasoning. I typically add an additional pinch of salt or two.
  3. Garnish with black pepper and an additional pinch of shredded cheese!

Bucatini Amatriciana

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 225 g guanciale diced, substitute pancetta or bacon
  • 60 g olive oil
  • 200 g diced red onion
  • 20 g minced garlic
  • 20 g Calabrian chiles
  • 25 g tomato paste
  • 100 g dry white wine
  • 400 g crushed tomatoes
  • 1 g oregano
  • 4 g sugar
  • Salt
  • 450 g bucatini
  • 40 g grated pecorino
Instructions
  1. Freeze the guanciale for 15 minutes and dice it into ½ inch cubes. In a pot over med heat, add in your guanciale and a splash of water. Cook guanciale, stirring often, for about 10 minutes until it is fully rendered but not browned. if needed add another splash of water to deglaze the pot if it is getting too brown.
  2. Remove the guanciale and fat from the pot and set aside.
  3. Return the same pot you rendered the guanciale in to the stove over medium heat. Add in oil, onion, garlic, a pinch of salt and a splash of water. Sweat for 5-10 minutes until the onion and garlic are fully cooked and translucent but not browned.
  4. Stir in the Calabrian chiles and cook for another 1-2 minutes and add in the tomato paste. Stir and cook for another 2 minutes.
  5. Deglaze with white wine and reduce to au sec.
  6. Stir in tomatoes, tomato paste, oregano, sugar, and salt. Bring the pot to a boil and reduce to a simmer over low heat and cook for another 10 minutes. Be sure to stir the pot every couple minutes or so.
  7. While the sauce is simmering, bring a pot of water to a boil. Add a strong pinch of salt to the water and boil the bucatini noodles for 9-11 minutes depending on how much bite you want in your noodles.
  8. When the noodles are cooked to your desired doneness, set aside a cup or so of the pasta water and strain off the noodles. When the noodles are strained off, add the noodles into the sauce along with a splash of pasta water. Give it a few stirs and throw in 40g of pecorino. continue stirring to melt the cheese into the sauce.
  9. Taste the pasta and check for seasoning and consistency, add more salt, cheese or pasta water and bring it to where it is saucy but not too wet or too dry. Twirl some noodles and sauce into a bowl to serve and top it with more cheese.

Buffalo Chicken Sandwich with Blue Cheese Sauce

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 6 boneless skinless chicken thighs
  • kosher salt
  • 300 g ap flour for breading mix,
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 2-3 g black pepper
  • 3 g cayenne pepper
  • 3 g paprika
  • 5 g garlic powder
  • 300 g buttermilk
  • 200 g ap flour for dredge
  • 75 g buttermilk
  • 60 g sour cream
  • 15 g lemon juice
  • 1/2 clove garlic
  • 10 g about ¼ of a rinsed shallot
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 1 egg yolk
  • 300 g neutral oil light olive, avocado, canola, etc
  • 100 g blue cheese
  • 300 g franks hot sauce or brand of your choice
  • 15 g minced garlic 3-4 cloves
  • 20 g worcestershire
  • 15 g white distilled vinegar
  • 150 g unsalted butter cubed
  • very small pinch xanthan gum optional
  • 125 g warm water 86°F/30°C
  • 3 g yeast
  • 45 g melted butter
  • 75 g mashed potato
  • 35 g sugar
  • 300 g ap flour 11.7% protein
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • egg wash 1 egg + 2 tsp water, stirred
Instructions
  1. Salt chicken on both sides.
  2. Combine dry ingredients (except 200g ap flour) in a bowl.
  3. In separate bowls add buttermilk in 1 bowl and 200g ap flour in another bowl. Pour a small amount of buttermilk into the seasoned breading mixture and whisk. It should still be dry and powdery, but clumpy.
  4. Dredge chicken first in the unseasoned AP flour, then in the buttermilk, then the breading mixture, pressing breading into the meat to be sure it sticks. Allow to sit for 5-10minutes before frying.
  5. Add all ingredients except cheese and oil to high sided container. Blend with immersion blender.
  6. When combined, slowly stream in oil until emulsified. Add about 50g (or 1/2 of the blue cheese) and blend until it’s mixed into the dressing.
  7. Add another 50g (or less if you don’t LOOVE blue cheese) and stir into dressing.
  8. Add all ingredients except butter and xanthan to sauce pan over medium high heat.
  9. Whisk ingredients together and allow to cook for 4-5 minutes until thickened slightly. Whisk in butter.
  10. Whisk in xanthan gum.
  11. Add 3qt neutral oil (or enough oil to give you 3"/7.5cm of depth in your fry pot) to a large heavy bottomed pot over medium heat until temp reaches 350°F/175°C.
  12. Gently lay thighs into oil away from you, only frying 2-3pc at a time. Fry for 4-5 minutes until golden brown.
  13. Once fried, place chicken in a bowl and stream buffalo sauce on to coat (recipe below).
  14. Toast bun Shingle on sliced dill pickles On top of that add 1-2 pieces butter lettuce Followed by chicken Followed by generous spoonful of blue cheese dressing (recipe above) Top with top buy and give it a loving squish before shoving it in your mouth
  15. Into the bowl of a stand mixer fitted with dough hook attachment, measure water, yeast, melted butter, mashed potato, sugar, flour, salt.
  16. Mix on low for 3 minutes until combined. Increase speed to med-high and mix 5-6 more minutes.
  17. Cover & allow to ferment at room temperature for 90 minutes.
  18. After that 90 minutess, flip out dough onto a lightly floured work surface. Divide dough into six 100g pieces. lay out each piece of dough and cold each corned into itself until it resembles a rough ball. Flip the dough over seam side down and roll each piece of dough into a tight ball. You can do this by using the edge of your hand to push the dough along the table, creating tension on the surface of the dough. Repeat with the remaining pieces of dough. Refer to the video above if additional explanation is needed.
  19. Place on a sheet tray lined with a piece of oiled parchment, cover, and proof at room temp for 45-60 minutes.
  20. Brush each dough ball with egg wash & load into preheated 400°F/204°C oven for 15 minutes or until deep golden brown.

Cajun Gumbo

Serves 8
Ingredients
  • 225 g all-purpose flour
  • 225 g neutral oil (vegetable, canola, avocado)
  • 400 g onion small dice
  • 250 g celery small dice
  • 250 g poblano peppers small dice
  • 200 g okra sliced ¼–½ inch thick
  • 25 g garlic minced, about 6–8 cloves
  • 900 g bone-in skin-on chicken thighs, about 2 lbs
  • 380 g andouille sausage quartered and sliced, about 13 oz
  • 1500 g chicken stock
  • 35 g Better Than Bouillon chicken base
  • 3 packets unflavored gelatin
  • 10 g paprika
  • 3 g cayenne pepper adjust to taste
  • 3 g black pepper
  • 10 g Louisiana hot sauce or your preferred Louisiana-style hot sauce
  • salt to taste
  • cooked white rice (300g long grain rice, 350g water, 5g salt)
  • sliced scallions for garnish
Instructions
  1. Season chicken thighs with salt on both sides. Let sit for 10-30 minutes.
  2. In a nonstick skillet over medium-high, sear diced andouille until browned. Remove.
  3. In the same pan, brown chicken thighs over medium heat on both sides, about 5 minutes per side. Remove and set aside.
  4. Medium dice onion, celery, and poblanos. Slice the okra into 1 inch rounds. Mince the garlic.
  5. To make the roux, in a large dutch oven over medium heat, combine 225g neutral oil and 225g all-purpose flour. Stir constantly, reducing to medium-low as color develops. Cook 45 minutes until the color becomes a deep chocolate brown (see photos and a more detailed description of the roux process above)
  6. Add onion, celery, & poblanos to the roux. Toast 5-6 minutes. At this point, the roux should begin to stick to the bottom of the pot. Add garlic and stir for another minute.
  7. While veggies toast, make a fortified stock by whisking together stock, Better than Bouillon, and gelatin. Gradually add it to the toasted roux and vegetables pot about 2c at a time, stirring constantly and allowing liquid to come to a simmer before adding more liquid.
  8. Dice the seared chicken thighs into 1" cubes, then add to the pot with the seared andouille, any resting juices from the meats and the okra. Bring back to a gentle simmer and cook for about 25 minutes. Stirring every 5 minutes or so.
  9. 15 minutes in, come back and skim off the okra scum and any roux oil that has risen to the surface.
  10. Continue to cook until the chicken is just starting to be shreddable, and the trinity veg/okra are tender but not mushy.
  11. Once the chicken is tender, add cayenne, paprika, black pepper, and hot sauce. Taste and adjust the salt if needed.
  12. Serve in bowls with a scoop of rice on top. Garnish with scallions.

Carne Asada Tacos

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 907 g skirt steak cut to 6–8 oz pieces, 2 lb
  • Kosher salt as needed
  • 75 g neutral oil 1/3 c
  • 15 g soy sauce 1 T
  • 2 chipotle chiles in adobo
  • 25 g minced garlic 4-5 cloves
  • 5 g dried oregano 1 T
  • 5 g ground coriander 2.5 t
  • 15 g smoked paprika 2 T
  • 5 g ground cumin 2 t
  • 6 g Mexican bouillon powder 1.5 t
  • 10 g Black pepper
  • 25 g chopped cilantro ½ c loose
  • 60 g fresh lime juice 1.5-2 limes worth
  • 150 g fresh orange juice 2 oranges
  • 300 g diced ripe tomato flesh 2 c
  • 150 g white onion small-diced & rinsed, 1 med-large
  • 30 g finely diced jalapeño 2 T
  • 15 g finely chopped cilantro 1 c loose
  • 30 g fresh lime juice 1 lime
  • 8 g kosher salt 1 ¼ t
  • 300 g all-purpose flour 2 ½ c
  • 6 g kosher salt 1 t
  • 50 g cold unsalted butter cubed, 3.5 T
  • 170 g cold water ¾ c
  • 12 fresh tortillas
  • 1 ripe avocado mashed and seasoned with lime and salt
Instructions
  1. Jaccard each steak 10–12 times (or poke with fork 30–40 times). Cut into 4 equal pieces. Season both sides generously with salt; rest 15 min.
  2. Combine all marinade ingredients; blend 30 s to a smooth paste.
  3. Add steaks to a ziplock bag, add marinade, coat well, expel air, and seal. Chill overnight (or minimum 2 h).
  4. To make the pico de gallo, dice tomatoes (discard pith/gel), place in strainer to remove excess tomato water. Combine tomato, onion, jalapeño, cilantro, lime juice & salt; stir. Let sit 20 min for flavors to mingle.
  5. To make the tortilla dough, pulse flour, salt & butter to gravel in a food processor. Stream in water until a shaggy dough forms. Knead 2 min to a soft, cohesive ball.
  6. Divide dough into 12 × 40 g pieces; roll into balls. Cover with damp towel 5 min. Roll each to a 7 in (18 cm) round, dusting lightly with flour.
  7. To cook tortillas, cook in griddle or nonstick pan over medium heat: first side ~90 s until bubbles form, flip and cook another 30 s. Keep finished tortillas wrapped in warmer.
  8. To grill the steaks, heat grill to high; oil grates well. Grill steak 3–4 min per side until lacquered and the internal temp reads 130–140 °F (54–60 °C). Rest 5–10 min.
  9. Slice steak across the grain, then into ½-inch cubes.
  10. To build the tacos, re-warm tortillas if needed. Spread mashed avocado, load with steak, top with drained pico. Fold and devour immediately—best carne asada tacos, period.

Cheeseburger Casserole

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 340 g large elbow macaroni
  • 450 g ground beef 90/10
  • 125 g diced onion
  • 20 g minced garlic
  • 450 g baby bella mushrooms sliced
  • 25 g tomato paste
  • 50 g all-purpose flour
  • 300 g beef stock
  • 40 g Worcestershire sauce
  • 50 g Better Than Bouillon beef base or 25g powdered beef bouillon
  • 30 g Dijon mustard
  • 225 g heavy cream
  • 75 g sour cream add off heat
  • 200 g shredded mozzarella cheese
  • 50 g grated parmesan cheese
  • fried onions crumbled, store-bought
Instructions
  1. Preheat oven to 425℉.
  2. Bring 4 quarts of water to a boil. Salt well and then cook macaroni until al dente. Strain off the noodles, coat them with olive oil, and set aside.
  3. In a Dutch oven over medium-high, cook ground beef with a pinch of salt, breaking it down finely as it cooks. When the beef is no longer pink, add onion, garlic, and another pinch of salt. Cook, scraping up any ground beef fond, until onions are soft (5 minutes).
  4. Add mushrooms and a pinch of salt and cook until softened and lightly brown (5-8 minutes). Add a bit of water to deglaze as mushrooms cook.
  5. Once mushrooms have begun to brown, stir in tomato paste and flour. Cook briefly, then add beef stock, Worcestershire, beef bouillon, Dijon, and heavy cream. Simmer for 2-3 minutes.
  6. Off heat, stir in sour cream. The sauce should be thick and creamy.
  7. Fold pasta with sauce, mozzarella, and parmesan. Adjust with a little water if needed.
  8. Transfer to a baking dish. Top with crumbled fried onions and more parmesan.
  9. Bake at 425°F/220C for 20-25 minutes until golden and bubbly.
