Fried Catfish & Hush Puppies with Spicy Mayo Tartar Sauce

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 1 kg thawed american catfish filets use fresh catfish if you can find it. I couldn't find it in my area so I used thawed. Sub tilapia if you can't find catfish 2 lbs
  • 100 g ap flour
  • 15 g kosher salt
  • 5 g black pepper
  • 10 g garlic powder
  • 10 g onion powder
  • 5 g paprika
  • 2 g cayenne
  • 100 g medium grind corn meal
  • 100 g finely ground corn meal blitz medium grind in blender/food processor
  • 500 g buttermilk or sour cream and milk
  • 30 g hot sauce
  • 65 g ap flour
  • 150 g medium-fine grind cornmeal
  • 8 g kosher salt
  • 10 g baking powder
  • 10 g sugar
  • 2 g cayenne
  • 75 g onions small diced
  • 25 g unsalted butter melted
  • 1 egg
  • 200 g buttermilk or milk and sour cream
  • 100 g mayonnaise
  • 75 g ketchup
  • 30 g hot sauce
  • 20 g yellow mustard
  • 25 g lemon juice
  • 15 g worcestershire
  • 25 g shallot
  • 1 garlic clove
  • 3 g paprika
  • 3 g black pepper
  • 3 g kosher salt
Instructions
  1. Prepare frying pot by adding 2-3qt/L (depending on size of pot) of neutral oil to a large heavy bottom pot and heat over medium high until it reaches a temp of 350°F/175°C. Be sure your pot is not filled more than halfway up the sides with oil.
  2. Cut filets on a bias so that you have pieces that are roughly the same weight. Set on a wire rack and salt both sides of fillets. Allow to cure for 20 minutes
  3. In a bowl, stir together flour, 15g salt, black pepper, garlic powder, onion powder, paprika, cayenne, and corn meal.
  4. In a separate bowl, stir together buttermilk and hot sauce.
  5. To bread the fish, drop each fillet into the buttermilk mix to coat. Drain off well then drop fillet into dry mixture and coat well. Toss back and forth in your hands to remove excess batter.
  6. Carefully lower filets away from your body into preheated 350°F/175°C oil, frying 2 at a time, for about 4-5 minutes flipping filets over halfway through. Fish is done with it's crispy and golden brown.
  7. Transfer to wire rack or towels to drain why you fry the rest.
  8. For the batter, whisk together 65g ap flour, med-fine grind corn meal, salt, baking powder, sugar, cayenne, onions, melted butter, egg, and buttermilk.
  9. Carefully drop large spoonfuls of batter (working in 2 batches) into hot oil and fry for about 2½ minutes, flipping over halfway through. Puppies are done when they're crispy and dark golden brown.
  10. Remove from oil and drain on wire rack.
  11. Spin ingredients in a food processor until smooth.

General Tso’s Chicken

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 450 g boneless skinless chicken breast 2 breasts
  • 30 g soy sauce
  • 30 g mirin
  • 2 g msg
  • 2 g baking soda
  • 30 g potato starch sub cornstarch
  • 350 g all-purpose flour
  • 10 g baking powder
  • 8 g garlic powder
  • 20 g kosher salt
  • 350 g potato starch
  • 250 g dry mix from Dry Mix above
  • 300 g water
  • 60 g soy sauce
  • 30 g sesame oil
  • 60 g mirin
  • 45 g rice vinegar
  • 30 g sambal oelek or sriracha
  • 20 g ketchup
  • 70 g sugar
  • 10 g brown sugar
  • 10 g cornstarch
  • 1 g msg
  • 40 g scallion whites and light greens, sliced. reserve dark greens for garnish
  • 5-8 chiles de árbol cracked and deseeded, optional if spice sensitive
  • 20 g ginger grated
  • 20 g garlic grated
  • neutral high smoke oil for frying peanut, canola, vegetable, about 1 ½-2 inch depth in pot
  • steamed rice to serve
Instructions
  1. Jaccard each breast 10 times per side (or poke 20-30 times per side with a sharp fork). Slice each breast into 3-4 long strips, then cut into 3/4in cubes.
  2. In a medium bowl combine soy sauce, mirin, MSG, baking soda, and potato starch. Add chicken and mix to coat. Let the chicken marinate for 20 minutes.
  3. In a large bowl whisk flour, baking powder, garlic powder, salt, and potato starch until uniform.
  4. In a separate bowl, weigh 250g of the dry mix. Add 300g water and stir until it looks like thin pancake batter/low-quality paint.
  5. Pour 200g of the batter into the remaining dry mix. Briefly whisk, then use your hand to rub and crumble until you've got rice-to-lentil sized pebbles with a powdery background. These dehydrated starchy flecks are the crisp makers.
  6. Toss about ⅓ of the marinated chicken in the batter bowl to lightly coat (be gentle to keep marinade on the meat). Lift, let excess drip, then drop into the craggy dry mix.
  7. Fluff/tumble to build a strong base layer without rubbing it off. For each piece, shower on extra craggy bits and give a soft squeeze, then shake off excess. Repeat with remaining chicken.
  8. Spread breaded chicken on a ¼ sheet tray and refrigerate 20 minutes. This hydrates the outer starch and boosts adhesion 30-50%. Do not skip this step.
  9. In a bowl whisk soy sauce, sesame oil, mirin, rice vinegar, sambal, ketchup, sugar, brown sugar, cornstarch, and MSG until smooth. Set aside.
  10. Heat neutral oil in a Dutch oven to about 325°F (1.5-2in depth; never fill pot more than 50%). Fry chicken in two batches. Gently lower in, don't touch for 1 minute, then nudge to prevent sticking. Fry 6-7 minutes until deeply golden. Drain on a rack or paper towel. Repeat with second batch.
  11. Stir-fry aromatics. Carefully move the hot oil aside (off oven). Heat a wok or large nonstick pan over high. Add a squeeze of oil, then scallions; stir-fry 60 seconds. Add cracked chiles (optional) and toast briefly. Add ginger and garlic; stir constantly for 30-40 seconds until just fragrant.
  12. Pour in the sauce around the hot pan edge. Bring to a boil and reduce 15-20% until it leaves a trail. Add fried chicken and toss for 30 seconds to simmer in the sauce. The coating will drink it in and turn shiny, sticky, and still-crisp in spots.
  13. Pile over steamed rice. Spoon on any extra sauce from the wok. Garnish with reserved scallion greens.
Full recipe →

Greek Chicken & Potatoes

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 50 g olive oil
  • 45 g garlic (5-6 cloves)
  • 5 g dried oregano
  • 2 g dried thyme
  • 5 g ground black pepper
  • zest of 1 lemon
  • 25 g fresh lemon juice
  • 900 g chicken thighs (about 4) bone in, skin on
  • 10 g salt
  • 750 g russet potatoes (2.5 large)
  • 150 g chicken stock
  • 50 g additional chicken stock for the pan sauce
  • 5 g Dijon mustard
  • pan spray or oil
  • fresh parsley for garnish chopped
  • additional lemon for serving
Instructions
  1. Trim excess skin or dangling fat off the chicken thighs. Season both sides with salt and place in the fridge, uncovered, for 30 to 60 mins
  2. Stir together olive oil, minced garlic, dried oregano, dried thyme, black pepper, the zest of 1 lemon, and the juice of 1 lemon (approximately 20–30g) in a medium bowl.
  3. Peel and cut potatoes in half, then cut into large wedges. Toss the potato wedges and the salted chicken thighs into the bowl with the marinade to coat thoroughly.
  4. Spray or lightly oil a metal baking pan. Lay the potato wedges in a single layer on the bottom of the pan, then nestle the chicken thighs on top, skin side up. Pour 150g of chicken stock into the pan around the potatoes
  5. Bake uncovered at 350°F/175°C for 75-90 minutes.
  6. Remove the pan when the chicken reaches an internal temperature of 185–190°F (85°C). Rest in the pan for 15-25 minutes.
  7. Transfer the chicken and potatoes to a serving platter and keep warm in a very low oven. Pour 50 g (1/4 c) chicken stock into the roasting pan and scrape up the browned stuff on the bottom and around the sides. Move this liquid to a small pan and whisk in dijon mustard. Reduce for 2-3 minutes over high heat until the sauce is thickened and a drawing a spatula through it leaves a trail.
  8. To serve, pour the sauce over the finished greek chicken and potatoes. Top with fresh chopped parsley and a squeeze of lemon juice.

Greek Chicken Souvlaki

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 900 g boneless skinless chicken thighs
  • 125 g olive oil
  • 15 g kosher salt
  • 30 g garlic minced, 5-6 cloves
  • 5 g dried oregano
  • 5 g black pepper ground
  • 100 g Dijon mustard
  • zest of 2 lemons
  • 30 g lemon juice about 1 lemon
  • skewers for grilling
  • 350 g all-purpose flour
  • 7 g instant yeast
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 8 g sugar
  • 15 g olive oil
  • 210 g flavorful yeasty beer warmed to 86-90°F/30-32℃
  • 160 g english cucumber grated
  • 200 g plain Greek yogurt
  • 75 g mayonnaise
  • 1 clove garlic minced
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 5 g fresh dill chopped
  • 15-20 g lemon juice approx ½ lemon
  • 120 g tomatoes diced
  • 60 g red onion finely diced
  • 20 g fresh parsley chopped
Instructions
  1. Begin by grating the cucumber on a box grater. wrap the cucumber up in a wad of paper towels and squeeze out as much of the excess moisture as you can.
  2. Combine tzatziki sauce ingredients and mix to combine. This will last in the refrigerator for 3-5 days.
  3. Combine the ingredients and mix thoroughly. this will last in the refrigerator for 1-3 days.
  4. If using wood skewers, soak for a few hours before grilling.
  5. Cut the chicken thighs into roughly 1-inch pieces.
  6. To the chicken, add olive oil, salt, garlic, oregano, black pepper, Dijon, lemon zest and juice. Mix well & refrigerate to marinate for at least 30 minutes, up to 24 hours.
  7. While chicken marinates, heat your beer to 98℉/36℃ in a small sauce pan. Then, in a food processor, combine flour, yeast, salt, sugar, and olive oil. Pulse about 5 times to mix. With the processor running, slowly stream in the warmed beer until the dough forms. It will be shaggy.
  8. Flip the dough onto a floured surface and knead for about a minute or two, until it becomes slightly elastic.
  9. Cut the dough into 4 equal pieces (about 150g each). Shape each into a ball, cover with a damp paper towel, and let them rest at room temp for 15 minutes.
  10. After 15 minutes, lightly flour a surface and flatten a dough ball with your hands slightly. Then switch to a rolling pin and roll dough into a 6-8 inch round. Repeat this with the next 3 dough balls.
  11. Once the dough is rolled out, allow the pita to rise at room temperature for 30 minutes.
  12. Preheat your grill to 550°F/285°C.
  13. Assemble the marinated chicken onto the soaked skewers. This should make roughly 6-8 skewers.
  14. Using a can of pan spray or a towel soaked in oil, lubricate your grill grates well and grill the chicken and pita alongside each other. Grill the pitas on one side until the top begins to bubble up and you can see the edge browning. Flip the pita and cook for about two minutes. Cook the pita for a total of about 2 minutes per side.
  15. To grill the chicken, place the skewers on the other side of the grill from the pita. Grill the chicken for 12-15 minutes, flipping every few minutes or so until the outside is well caramelized but not burnt and the chicken is fully cooked to at least 170℉/76℃.
  16. Remove the chicken from the skewers and cut it into smaller pieces. Just run your knife through the chicken 2 or 3 times. Place the chicken in a pita and top with tzatziki sauce, tomato-onion relish, and pepperoncini.

Grilled Cheese and Tomato Soup

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 40 g extra virgin olive oil
  • 300 g yellow onion finely chopped
  • 40 g garlic or 6-7 cloves, minced
  • 40 g tomato paste
  • 600 g chicken stock
  • 1600 g canned whole peeled tomatoes
  • 15 g coarse kosher salt
  • 35 g granulated sugar
  • 1 whole chipotle in adobo
  • 25 g Better Than Bouillon Roast Chicken Flavor
  • 115 g butter unsalted, room temperature
  • 60 g mayonnaise
  • 2 Pieces Texas Toast
  • 2 Slices American cheese
  • 1 Piece aged provolone thinly sliced
  • gruyere thinly sliced, to cover
Instructions
  1. In a 4 quart stainless steel sauce pan, heat up the olive oil over medium-high heat until shimmering add in onion and garlic, then cook for 5-7 minutes or until translucent and soft.
  2. When onions and garlic are soft and translucent, stir in tomato paste and cook until it looks rusty and starts to grip bottom of the pan (1-2 Min)
  3. Pour in chicken stock, 2 28-oz cans whole peeled tomatoes, salt, sugar, 1 chipotle and Better Than Bouillon. Be sure to scrape any bits off of the bottom of the pan.
  4. Raise heat to high and bring soup to a boil. Once boiling, reduce heat to medium low and bring simmer for 20-25 Minutes, or until tomatoes and onions are fully softened.
  5. In 2 batches puree soup until VERY smooth in a blender, most blenders wont be able to blend all soup at once. To account for this, have a container that can fit 4 quarts of liquid nearby to pour blended soup into. Mix all blended soup together and adjust seasoning with salt if needed, and adjust thickness of soup with extra stock if it got thick during cooking.
  6. Pour soup into desired bowl and garnish with black pepper, parmesan, chives and olive oil.
  7. In a mixing bowl, combine room temperature butter and mayonnaise and mix with a whisk or rubber spatula until fully combined and smooth.
  8. Spread butter mixture on one side of each piece of bread using the back side of a spoon.
  9. Place bread on a sheet tray with a rack with butter sides resting on top of each other to avoid mess.
  10. Layer cheese on bread in this order: American, provolone, gruyere, American.
  11. Get a 10-12″ non-stick pan hot on the stove over medium low heat. Bring it up to 300 F surface temperature and drop in one piece of buttered bread butter side down with the 4 layers of cheese on it. Lower the heat and use your hand or spatula to press down on the cheese for 30 seconds, this will help ensure full contact of the bread.
  12. Place second piece of bread on top and flip over when the cooked side is golden brown (3 Min) When you flip the grilled cheese, press it down slightly with the spatula for 30 seconds and then fry gently over medium low heat until golden brown and cheese is fully melted (2.5-3 Additional Mins)

Grilled Kebabs

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 680 grams 1.5lb chicken breasts
  • 75 grams 1/3 cup olive oil
  • 12 grams 2 teaspoons salt
  • 4 grams 2 teaspoon black pepper
  • 2 grams 1 teaspoon chili flakes
  • 3 grams 1 ½ teaspoon dried oregano
  • 15 grams 1 tablespoon minced garlic
  • Zest of 1 lemon
  • Juice of 1 lemon
  • 15 grams parsley ½ cup, stems removed, chopped finely
  • 3 grams fresh thyme leaves 1 tablespoon, chopped finely
  • 680 grams sirloin steaks (4 steaks, 6 ounces each) 24 ounces
  • 75 grams Worcestershire sauce 1/4 cup
  • 40 grams 2 tablespoons + 2 teaspoons soy sauce
  • 40 grams aged balsamic vinegar 2 ½ tablespoons
  • 12 grams salt 2 teaspoons
  • 5 grams black pepper 2 teaspoon
  • 30 grams Dijon mustard 2 tablespoons
  • 65 grams 1/4 cup + 1 tablespoons olive oil
  • 35 grams agave syrup or honey (or brown sugar as an alternative) 2 tablespoons
  • 2-3 large portobello mushrooms
  • 1 Eggplant
  • 1 med-large Zucchini
  • 2-3 Bell peppers depending on size
  • Olive oil
  • Salt
  • Black pepper
  • 400 g water or stock
  • 300 g rice
  • 20 g better than bouillon 1Tbsp
  • 10-12 threads saffron crushed
  • 10 g onion powder 1Tbsp
  • 15 g garlic powder 1Tbsp
  • 30 g butter 2Tbsp
  • 2 g salt 1/2tsp
Instructions
  1. Begin with 16-20 bamboo skewers. Break each in half and soak in water for at least 30 minutes. This will prevent them from burning on the grill. Alternatively, you could use flat metal skewers if you have them, but I find wood or bamboo to be the most grippy.
  2. Prepare the chicken by cutting it into large cubes. Each breast, assuming they’re a similar size to mine) should yield about 9-10 cubes.
  3. Place the chicken cubes in a freezer bag. Add olive oil, salt, black pepper, chili flakes, dried oregano, minced garlic, lemon zest, lemon juice, parsley (stems removed), and fresh thyme leaves to the bag.
  4. Seal the bag and mix the marinade well, ensuring the chicken is coated. Marinate the chicken in the fridge for 2 hours.
  5. Tenderize the sirloin steaks using a jaccard tool (or poke it many times with a thin, sharp knife or fork). Avoid jabbing the steaks excessively; 10-12 times should be sufficient. Cut each steak in half and then into chunky squares, around the same size as the chicken.
  6. In a separate bowl, combine Worcestershire sauce, soy sauce, balsamic, salt, pepper, Dijon, olive oil, and agave. Whisk well to create the marinade for the beef.
  7. Place the beef in a freezer bag and pour 2/3 of the marinade into the bag. Reserve the remaining marinade. Seal the bag and toss to coat the beef. Marinate in the fridge for 2 hours.
  8. Clean the portobello mushrooms by removing any excess thin cap around the edges. Dice the mushrooms into chunky cubes, slightly smaller than your meat cubes.
  9. Peel the eggplant and slice into large cubes, about 1″/2.5cm in diameter.
  10. Place the eggplant and mushrooms in a freezer bag. Pour the reserved marinade from the beef over the vegetables and mix to coat. Seal the bag and let them marinate for 2 hours.
  11. Preheat the grill on its highest setting.
  12. Skewer the chicken cubes onto the soaked bamboo skewers, about 5 pieces per half skewer.
  13. Skewer the beef cubes onto separate soaked bamboo skewers, also about 5 pieces per skewer.
  14. Cut zucchini into thick half moons. Cut the flesh from the bell pepper in large planks and remove the rib. Cut into large rectangles. Add both to a bowl with a long drizzle of olive oil and a pinch of salt and pepper. Mix and toss to combine. Allow to season for 10 minutes before skewering.
  15. Remove the marinated vegetables from the bag and load them onto additional soaked skewers, alternating between zucchini, bell pepper, mushroom, and eggplant. I like to stab an end of the skewer into a potato to create a base for loading the veg onto the skewers. Starting and ending with the zucchini (placed through the skin) makes a good stopper on either end of the skewer.
  16. Clean and oil your grill, then place the chicken and beef skewers on the preheated grill. I usually toss my veggies on a littler later than the meats to allow meat to rest while the veg finishes cooking.
  17. On my Weber spirit grill, I grill the meat skewers for about 10-15 minutes with the lid closed, flipping after about 5 minutes, until the chicken is cooked through and the beef is cooked to your desired level of doneness. Rotate meats to hotter or cooler parts of the grill if needed. An instant read thermometer should read 130F/55C on the steak when done. The temp on the chicken should read 150F/60C when done. The chicken will likely take longer to cook than the steak. I add the veggies to the grill last. The veggies will need to cook for around 10-12 minutes, flipping halfway through, until well browned and tender.
  18. Remove the cooked meat skewers from the grill and let them rest for a few minutes under a foil tent while the veg finishes cooking.
  19. Serve over saffron rice.
  20. Stir ingredients into a rice cooker and cook on white rice setting.

Grilled Pizza

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 400 g warm water
  • 6 g instant yeast
  • 12 g salt
  • 20 g sugar
  • 20 g olive oil
  • 690 g bread flour
  • fine semolina flour for dusting
  • fresh mozzarella cheese grated/crumbled
  • fresh basil leaves
  • olive oil
  • flaky salt
  • pizza sauce recipe below
  • 325 g crushed tomatoes
  • 175 g tomato paste 1 small can
  • 5 g salt
  • 10 g sugar
  • full fat shredded mozzarella
  • fresh mozzarella cubed
  • grilled cherry tomatoes procedure below
  • pinch salt
  • olive oil
  • fresh cracked black pepper
  • fresh basil
  • handful grated parmesan
  • pinch chile flakes
  • pinch dried oregano
  • marinated fresh garlic
  • pinch flaky salt
  • grilled broccolini procedure below
  • full fat aged mozzarella cheese
  • grated fresh mozzarella
  • salted anchovies chopped into small pieces
  • marinated fresh garlic (thinly slice garlic and cover with olive oil)
  • calabrian chiles chopped
  • grated parmesan
Instructions
  1. In a stand mixer, combine warm water, instant yeast, salt, sugar, olive oil, and bread flour. Mix on medium speed with the dough hook attachment for 8 minutes until well combined and the dough clears the bowl. That’s about a 2 on my kitchen aid.
  2. Transfer the dough onto a lightly floured surface and divide it into 4 equal-sized pieces, each weighing approximately 275g.
  3. Shape each piece of dough into a pizza ball by flattening it, folding the sides over, rolling it, and tucking the edges to create tension and strength.
  4. Place the dough balls on a lightly oiled sheet tray and cover with a lid or plastic wrap and ferment at room temperature for 30 minutes before transferring it to the refrigerator. Ferment the dough in the fridge for at least 8 hours, preferably 24-48 hours.
  5. 90 minutes before cooking the pizzas, take the dough out of the fridge to let it proof and come to room temperature.
  6. Preheat your grill for 30 minutes before making the pizzas. Set up 3 temp zones: Low on one side, medium in the middle, and high on the other side. The high temp side creates a higher overall temperature in the grill to help cook the top of the pizza.
  7. Prepare the toppings for your pizzas (see above for ingredient lists and preparations)
  8. Dust a cutting board with flour and place one dough ball on it.
  9. Flatten the dough and use a rolling pin to roll it into a 10-12 inch (25-30cm) circle.
  10. Transfer the rolled dough onto a pizza loading peel dusted with fine semolina flour.
  11. Spread sauce edge to edge, sprinkle on fresh mozzarella, 8-10 leaves of basil squirted with a little olive oil, flaky salt.
  12. To grill tomatoes: combine a pint of cherry tomatoes, a pinch of salt, cracked black pepper, and just enough olive oil to lightly coat them. Grill on high for 2-3 minutes until blistered and just softened
  13. Spread about a cup of shredded full fat mozzarella, 12-14 small cubes of the fresh mozzarella, 15-20 of the grilled tomatoes, about 12 fresh basil leaves, grated parm, chile flakes, dried oregano, a sprinkle of marinated garlic (making sure to drizzle on the basil leaves), and flaky salt. Finish with fresh cracked black pepper after grilling.
  14. To grill Broccolini: Toss in a bowl with a pinch of salt,pepper, and olive oil. Grill on high for 3-4 minutes until it's 65-75% cooked. Transfer to a bowl and cover to allow to steam for about 5 minutes. Cut into bite sized pieces
  15. For the pizza: spread about 1 cup of shredded mozzarella, 1 cup of grated fresh mozzarella, about ½ cup of the grilled chopped broccolini, chopped anchovies, sliced marinated garlic and oil, and chopped calabrian chiles. Top with a sprinkle of grated parmesan after grilling
  16. Shimmy pizza on the peel to be sure it’s not stuck. If it is, add another sprinkle of semolina flour under any stuck edges.
  17. At the grill, spray the low heat side with pan spray or dip a towel into olive oil and use tongs to oil the grates.
  18. Carefully slide the pizza off the peel and onto the low side of the preheated grill Cook with the lid closed for 2 minutes.
  19. Check the bottom of the pizza to ensure it’s not stuck or burning. Rotate the pizza 90 degrees and cook for an additional 3-4 minutes, lid closed.
  20. After 6-7 minutes of grilling, remove the pizza from the grill and let it rest for a moment.

Herby Pesto Chicken Salad

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 450 g chicken breast
  • 50 g fresh basil
  • 30 g parsley stems removed
  • 20 g cilantro stems okay
  • 35 g lemon juice 1 lemon
  • 200 g mayonnaise
  • 400 g cooked chicken breast
  • 30 g parmesan cheese grated
  • 2-3 g chili flakes
  • 4 g kosher salt
  • 50 g shallots minced
  • 2 garlic cloves grated or minced
  • 50 g toasted pine nuts
  • 1 large egg
  • 20 g water
  • 20 g white distilled vinegar
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 325 g neutral oil avocado, canola, grapeseed, etc
Instructions
  1. To cook the chicken, salt well on both sides then allow to sit and dry brine for around 15-20 minutes.
  2. Boil a pot of water, then reduce the heat to low until water temp reaches 180°F/82°C (to test water temp, I just use my meat thermometer)
  3. Stir a very large handful of salt into the water then gently drop in the chicken breasts. This should drop the water temp to about 165°F/75°C. Poach for 20 minutes at a temp of 165°F/75°C.
  4. Note: if you're poaching more than 2 breasts, the temperature may drop below 165°F. If this happens, increase heat and monitor the water temp to keep it as close to 165°F/75°C as possible while poaching.
  5. When cooked, the internal temp of the chicken should read 150-155°F/65-68°C.
  6. In a food processor, combine basil, parsley, (stem removed), cilantro, lemon juice, and mayo. Process until the herbs are broken down.
  7. Transfer the herby mayo to a stand mixer bowl and add most of the chicken breast , about ¾ (setting aside about ¼ of the chicken). Also add grated parmesan, chili flakes, salt, minced shallots, garlic cloves, and toasted pine nuts.
  8. Mix with the paddle attachment until well combined and the chicken is becoming shredded.
  9. Add the remaining chicken breast and mix on low for 20 or 30 seconds, until just combined
  10. Add all ingredients except oil into a high sided container. Blend with an immersion blender then slowly stream in oil. When you get to the last of the oil, it may start pooling on top of the mayo. If that happens, move the immersion blender to the top of the mayo and work it up and down a bit to incorporate the oil into the mixture.
Full recipe →

Homemade Pastrami on Rye Sandwich

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 5 lb corned beef brisket flat (I used 2 pieces for my recipe, but 1 larger piece will also work)
  • 30 g whole black peppercorns
  • 30 g whole coriander seeds
  • 30 g granulated sugar
  • 5 g garlic powder
  • 2 g chili flakes
  • Jewish caraway rye bread
  • spicy brown mustard
Instructions
  1. To prep the beef, rinse the corned beef under cold water to remove excess brine. Pat it aggressively with paper towels until completely dry.
  2. Toast peppercorns and coriander in a dry pan over medium heat for 3–4 minutes until fragrant and lightly smoking. Cool slightly, then grind to a medium-coarse texture (not a powder).
  3. Mix the toasted spices with the sugar, garlic powder, and chili flakes.
  4. Press the spice blend firmly into all sides of the meat until fully coated.
  5. Place meat on a wire rack over a tray. Refrigerate uncovered for 8–24 hours to allow the surface to dry (crucial for smoke adhesion).
  6. Set up your grill for indirect heat with a smoker box directly on top of the active burner over high heat. Once the smoke starts rolling, reduce heat to med-high, add the pastrami to grill grates on the inactive side of the grill and smoke it for 90 minutes, replacing wood chips every 30 minutes. Keep the lid closed as much as possible and aim to keep grill temperature between 225-250°F (110°C). Remove pastrami when the bark is deep mahogany and dry to the touch.
  7. Wrap the meat in a double layer of foil and place it on a rack in a roasting pan. Roast at 300°F (150°C) until the internal temperature hits 200–205°F. At that point, continue cooking for another 90–120 minutes until tender and pull-apart, but not fall-apart.
  8. Let the meat rest in the foil for at least 45 minutes. Slice the pastrami 1/4-inch thick against the grain and serve on a nice jewish rye style bread with plenty of spicy brown mustard on both sides. You want at least a 4 to 1 meat to bread ratio.

Homemade Roast Beef

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 5 sirloin tip beef roast sub rump roast (lb/2.5kg )
  • 50 g olive oil 3.7T
  • 50 g dijon mustard 3.3T
  • 5 g cracked black pepper 2t
  • 20 g salt additional salt (for directly seasoning beef) 3t
  • 1 c water to pour under roast
  • 300 g eggs large eggs 6
  • 9 g salt 1.5t
  • 375 g whole milk 1.5c
  • 240 g all-purpose flour 2c
  • 50 g butter 3.5T
  • 25 g all-purpose flour 3T
  • 450 g store-bought beef stock 2c
  • 10 g Worcestershire sauce 2t
  • 30 g Better than bouillon beef base 2T
  • Steamed Brussels sprouts and smashed potatoes for serving
Instructions
  1. Tie the sirloin tip roast using butcher’s twine every 2 inches, securing it tightly. If this step is too advanced, ask your butcher to tie it for you.
  2. In a bowl, mix olive oil, dijon, pepper, and salt.
  3. Slather the mustard mixture over the roast
  4. Add an extra 3-finger pinch of salt to each side for additional seasoning.
  5. Transfer the roast to the fridge, uncovered, for at least 1 hour, preferably overnight.
  6. Preheat oven to 500°F (260°C).
  7. Remove the roast from the fridge. Drizzle with olive oil and spread evenly.
  8. Place the roast in the oven to “sear” for 15-20 minutes (10-12 minutes with convection).
  9. Lower the oven temp to 275°F (135°C) and open the oven door briefly to cool.
  10. Pour 1 cup of water into the roasting pan to prevent drippings from burning.
  11. Insert a probe thermometer into the thickest part of the roast.
  12. Roast for 90-120 minutes until the internal temperature reaches 122°F (50°C) for medium-rare.
  13. While the roast cooks, whisk together eggs, salt, and milk in a bowl.
  14. Sprinkle in flour and whisk until smooth and lump-free.
  15. Transfer batter to a container and refrigerate until 30 minutes before baking.
  16. In a saucepan over medium heat, melt butter, then add flour and whisk for 1 minute.
  17. Slowly add beef stock, whisking continuously.
  18. Stir in Worcestershire sauce and beef base.
  19. Simmer for 10 minutes, stirring occasionally, then cover and set aside.
  20. Resting the Roast & Prepping the Yorkshire Pudding Pan
  21. Remove the roast from the oven and tent loosely with foil. Rest for 30 minutes.
  22. Place a 12-count muffin pan in the oven and preheat to 500°F (260°C).
  23. Once hot, spray liberally with pan spray (or use beef fat).
  24. Pour half of the batter into the muffin pan, coming halfway up the sides of each (this recipe makes 24 puddings)
  25. Immediately reduce oven temp to 425°F (220°C).
  26. Bake for 20-22 minutes until deep golden brown.
  27. While still in the oven, pierce each pudding with a sharp knife to release steam, then let sit in the oven for 1-2 more minutes with the door open (i didn’t show this in the video, but it helps to prevent a large temperature gradient)
  28. Lift the rested roast and pour a small amount of water into the roasting pan to loosen the drippings.
  29. Strain the drippings and stir into the warm gravy. To serve, slice the roast into thin slices. Serve with Yorkshire puddings, beef gravy, Brussels sprouts, and smashed potatoes.

How I Cook a Week’s Worth of Healthy Food in 1 Hour

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2 lbs Boneless skinless chicken breast 1kg
  • 2 lbs of Skin on Norwegian salmon 1kg
  • 2 heads of broccoli
  • 1 lb brussel sprouts .5kg
  • 1.5 lb thick cut carrots 1kg
  • 2 large bunches of kale or chard leaves cut into pieces with stem end thinly sliced
  • 600 g rice I like medium grain calrose
  • 1.5 lb potatoes 1kg, cut into large bite sized pieces, i'm using red baby potatoes
  • 2 med-lrg sweet potatoes peeled and cut into bite size cubes
  • Salt
  • Pepper
  • Olive oil
  • Arugula 2-3 clamshells
  • Roasted red bell pepper
  • Pepperoncinis
  • Marinated olives
  • Feta cheese
  • Pickled red onions RECIPE: https://youtu.be/QLX6wCDWrIc?t=443
  • Grainy mustard
  • Balsamic vinegar
  • Shredded cabbage i buy the bagged coleslaw mix or you can slice your own
  • Sushi ginger
  • 3 avocados
  • Roasted seaweed or furikake
  • Kimchi
  • Sesame seeds
  • Corn tortillas or tortillas of choice
  • Pickled jalapenos
  • Lime
  • Garlic powder or garlic
  • Onion powder
  • Chili flakes/red pepper flake
  • Honey
Instructions
  1. Boil a very large pot of water. Add a lid to the pot to speed the time it takes to boil.
  2. Preheat oven to 425F/220C.
  3. Lay out chicken onto a parchment lined sheet tray and season with salt and pepper.
  4. Lay the salmon onto a parchment lined sheet tray skin side down, and season with salt and pepper
  5. Bake both the chicken and salmon at 425F/220C
  6. At around the 15 minute mark, check the Salmon. It’s done cooking when the internal temp reaches 130-140F/55-60C.
  7. The chicken should be done after cooking for between 20-25 minutes depending on the size of the breasts. They’re done when the internal temperature reaches 150-160F/65-70C
  8. Eat immediately or transfer salmon and chicken to wide, flat storage containers in the fridge (lid off) to cool.
  9. Salt the now-boiling water well and add in chopped broccoli. Cook for about 90 seconds or until the broccoli has softened, but still has a little bite to it. Transfer the broccoli to a wire rack to drain. Then into paper towel lined containers for storage in the fridge.
  10. Add the carrots into the boiling water and cook for 3-4 minutes until just tender, but still a little snappy. Transfer to a wire rack to drain.
  11. Add the kale to the boiling water and cook for about 2 minutes. Transfer to wire rack to drain then load into paper towel lined storage containers.
  12. Add the brussel sprouts to the boiling water and cook for 3-4 minutes. Transfer to wire rack to drain then load into paper towel lined storage containers.
  13. Increase oven temperature to 475F/245C.
  14. To cook the rice, combine 800g water with 600g rice and a very large pinch of salt. Stir and set to cook on the white rice setting. Store in a container or two in the fridge until ready to use.
  15. Slice potatoes in half. Replace the parchment paper from one of the protein sheet trays with a fresh sheet and add on sliced potatoes. Add a generous squeeze of olive oil and a large pinch of salt. Toss on the sheet tray to coat and arrange potatoes cut side down.
  16. Peel and cut sweet potatoes into bite size cubes. Replace parchment paper from the second protein sheet tray with a fresh sheet and add diced sweet potatoes. Drizzle with olive oil and salt and toss to evenly coat.
  17. Add both trays of potatoes into the oven to cook at 475F/245C. Cook for about 25 minutes until roasted, tender, and charred around the edges. Transfer potatoes into paper towel lined containers and store in the fridge.
  18. If you’d like to make your own sauces, i’ve linked to recipes for all of them (except chili crisp) above

  21. Pepperoncinis

Hungarian Goulash

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 3 lb chuck roast 1–2 in cubes, 1.5 kg
  • salt & pepper
  • 40 g olive oil 3 Tbsp
  • 600 g yellow onion small dice, 4 lg
  • 20 g garlic minced, 6 cloves
  • 30 g tomato paste 2 Tbsp
  • 50 g sweet paprika ¼ c
  • 2 packs gelatin 14 g
  • 1000 g stock chicken or beef, 4 c
  • 3 bay leaves
  • 5 g BTB beef base 1 heaping tsp
  • 150 g red bell peppers lg dice, 2
  • 300 g carrots chunky dice, 3 med
  • 450 g potatoes 1 in dice, 3 med
  • sour cream & chives for garnish
Instructions
  1. Toss meat in oil, salt and pepper. Lay out on a sheet tray with a wire rack and broil on high until meat is fully browned.
  2. While meat is browning, in a dutch oven, heat about 3 tbsp of oil and sweat onions on medium low until they are light brown, about 15 minutes. Add garlic and cook for another minute.
  3. When the meat is out of the oven, set aside and preheat oven to 300 F.
  4. Stir in flour and tomato paste and cook until it begins to brown. Turn off the heat and add in paprika and stir for 30 seconds.
  5. Pour in chicken stock, bay leaves, gelatin and Better Than Bouillon and give it a good stir. Bring up to a simmer and cover with a lid. Put the dutch oven in the oven and cook for 90 minutes. When the 90 minutes is up, remove from the oven and pour in carrots, bell peppers and potatoes. Place back in the oven and cook for another hour.
  6. Pull from the oven and allow to rest for at least 20 minutes but preferably overnight in the refrigerator.
  7. The next day, reheat the goulash and garnish with sour cream and chives.

Indian Curry

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 8-10 chicken drumsticks skin removed
  • 35 g plain greek yogurt
  • 20 g kosher salt
  • 2 g coriander ground
  • 2 g cumin ground
  • 2 g garam masala
  • 10 g Kashmiri chili sub paprika + pinch cayenne
  • 325 g yellow onion ¼" sliced
  • 275 g tomato roughly chopped
  • 30 g serrano chiles sub poblano for less spice
  • 30 g ginger roughly chopped
  • 30 g garlic roughly chopped
  • 100 g ghee
  • 5 g cumin ground
  • 3 g turmeric ground
  • 10 g garam masala
  • 3 g coriander ground
  • 10 g Kashmiri chili sub paprika + cayenne
  • 500-600 g water
Instructions
  1. Combine the chicken legs with the marinade ingredients and mix thoroughly. Place marinating chicken in the refrigerator until ready to cook.
  2. In Dutch oven over medium-high heat, fry the onions in ghee for 10 minutes until they are well browned.
  3. Stir in chopped tomato, chiles, ginger and garlic. Fry for 5-10 minutes until veggies are broken down and soft. Add in a splash of water every once in a while if the onions begin to stick or get too dark.
  4. Remove the contents from the dutch oven and place them in a blender. Blend it into a rough puree.
  5. Return the dutch oven to stove and place back on medium heat. Throw a little more ghee into the pan dan fry the puree, stirring often, for 10-15 minutes until the oil begins to separate.
  6. Add in cumin, turmeric, garam masala, coriander, and chili powder. Fry for 2-3 minutes until the paste begins to take on a much darker color.
  7. Increase the heat on the to high and add the chicken legs into the dutch oven. Stir and cook the chicken for about 5 minutes. Pour in the water and bring it to a simmer.
  8. When the pot is up to a simmer, lower the heat to low and cover with a lid. Cook for 20 minutes, stirring occasionally and basting the chicken with the sauce every time you do stir it.
  9. Remove the lid from the pot and reduce for 15 minutes until oil the separates again.
  10. Turn the heat off and return the lid to the pot. Let rest for 15 minutes.
  11. Serve with steamed basmati rice.

Irish Beef & Stout Stew

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 3½ lb beef chuck 1–2 inch cubes
  • 15 g salt
  • 40 g olive oil
  • 175 g cremini mushrooms quartered
  • 250 g carrots large dice
  • 300 g gold potatoes halved
  • 120 g pearl onions peeled
  • 75 g unsalted butter
  • 75 g AP flour
  • 600 g stout beer I'm using Guinness
  • 750 g beef stock
  • 21 g powdered gelatin 21 g = 3 packets
  • 20 g BTB beef base
  • 20 g tomato paste
  • 20 g Worcestershire sauce
  • 2–3 bay leaves
  • 2–3 sprigs thyme
  • 90 g frozen peas
  • black pepper
Instructions
  1. Preheat the oven to 500F/260C.
  2. Remove the band of fat in the center of the pieces of chuck. Cut the rest of the meat into cubes. You should be left with about 3.5lb/1.5kg of meat. Add meat cubes to a bowl and drizzle with a generous amount of olive oil and 2 large pinches of salt. Toss to coat and spread beef onto a half sheet tray and load into the oven to sear for about 20-25mins until well browned.
  3. Preheat a large heavy-bottomed pot over medium high. Add a generous glug of olive oil followed by mushrooms, carrots, onions, and pinch of salt. Sweat the veg to par-cook for 3-5mins until beginning to soften and caramelize. Transfer veg into a bowl and set aside.
  4. Return the pan to the stove over medium heat and add 75g butter. When butter is melted, add 75g flour. Stir to cook, scraping up brown bits from the bottom of the pot. Fry for 60-90 seconds until smooth and taking on color.
  5. Add beef stock and beer. Bring to a simmer, stirring and scraping frequently. When simmering, add in beef. Add a splash of water to the beef cooking tray, scraping up beef fond. Add to pot with beef. Stir to combine. Load pot, uncovered, into 300F/150C oven and cook for 2 hours, stirring halfway through.
  6. Add potatoes/veggies into stew pot and gently stir. Add in bay and thyme. Return to the oven to cook for 1 more hour.
  7. At this point, check the beef and potato for done-ness. Beef should be tender and shreddable, but not shredded. Potatoes should be fork tender, but not mushy.
  8. Ladle off excess fat from top of stew (or allow to cool slightly, refrigerate, then scrape off fat from top once cooled). Stir peas into hot stew.

Japanese Curry

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 450 g chicken thighs diced 1 ½" cubes
  • 6 g kosher salt
  • 225 g Yukon gold potatoes large diced
  • 175 g carrots large diced
  • 125 g shiitake mushrooms quartered
  • 150 g yellow onion small diced
  • 15 g grated ginger
  • 15 g grated garlic
  • 20 g Japanese curry powder sub Indian curry powder
  • 10 g garam masala
  • 115 g butter 1 stick
  • 60 g flour
  • 1000 g chicken stock
  • 25 g instant dashi powder or bouillon
  • 50 g soy sauce
  • 15 g Worcestershire sauce
  • 45 g honey
  • high smoke point oil
Instructions
  1. In a bowl, salt the chicken thighs and let them sit for at least 10 minutes.
  2. Heat your Dutch oven over medium-high heat. Add in 2 tbsp of oil followed by the chicken thighs. Sear the chicken thighs for 5 minutes, until some color has formed.
  3. Add in the onions and mushrooms and cook until the onions are soft, translucent and just starting to caramelize, roughly 5 minutes. Deglaze with a little bit of water if needed to scrape up the brown bits.
  4. Stir in garlic and ginger and cook for 1-2 minutes.
  5. Stir in the curry powder and garam masala. Allow the spices to toast for 1 minute and then add in the butter.
  6. Allow the butter to melt fully and then stir in the flour. Cook for one minute and then add in the stock and dashi powder. Bring the pot to a simmer.
  7. Add in the potatoes, carrots, soy, Worcestershire and honey.
  8. Give everything a nice stir and the cover and simmer on medium-low heat for 15-20 minutes stirring occasionally. When the vegetables are tender, the soup is ready. Adjust seasoning or consistency if needed with salt and stock. Serve with Japanese rice.

Jerk Chicken

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 1800 g skin-on chicken leg quarters 4 large
  • 30 g kosher salt
  • 15 g sugar
  • 25 g fresh thyme leaves
  • 50 g garlic 10 cloves
  • 50 g scallions
  • 8-10 g black pepper
  • 50 g sliced ginger
  • 75 g habanero peppers 1-3 peppers, deseeded, adjust to taste
  • 100 g olive oil
  • 75 g soy sauce
  • 50 g brown sugar
  • 20 g allspice ground
  • 5 g cinnamon ground
  • 5 g nutmeg ground
  • 60 g fresh lime juice
  • remaining jerk marinade
  • 50 g water
Instructions
  1. Remove backbone from leg quarters using a paring knife. Split into drumstick and thigh along the joint to create 8 pieces.
  2. Mix salt and sugar. Season both sides of chicken generously. Chill uncovered in fridge for 15–20 minutes.
  3. In a food processor, combine thyme, garlic, scallions, ginger, habanero, olive oil, soy sauce, brown sugar, allspice, cinnamon, nutmeg, and lime juice. Blend until mostly smooth but still slightly textured.
  4. Toss the brined chicken with half the marinade. Arrange skin-side up on a sheet tray. Brush any exposed areas with extra marinade. Chill uncovered for at least 4 hours, preferably 24.
  5. Set up for 2-zone cooking. Add wood chips (preferably pimento or hickory) to a smoker box or foil pouch on the hot side. Preheat grill to about 300°F with good smoke flow.
  6. Oil the grates well. Place chicken skin-side down on the cooler side. Grill for 10 minutes. Flip, brush grilled side with more marinade, and cook another 10 minutes. From this point on, flip the chicken every 5-10 minutes until deep browning has occurred and the internal temperature hits 185℉. Be sure to brush more marinade on after each flip. When the process is done, the skin should be rendered and slightly crisp.
  7. Make sauce (optional): Simmer remaining marinade with water over med heat for 5 minutes. Blend until smooth.
  8. Let the chicken rest for 10 minutes. Serve hot, optionally with a drizzle of jerk sauce and a squeeze of lime.

Korean Short Ribs (Galbi)

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 3.5 lb short ribs flanken cut
  • 100 g soy sauce
  • 10 g salt
  • 50 g brown sugar
  • 25 g mirin
  • 25 g sesame oil
  • 150 g asian pear (1 whole) peeled and chopped
  • 50 g kiwi (1 whole) peeled and chopped
  • 100 g onion chopped
  • 30 g garlic about 6-8 cloves
  • 10 g ginger chopped
  • 3 g ground black pepper
Instructions
  1. Rinse each rib under cold water to wash off any bone dust from the saw. Lay them out on paper towels and dry them very well.
  2. Combine soy sauce, salt, brown sugar, mirin, sesame oil, pear, kiwi, onion, garlic, ginger, and black pepper in a blender. Blend until completely smooth.
  3. Lay all the ribs in 2 layers in a 9×13 baking dish. Pour the blended marinade over them and brush every piece so it's heavily coated on both sides. Cover with a lid (plastic wrap works too) and refrigerate overnight (12-18 hours is ideal).
  4. Preheat Grill on high to 450-500°F (260°C)
  5. Spray or wipe the grates liberally with oil, then lay each rib on the grill, letting the excess marinade drip off first. Close the lid and sear 3-4 minutes. Reserve the excess marinade.
  6. Flip each rib, brush the top sides with marinade, then close the lid again. Reduce grill heat to medium and cook for 1-2 minutes. Open the lid, flip the ribs, brush with more marinade, close the lid and reduce heat to medium. Open, flip, brush, and close a second maneuver 2 more times. Don't brush with marinade on the last flip (you'll likely be out of marinade at this point anyway). The total cook time for ribs should be 10-12 minutes: 5-6 minutes over medium high heat, 5-6 minutes over medium.
  7. Transfer the ribs to a plate and rest for 5 minutes. They're thin, so they don't need long.
  8. To serve cut into bite sized pieces with scissors between the bones

Lobster Rolls

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 600 g lobster meat two 2-2.5lb whole live lobsters
  • 120 g mayonnaise
  • 20 g chives minced
  • 10 g kosher salt
  • 4 g black pepper ground
  • 1 lemon zested
  • 15 g lemon juice
  • 200 g warm water 86°F/30°C
  • 70 g sugar
  • 1 large egg
  • 75 g melted butter + extra for brushing not hot
  • 8 g instant yeast
  • 15 g dough conditioner
  • 150 g riced baked potato
  • 625 g all purpose flour
  • 12 g kosher salt
  • egg wash 1 egg + splash of water
  • melted butter to brush on top
  • 1 large egg
  • 20 g water
  • 25 g white vinegar
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 325 g neutral oil i like light olive oil
Instructions
  1. To cook the lobster, freeze the lobsters for 15 minutes.
  2. Add a generous amount of salt (about 3 handfuls) and 2 halved and juiced lemons to a large pot of water and bring temp to 185°F/85°C.
  3. Add "chilled" lobsters to the pot and poach for 8-9 minutes for large lobsters or closer to 6 minutes if using smaller lobsters. When done, the internal temperature of the tail meat should read 150°F/65°C. Remove from the water and allow to cool. If using lobster tails, cool in an ice bath.
  4. Remove meat from shells. Cut meat into large rustic chunks and place in bowl. Add mayo, chives, salt, pepper. Lemon zest & juice. Gently stir to combine.
  5. Add ingredients to mixing bowl and mix with dough hook on medium speed for 8-10 minutes. The dough will look loose at around 4-5 minutes before it comes together into a nice ball that's no longer extremely sticky.
  6. Use a soaking wet hand and a grab and round in a 10 & 2 motion to tuck the dough into a taught ball. Cover and ferment at room temperature for 30 minutes.
  7. After 30 minutes, the dough should have gassed up quite a bit. Aggressively de-gas the dough so that you can perform a strength building fold. To perform a strrength building fold, pull a side of the bread up into the air until you meet resistance and then fold it into the center of the dough. Repeat this with each edge of the dough until it looks like it's starting to look round (about 4-5 times) and flip it over to where the seem side is on the bottom. After the strength building fold and another 10-2 rounding, cover the dough again and allow to ferment at room temp for another 30 minutes.
  8. After the second 30 minute fermentation, the dough will have risen significantly. Aggressively de-gas it. Divide into ten, 150g pieces. Pre-shape each into loose round balls by dragging the ball along the table with the side of your hand until surface tension is created on the surface of the dough. do this until the dough forms a ball shape. Cover with a towel and rest for 10min.
  9. After resting, shape the buns. press the dough flat with your hands and then roll it into itself to where it looks like a cigar. Lightly roll from the center out with your hands to lengthen and create some more surface tension on the roll.
  10. Line each up on a parchment lined half sheet tray, cover, and proof at room temp for 60-90 minutes. Buns should have doubled in size and spring back when poked.
  11. Brush each with egg wash (1 egg with splash of water).
  12. Load into a preheated 400°F/205°C oven, spritz oven with 12-15 squirts of water and bake for 15-17 minutes.
  13. Brush with melted butter.
  14. Perfect Burger Bun Video
  15. Place ingredients except oil into a high sided container and spin with an immersion blender to combine. Slowly stream in oil while blending. Use a regular food processor if you don’t have an immersion blender.
  16. Spread butter on sides of roll and toast until golden brown. Cut bun top down, line with lettuce, and load with generous portion of lobster salad. Garnish with black pepper and minced chives.

Meatball Sub

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 380 g or 1 5/8c water 86F/30C
  • 12 g or 3tsp instant yeast
  • 20 g or 1 3/4Tbsp granulated sugar
  • 525 g or 4 1/4c bread flour
  • 12 g or 2tsp salt
  • Untoasted white sesame seeds
  • 200 g 3/4c + 1Tbsp whole milk
  • 50 g or 1/4c sour cream
  • 1 egg
  • 150 g white bread about 6 slices, cut into cubes
  • 1 lb 80/20 ground beef .5kg
  • 1 lb 80/20 ground pork .5kg
  • 125 g or 1 1/4c grated parm
  • 15 g or 1/4c fresh parsley chopped
  • 15 g or 4-5 cloves garlic minced
  • Pinch chile flakes
  • 3 g or 1.5tsp ground black pepper
  • 18 g or 1Tbsp salt
  • 15 g lemon juice juice ½ lemon
  • Olive oil
  • White onion small diced
  • 15 g garlic 4-5 cloves
  • Salt
  • Chile flakes
  • 28 oz can tomato puree
  • 28 oz can crushed tomatoes
  • sugar
  • Full fat mozzarella sliced (2 per sandwich)
  • Giardinara chopped finely (recipe below)
  • 150 g or about 1 1/3c each of Cauli Celery, Green Bell Peppers, Carrots
  • 75 g or 1/2 c serrano or another hot pepper of choice
  • 400 g or 1 3/4c white distilled vinegar
  • 200 g or 3/4c water
  • 24 g or 1 1/2 tbsp salt
  • 300 g or 1 1/3c light olive oil
  • 150 g or 3/4c ex virgin olive oil
Instructions
  1. Add water, yeast, sugar, flour, & salt to mixer with dough hook. Mix on high for 6min. When ready dough should be formed into a ball that clears the sides of mixer and should pass the tug test (see video @0:42).
  2. Place dough into a bowl and round as shown in video @0:49. Cover and let sit at room temp for 45min.
  3. Perform a strength building fold then round and tuck into a ball as shown @1:09. Cover and allow to sit at room temp again for another 45min.
  4. Flip dough onto floured cutting board and degass well. Divide dough into 6 pieces (about 150g each). Preshape each piece as shown @4:20.
  5. Cover dough balls with damp towel and rest for 15min.
  6. Prepare 2 trays/plates: 1 with white sesame seeds, the other with a wet towel.
  7. Spray/oil baguette pan (if you don’t have a baguette pan, proof in couche and bake on sheet tray)
  8. Shape rolls as shown @5:12. Place shaped roll onto wet towel, seam side up, then into sesame seeds. Place in baguette proof pan, seed side up. Cover with a towel and proof for 45-60min.
  9. Score rolls and mist well with water. Load pan into 400F/205C oven to bake for 18-20min, spritzing rolls and oven again before closing door.
  10. Whisk milk, sour cream, and egg. Add bread. Whisk/stir until pasty and broken down. Add the remaining ingredients and mix well.
  11. Divide into fifteen 100-105g balls. Work and roll balls between your hands.
  12. Place each ball on parchment lined sheet tray.
  13. Bake at 475F/245C for 25min, flipping after 15min.
  14. SAUCE
  15. ▪Olive oil
  16. ▪White onion, small diced
  17. ▪15g (4-5 cloves) garlic
  18. ▪Salt
  19. ▪Chile flakes
  20. ▪28oz can tomato puree
  21. ▪28oz can crushed tomatoes
  22. ▪sugar
  23. Preheat large, heavy bottomed pot over medium. Add in a long glug of olive oil. Add onion, garlic, and pinch of salt. Sweat, stirring every couple of minutes until onions have softened.
  24. Add pinch of chile flakes and allow to bloom for about a minute. Stir in tomatoes and bring to a simmer.
  25. Reduce heat to med low to reduce for about 25min.
  26. Gently stir cooked meatballs into pot of sauce. Taste for seasoning. Stir in pinch of salt and pinch of sugar.
  27. Roast in 350F/175C oven to reduce for 20-25min.
  28. To prep sub rolls, halve then remove about half of bready filling from the top piece.
  29. Put 2 pieces of mozz on bottom piece and place under broiler on high until cheese is melted and bread is a little toasty (30-50sec).
  30. Spoon giard on top of cheese followed by 3 saucy meatballs.
  31. Top with toasty top bun.
  32. Chop all veg into small dice and mix to combine.
  33. Combine vin, water, salt in a sauce pan, bring to a boil, add veg, stir, turn off heat and allow to cook as it comes to room temp.
  34. Transfer to container and refrigerate 2 days.
  35. After 2 days, strain liquid off veg. To the strained veg mix, add oils & store in fridge until ready to use.

Meatloaf

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • Olive oil
  • 150 g or 1 large onion small diced
  • Salt
  • 75 g or 1 1/2c panko breadcrumbs
  • 85 g or 1/3c buttermilk
  • 450 g or 1lb 90/10 ground beef
  • 450 g or 1lb 90/10 ground pork
  • 2 eggs + 1 yolk
  • 3 cloves garlic finely minced
  • 15 g or 1Tbsp worcestershire
  • 15 g or 1Tbsp dijon mustard
  • 2 g or 1 1/4tsp poultry seasoning
  • 3 g or 1 1/3tsp black pepper
  • 12 g or 2tsp salt
  • 60 g or 1/3c brown sugar
  • 40 g 2 2/3Tbsp cider vinegar
  • 10 g or about 1/3Tbsp worcestershire
  • 20 g 1 1/4Tbsp sriracha
  • 300 g or 1 1/4c ketchup
  • 50 g or 3 1/2Tbsp butter
  • 100 g or 2c onion medium diced
  • salt
  • 25 g or 3 1/4Tbsp AP flour
  • 450 g or 1 ¾-2c beef stock
  • 10 g or 1Tbsp beef flavored better than bouillon
  • 5 g or 1tsp soy sauce
Instructions
  1. Preheat medium saute pan over medium, add a long squeeze of olive oil, followed by onions and a pinch of salt. Stir and cook until softened, about 5min. Move onions to a bowl and set aside.
  2. In a food processor, add breadcrumbs. Spin to break down until powdery. Add buttermilk then spin for 10 seconds or until mixture is hydrated. Add ground meats and process to combine and break down meat, about 15sec.
  3. Transfer meat mixture to onion bowl and add eggs, garlic, worcestershire, mustard, poultry seasoning, pepper, and salt. Mix and fold until just combined. Shape into a loaf. I use a loaf pan to shape it, then transfer out onto a foil lined baking tray. Refrigerate for about 10-15min while you make the glaze (recipe below).
  4. Sear meat loaf under medium-low broiler for 5-10min until beginning to take on color on top. Add a layer of glaze and return to cook under broiler for another 3min.
  5. When glaze gets sticky and begins to take on color, remove loaf from oven and glaze again. Return to oven under broiler and repeat, rotating as necessary.
  6. Glaze a third/final time then load into a 325F/160C to bake for 25-35min or until internal temp reaches 155F/70C
  7. Add ingredients to small sauce pot and stir.
  8. Once meatloaf has been glazed three times and is baking (see recipe above), place over medium low heat to reduce by half, about 3-4min
  9. Stir often to prevent burning.
  10. Preheat saute pan over medium heat. Add butter, onion, and a pinch of salt. Sweat for about 5min to soften.
  11. Add flour and whisk to combine and cook for 3-5min.
  12. Whisk in 100g or about 1/3 of the beef stock. When stirred in and thickening, whisk in remaining (350g) stock and bring to a simmer.
  13. Once simmering, add bouillon paste and soy sauce. Simmer for about 5min or until thickened and onions are fully softened.

Miso Glazed Salmon with Corn & Asparagus Succotash

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 6 oz portions of salmon
  • 25 grams soy sauce 1T
  • 25 grams sesame oil 2T
  • 10 grams rice vinegar 1 2/3t
  • 50 grams honey 2 1/2T
  • 100 grams white miso 1/3c
  • 400 g 4 ears corn, kernels removed
  • 1 pound asparagus, cut into coins 450g
  • 125 g scallions, chopped into coins 2 bunches
  • 150 g broccolini, chopped 1 bunch
  • Olive oil
  • Salt
  • Black pepper
Instructions
  1. Preheat the oven and sheet tray to 450°F (232°C).
  2. In a bowl, combine soy sauce, sesame oil, rice vinegar, honey, and white miso. Blend until smooth.
  3. Brush salmon with miso glaze. Reserve half of the glaze for later or another use.
  4. In a large mixing bowl, combine corn, asparagus, scallions, broccolini, olive oil, salt, and black pepper. Toss to combine.
  5. Spread the veggie mix on preheated sheet tray and roast 15-20 minutes.
  6. Halfway through, add the salmon to the top rack and continue roasting until salmon reaches an internal temp of 135°F (57°C).
  7. Plate the succotash, top with salmon, and drizzle with the reserved miso glaze.

Mongolian Beef

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 450 g flank steak (or sub top round) 1lb
  • 10 g soy sauce (marinade) 2t
  • 10 g sesame oil 2t
  • 15 g Shaoxing cooking wine (or mirin/white wine) 1T
  • 1 g baking soda ¼t
  • 10 g cornstarch (marinade) 1T
  • 5 g MSG 1t
  • 200 g 1 1/2c cornstarch for dredging
  • 225 g bell peppers, sliced 2 medium
  • 75 g scallions, whites sliced thinly, greens cut into 2" pieces 1½ bunches
  • 15 g grated or minced 1T ginger
  • 25 g garlic, minced 5-6 cloves
  • 5-6 dried chilies e.g., Chile de Arbol
  • 360 ml peanut oil (for frying) 1.5c
  • 60 g packed brown sugar ¼c
  • 85 g soy sauce (sauce) ¼c
  • 120 g beef stock ½c
  • 5 g corn starch (sauce) 1 3/4t
Instructions
  1. Slice flank steak into three pieces along the grain. Then slice each piece against the grain into ¼” / .5cm strips.
  2. In a bowl, combine sliced beef with soy sauce, sesame oil, Shaoxing, baking soda, 10g/1T cornstarch, and MSG. Toss to coat then marinate for 10-15 minutes.
  3. Place about 1.5c cornstarch in a medium bowl. Toss marinated beef strips in cornstarch, ensuring each piece is lightly coated.
  4. Shake off excess using a colander or salad spinner. Let coated beef sit for 10 minutes to hydrate.
  5. Grate or mince ginger and mince garlic. Keep separate.
  6. Slice scallions, separating whites and greens. Thinly slice whites. Cut green tops into 2″/5cm pieces.
  7. Slice bell peppers into ¼” strips.
  8. In a bowl, mix brown sugar, soy sauce, beef stock, and 5g cornstarch. Stir until cornstarch is dissolved.
  9. Heat peanut oil in a wok or saucepan over medium heat to 375-400°F (190-205°C).
  10. Fry beef in 3 batches, cooking for 2-3 minutes per batch until crispy. Avoid touching beef during frying. Transfer to a paper towel-lined tray.
  11. Remove the oil from the wok and wipe out any leftover solids with paper towels. Reheat wok over high heat until water sizzles upon contact.
  12. Add a generous drizzle of peanut oil and dried chilies. Fry for 30 seconds.
  13. Add scallion whites. Toss. Add ginger and garlic. Stir-fry for 1 minute, stirring constantly. Lower heat if necessary to be sure ginger and garlic don’t burn.
  14. Add bell peppers and stir-fry for 90 seconds.
  15. Pour in the sauce around the edge of the wok and bring to a simmer, cooking for 60-90 seconds until slightly thickened.
  16. Add crispy beef to the wok and toss to coat with sauce. Add scallion greens and fold in.

Moroccan Ground Beef Stew

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 450 g ground beef 1lb
  • 225 g onion diced, 1½c
  • 20 g garlic pressed, 4-5 cloves
  • 15 g flour 2T, AP or GF
  • 20 g ras el hanout 4t, see below for recipe
  • 1 g cinnamon ¼t
  • 35 g tomato paste 2½T
  • 15 g beef bouillon 1T, paste or powder
  • 400 g beef stock 1 2/3c
  • 240 g chickpeas drained, 1 can
  • 5–10 g brown sugar 1–2t
  • 10 g chopped cilantro ¼c
  • 15 g smoked paprika
  • 3 g turmeric
  • 3 g cumin
  • 3 g coriander
  • 1 g ground clove
  • 2 g cinnamon
  • 1 pinch cayenne
  • 1 c couscous
  • 1 c water
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 3 t butter
Instructions
  1. Combine all spices and mix well.
  2. Brown and crumble beef. Add onion + garlic. Sauté 4–5 min.
  3. Add flour & spices. Stir 1 min.
  4. Add tomato paste, BTB, stock. Simmer 10 min to thicken.
  5. Add chickpeas + brown sugar. Simmer 2 min.
  6. Stir in cilantro. Serve w/ couscous + thinned yogurt w/ lemon.
  7. Boil water, add salt, butter. Stir in couscous, remove from heat, cover, let sit 5min.

Mussels in White Wine Sauce

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 450 g mussels
  • 1500 g water
  • 500 g ice
  • 60 g salt
  • 3 tbsp olive oil
  • 30 g shallot finely chopped
  • 30 g garlic pressed or minced
  • 1 pinch chili flake
  • fresh cracked black pepper
  • 15 g tomato paste
  • 100 g dry white wine sauvignon blanc or unoaked chardonnay
  • 60 g cold butter cut into pieces
  • 10 g parsley chopped
  • crusty baguette crisped in the oven and sliced
Instructions
  1. Mix water, ice, and salt in a big bowl and stir until the salt dissolves. Then add mussels and soak for 30 minutes in the refrigerator.
  2. Scrub shells with a paper towel or a steel wool, then remove the beard by pinching it against the shell and pulling toward the hinge. Hold cleaned mussels in the fridge until you're ready to cook. Discard any dead mussels.
  3. Finely chop shallot and mince/press garlic through a garlic press. Heat a baguette or chunk of bread in a 375°F / 190°C oven for 8-10 minutes.
  4. In a saucier or dutch oven, combine oil and shallot. Sweat over medium heat, 5 minutes until softened and translucent. Once softened, add a pinch of chili flakes and a few cranks of black pepper and fry for 30 seconds.
  5. Add tomato paste to the pan and cook it for another 1-2 minutes, stirring so it darkens a shade and loses its raw edge.
  6. When tomato paste is beginning to stick to the bottom of the pot, add wine and bring it to a hard boil while scraping up any tomato from the bottom of the pot.
  7. Add cleaned mussels, bring liquid back to a boil, add tight lid, and steam for about 2½ minutes or until sheets have opened and mussel meat looks plump and cooked through.
  8. Drop heat to low, add cubed cold butter, add lid and swirl the pan to emulsify the butter for a minute or until the butter has melted.
  9. Add parsley, then taste broth and adjust salt if needed. Serve right away with the toasty crusty bread for soaking up the broth.

Neapolitan Pizza for a Home Pizza Oven Recipe

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 450 g cool water 73-75℉ / 22-23℃
  • .7 g instant yeast
  • 665 g 00 flour
  • 18 g salt
  • 28 oz can tomato sauce
  • 7 g salt
  • 75 g tomato sauce
  • fresh buffalo-milk mozarella torn into pieces
  • fresh basil leaves
  • extra virgin olive oil
  • flaky salt
Instructions
  1. Add yeast and salt to 450 grams of 73 to 75°F (23°C) water and stir until dissolved. Add 00 flour and stir with a sturdy spoon until the water has been mostly absorbed and the dough has formed into a shaggy mass.
  2. Using a wet hand, pinch and squeeze the dough with a claw grip while rotating the bowl. Scrape down the sides and continue until there are no major lumps or dry spots. The dough will still be sticky.
  3. Cover bowl and rest dough at room temperature for 30 minutes.
  4. Using a wet hand, grab one edge of the dough and stretch it until you feel resistance. Fold it over the center toward the other side of the dough ball. Rotate bowl and repeat 4-6 times or until the dough starts to tighten. Flip the dough over so the folded side is down and round and tuck it under itself to create a ball.
  5. Cover and rest for 30 minutes, then repeat the same series of folds once more. After the second fold, it should look and feel like real dough, with a smooth, tight skin and very little stickiness.
  6. Cover the bowl and leave the dough at room temperature for 8 to 10 hours. It is ready when it has roughly doubled in size, the top is domed, and bubbles are visible across the surface and along the sides.
  7. Place dough onto your work surface and divide into four roughly 275-gram pieces. Gently degas the dough to eliminate excess bubbles, then fold over the sides and roll it into a taut ball.
  8. Lightly oil a plastic half sheet-tray cover and place the dough balls on it. Refrigerate uncovered for five minutes so the warm dough can begin cooling without trapping that room temperature heat. Flip the metal sheet tray upside down over the dough as a lid. Cold ferment for at least 2 hours, ideally 8 to 24 hours and up to 3 days.
  9. Remove dough from the refrigerator 60 minutes before baking.
  10. Thoroughly preheat the pizza oven before you begin shaping. I run the oven at full heat for 1 hour, until the stone is at or around 1000°F (535°C). If your oven gets this hot, time your pizza build so you can turn the flame way down or even off for 5- 10 minutes before baking (if needed) to let the oven floor temp cool to 900°F (485°C) before baking.
  11. Make tomato sauce by combining a 28-ounce can of crushed tomatoes with 7 grams salt. Pulse briefly with an immersion blender, stopping while the sauce is still slightly coarse.
  12. Flour your work surface and transfer a dough ball onto it. Lightly flour the top, then press from the center outward, leaving the outer inch untouched.
  13. Pop any large, thin surface bubbles as you go, but avoid pressing down or degassing the outer rim. Once the dough is pressed into an 8-inch round, pull and stretch it as shown in the video until it is roughly 11 to 12 inches across.
  14. Transfer the shaped dough to a perforated pizza peel dusted thoroughly with flour.
  15. Spread 70 to 80 grams tomato sauce in a thin spiral, leaving about 1 inch of bare dough around the edge. Tear the fresh mozzarella into small pieces and space them evenly around the pizza. Add a few fresh basil leaves, a drizzle of extra-virgin olive oil, and a light sprinkle of flaky salt.
  16. Give the peel a small test shimmy before walking to the oven. The entire pizza should move freely on the peel. If one spot sticks, lift that edge and flick a little flour underneath it. A perforated peel helps extra flour fall away, which means less burnt, bitter flour on the bottom of the pizza.
  17. Again, if needed, plan to turn your preheated oven temp down or off about 10 minutes before you’re ready to bake to let the floor cool to 900°F (485°C). When you’re ready to bake, turn the oven back up to medium-high heat (or to high if your oven struggles to reach 1000°F (538°C) . The stone should be 900°F to 950°F (510°C) when the pizza goes in. If it climbs above 950°F, turn the flame off again for about 5 minutes.
  18. Launch the pizza into the oven and keep it on the spot it lands for most of the bake. Rotate it a quarter turn every 15 to 20 seconds using a turning peel so every edge gets an even blast of heat from the flame.
  19. Bake for about 90 seconds total. Pull the pizza when the rim is puffed and spotted, the cheese is juicy and just melted, and the bottom has scattered char.
  20. Move the pizza to a wire rack for about one minute. Cut and serve immediately.

New Jersey Style Pizza

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 375 g warm water 86F/30C
  • 4 g instant yeast
  • 15 g olive oil
  • 15 g sugar
  • 12 g kosher salt
  • 600 g all purpose flour I use 11.7% protein
  • 800 g whole peeled tomatoes canned
  • 50 g tomato paste
  • 1 g dried oregano
  • 1 g dried basil
  • Pinch of red chile flakes
  • 7 g kosher salt
  • 10 g brown sugar
  • olive oil
  • 3-4 cloves garlic minced
  • full fat aged mozzarella
  • parmesan grated
Instructions
  1. Add ingredients to bowl of stand mixer with dough hook attachment. Mix on low for 1-2 min or until dough forms into a mass. Increase speed to high and finish mixing for 3-4 more minutes. When dough has become smooth and doesn’t tear/shear when you tug it, it’s done.
  2. Divide dough into 4 approx 250g pieces. Shape each into a ball.
  3. Place each ball into a well oiled bowl, cover, and refrigerate for 1-2 days.
  4. 3 hours before you’d like to eat pizza, pull dough from fridge and allow to come to room temp.
  5. Remove 3 tomatoes from the 28oz can and roughly chop them.
  6. Add tomato paste, oregano, basil, chile flake, salt, and sugar into the 28oz can and puree with immersion blender until smooth.
  7. Heat a long squeeze of olive oil (about 2-3Tbsp) in a saute pan over med-high. Add garlic. Stir and saute for about 30 sec. Add chopped tomatoes and cook for about a minute until reduced. Add the rest of the tomato puree to the pan.
  8. Reduce heat to medium and cook for about 15 min or until the sauce is reduced by half.
  9. Preheat oven at highest setting (for me that’s 550F/290C) with pizza steel/stone.
  10. Grate mozzarella on largest holes of box grater. Place grated cheese in the freezer for about 45min.
  11. Flour work surface and one of the doughs, and flip it out on to your work surface and flour the wet/non-floured side of the dough.
  12. Flatten dough with your hand then with a rolling pin, roll out into about a 10″/25cm round. Finish shaping by hand as shown in video @7:50 until dough is about a 12″/30cm round.
  13. Load dough onto pizza peel that has been well dusted with semolina flour.
  14. Spread about 2c/240g of the chilled shredded cheese onto the dough followed by a generous pinch of grated parm. Add several large spoonfuls of the pizza sauce (about 120g/1/2c total per pizza). Spread slightly, making sure to leave plenty of the surface un-sauced. Load onto the pre-heated pizza steel/stone and cook for 6min. Bottom should be well crisped up. Place onto a wire rack to cool slightly before cutting.

New York Style Pizza

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 575 g water 68°F, 20C
  • 10 g instant yeast
  • 25 g olive oil
  • 450 g AP flour
  • 450 g bread flour
  • 20 g salt
  • 25 g granulated sugar
  • 800 g canned crushed tomatoes bianco di napoli if you can find it
  • 8 g salt
  • 15 g sugar
  • 225 g part-skim mozzarella shredded
  • 225 g whole-milk mozzarella shredded
  • Semolina flour for peel
  • pinch of flaky salt per pizza
Instructions
  1. In bowl, stir yest into water. Add oil salt, sugar, and flours and stir until no dry bits remain.
  2. Knead lightly with a squeezing motion 2 min. Continue kneading with your hand/palm for another minute and shape into a rough ball. (Dough temp should be 76F/25C).
  3. Rest 20 min covered.
  4. Perform stretch and fold by lifting one edge of the dough up about 12 inches or until resistance is met and laying it into the middle of the dough ball. Repeat this for each edge of the dough (act like the dough is a square and just do each side) then grab from underneath the dough and flip it over to put the seam side down. Round and tuck dough into a ball. Cover and rest again for 20 minutes.
  5. Perform final stretch and fold. Rest once more for 15 minutes.
  6. Divide into 4 × 385g pieces. Shape each piece by pulling sides into the middle, then rolling on the seam to shape into a ball, make sure you are creating tension on the surface of the dough.
  7. Place on parchment lined, oiled tray. Cover and refrigerate 24 hrs.
  8. Sauce: Stir tomatoes with salt + sugar. Blend half til smooth, recombine.
  9. Cheese: Grate both cheeses
  10. Remove dough from fridge 45–60 min before baking.
  11. Preheat oven 45 min with stone/steel on lowest rack to hottest temp (ideally 550°F/287°C) + second steel 8–10″ above.
  12. Place dough ball on floured work surface, bottom side facing up. Flour dough, press and stretch into ~12" (34cm) round.
  13. Place on semolina floured peel.
  14. Assemble & bake (per pizza):
  15. Spread ~200g/5oz (3/4c) sauce, leaving 1/2″ rim.
  16. Top with 175–200g mozz blend.
  17. Stretch pizza out to edges of the peel.
  18. Add pinch of flaky salt. Shimmy peel to check for sticking.
  19. Launch onto bottom steel. Bake ~3 min, rotate 180, then 2–3 min more until browned, bubbly, slightly charred.
  20. Cut into slices then re-heat individual slices on the bottom pizza steel for 90sec-2mins just before eating.

Oklahoma Onion Burger

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2 lbs boneless beef short ribs 1kg
  • 4 small white onions shaved
  • Salt
  • Ground black pepper
  • Brioche buns toasted
  • American cheese I use kraft singles bc you can’t match the melty sauciness. If you can’t find it, any mild and very melty cheese will work
Instructions
  1. Cut short ribs into chunks (about 1.5”/4cm). Place on plate or tray and freeze for 15min.
  2. Put 15-minute frozen chunks into a food processor or meat grinder. If using a food processor, in at least 2 batches, pulse meat until it has a loose ground beef texture.
  3. Measure patties into 2.5oz/70g pieces. Roll into a ball and work slightly until beef sticks together.
  4. Place between pieces of parchment then flatten into 5-6”/12-15cm in diameter, making sure to flatten patty so that it’s even thinner around the edges. When finished, refrigerate patties until you’re ready to cook them.
  5. Peel and cut poles off onions then slice into the thinnest rings possible. I like to use a mandolin.
  6. Preheat the largest nonstick pan or cast iron that you have over med-high. I use a 14” pan which can cook 4 patties at once. A griddle will work well if you have one. Place patties in the pan and hit them with a pinch salt and pepper, then about 1/2c of shaved onions onto each patty. Press each patty down for at least 10 seconds. When bottom side of patties has gotten brown and caramelized, flip to side 2, onion side down. Cook for about another 20 seconds before adding a slide of cheese. On half of the patties, place the bottom (pre-toasted) burger bun and the top burger bun directly on top of the bottom bun to steam and warm while patties finish cooking. Total cook time on the burgers should only be about 2-2.5 mins.
  7. To assemble, remove top buns. Flip patties with bottom buns over, bun side down. Onions will be facing up. Stack one of the patties without the bun on top of the onions (onions down, cheese up). Top with top bun.

One Pot Carbonara

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 650 g thick cut bacon 1 1/2 lbs
  • 150 g Parmigiano Reggiano cheese aged 18 months, 5.3 oz
  • 150 g Pecorino Romano cheese 5.3 oz
  • 3 large eggs
  • 9 egg yolks
  • 5-6 g cracked black pepper 2t
  • 10 g cornstarch 4t
  • Olive oil
  • 115 mL water 1/2c
  • 900 g spaghetti 2lbs
  • 2250 g water mL , 8 cups
  • 10 g salt 2t
Instructions
  1. Dice the bacon into small squares
  2. Shred the parm and pecorino on the feather shred side of your box grater
  3. Add cheese to a bowl and mix with eggs, yolks, pepper, and cornstarch
  4. Heat a large dutch oven or heavy bottomed pot over medium heat.
  5. Add a generous amount of olive oil, the diced bacon, and about 1/2c water. Bring it to a simmer and cook until the water evaporates, and the bacon fat is nicely rendered.
  6. Continue to cook the bacon in its own fat for 5-10 more minutes over medium until it becomes reddish-brown and crispy.
  7. Remove the pot from the heat and transfer the bacon, along with its fat, to a bowl. Pour off about 2/3 of the bacon fat, leaving approximately 3-4 tablespoons of fat in the pot.
  8. Prepare the Pasta by breaking 2 pounds of spaghetti noodles in half.
  9. Return the bacon pot to high heat and add 2250mL (8 cups) of water (leaving any bacon fat that was left in the pot) and deglaze the pot by scraping up the browned bacon bits that are stuck to the bottom. Bring to a boil then add 10g/2t salt and the broken spaghetti. Stir, making sure all the pasta is submerged. Reduce heat to medium low, cover, and cook for 10 minutes, stirring halfway through to ensure pasta isn’t stuck together and pasta remains submerged. Cook until just past al dente.
  10. Ladle about 1.5 to 2 cups of the hot pasta cooking liquid into the egg and cheese mixture. Whisk to combine, tempering the mixture.
  11. Pour the egg and cheese mixture and cooked bacon + about 3 spoons of bacon fat over the cooked pasta.
  12. Stir gently over low heat for 1-2 minutes until the sauce thickens and becomes creamy.
  13. Taste and adjust for salt if needed.
  14. Plate and top with additional pieces of bacon, grated parm, and a black pepper

One Pot Spicy Sausage Rigatoni

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • olive oil for searing
  • 680 g hot Italian sausage
  • 250 g onion (1 large) grated
  • 20 g garlic (5-6 cloves) minced
  • 50 g Calabrian chiles chopped
  • 15 g salt divided
  • 794 g crushed tomatoes (1 28 oz can)
  • 5 g granulated sugar
  • 1000 g water
  • 1 lb dried rigatoni pasta I like De Cecco brand
  • 100 g heavy cream
  • 50 g aged Parmesan grated
  • 10 g fresh basil minced
  • salt to taste
  • red pepper flakes
  • cracked black pepper
  • olive oil
Instructions
  1. Preheat the Oven to 425°F / 220°C, no convection
  2. Heat a 3 second glug of olive oil in a large heavy bottomed pot (at least 5 qts capacity) over medium-high. Add the Italian sausage, spreading it out for maximum surface area and start breaking down into a small crumble. If the bottom begins to take on a lot of fond, add a splash of water. A full crumble and cook shouldl take about 5-6 minutes.
  3. Once the sausage is finely crumbled and mostly cooked thru, add the grated onion, minced garlic, chopped Calabrian chilies, and about half of the salt or 8 grams / 1¼ tsp. Cook for 5-6 minutes, or until the onions have softened.
  4. Pour in one 28oz can of crushed tomatoes, water, sugar and remaining salt. Stir to combine and bring it up to a simmer.
  5. Once the sauce is simmering, stir in the rigatoni. Push the pasta down under the sauce until it's fully submerged.
  6. Bake, uncovered, at 425°F/220°C for about 20 minutes, stirring every 5 minutes. Be sure to re-submerge the rigatoni each time you stir.
  7. VERY GENTLY stir in the cream, grated parmesan and minced basil. Taste and adjust salt level if needed, then let the pot rest for at least 10 minutes before serving.
  8. To serve, top with additional grated parm, cracked black pepper, and some chili flakes if you want a little more heat.
