Orange Chicken

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 450 g boneless skinless chicken breasts large diced
  • 30 g soy sauce
  • 30 g mirin
  • 3 g corn starch
  • 4 g baking soda
  • 400 g ap flour
  • 15 g kosher salt
  • 6 g baking soda
  • 400 g corn starch
  • water
  • 300 g additional ap flour
  • neutral oil oil should be around 2″/5cm deep and pot should be no more than halfway full of oil
  • 30 g neutral high smoke point oil
  • 10 g garlic grated, 2 cloves
  • 10 g ginger grated, about a 1.5" piece
  • 20 g scallions thinly sliced, only about bottom ½/⅔ of scallions
  • 125 g chicken stock
  • 75 g mirin
  • 50 g soy sauce
  • 10 g sesame oil
  • 50 g sugar
  • 25 g rice vinegar
  • juice of 1 large orange
  • orange zest about 8-10 healthy zests
  • 5 g corn starch
  • pinch chile flake
  • 2 g corn starch slurry if needed 2g corn starch mixed with 4g of water
Instructions
  1. Cut chicken breasts into a large bite-sized dice.
  2. In a bowl, whisk together soy, mirin, corn starch, and baking soda. Add chicken chunks and stir to coat.
  3. Marinate for 20 minutes.
  4. Into a bowl, whisk together 400g ap flour, salt, baking soda and corn starch.
  5. In a second bowl, add about ¼ of the dry breading mixture. To that small portion of dry breader, whisk in water, a little at a time, until you're left with a loose pancake batter texture.
  6. Into the bowl which the dry flour mixture, add about ¼ cup of the wet breading slurry you just made. Mix and crumble the wet into the dry, creating a dry crumbly texture.
  7. Grab a third bowl and fill it with 2-3 cups of AP flour. Add in the marinated chicken pieces. Mix to coat well on all sides.
  8. Shake off as much of the ap flour mixture from the chicken as possible, then add chicken into the wet breading slurry. Toss to coat well. Now move pieces of wet battered chicken into the craggy dry breader mix. Mix to coat well.
  9. Transfer chicken pieces to a tray.
  10. Preheat a large heavy bottomed pot over medium-high heat with about 2 quarts of neutral oil (or about 2-3"/5-8cm of oil, leaving plenty of room at the top of your pot so oil doesn't boil over when you add chicken) for 10 minutes or until oil reaches a temp of 300°F/150°C.
  11. Gently lower breaded chicken into the oil (use a spider to avoid splashing oil).
  12. Fry for 4-5 minutes at 300°F/150°C. After 4-5 minutes or when nuggets are a light blonde golden color, remove from oil and transfer to a wire rack to drain.
  13. Increase heat under oil pot to medium high to increase oil temp to 350°F/175°C.
  14. Carefully lower the once-fried nuggets back into the oil to cook for about another 90 seconds or until they’ve taken on a nice golden brown color.
  15. Transfer onto a wire rack and keep warm in a low oven while you make your orange chicken sauce.
  16. To make the sauce, heat a generous squeeze of neutral oil in a large nonstick pan over medium high heat.
  17. Into that pan, add ginger, garlic, and scallions and cook, stirring often, for about a minute until fragrant.
  18. Whisk together chicken stock, mirin, soy sauce, sesame oil, sugar, rice vin, orange zest and juice and corn starch. Add sauce to pan with ginger/garlic/scallion mixture along with a pinch of chile flakes. Bring to a simmer and reduce sauce by about half, about 2 minutes.
  19. After reducing, check the consistency of sauce. If it’s not thick enough to leave a trail when you run a spoon through it, stir in a splash of corn starch slurry. Bring it back up to a boil and check the consistency again.
  20. Add cooked nuggets into pan with the reduced sauce. Gently toss to coat, careful not to over work to loosen the breading.
  21. Serve over rice and top with a pinch of sesame seeds and chile flakes.

Osso Buco

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 4-5 lbs veal shanks (or substitute with 5-6 lbs pork shoulder or beef chuck roast, cut into large pieces) 1.8-2.3 kg
  • 350 g onion, roughly chopped 2 ½ c
  • 250 g carrot, roughly chopped 2 c
  • 250 g bulb fennel, roughly chopped 1 medium
  • 30 g garlic, peeled 6-7 cloves
  • 20 g anchovies, chopped 5-6 pieces
  • 30 g tomato paste 2 T
  • 25 g unsalted butter 2 T
  • 25 g all-purpose flour 3 T
  • 300 g dry red wine 1 ¼ c
  • 15 g Better Than Bouillon Roasted Beef Base 1 T
  • 850 g beef stock 4 c
  • 2-3 T olive oil for cooking
  • Salt
  • 1000 g chicken stock 4 c
  • ½ g saffron threads (or 1 g if not grinding) 1t
  • 5 g salt 1 t
  • 225 g onion, finely minced/brunoised 1 ½ c
  • 10 g garlic, minced 2 t
  • 400 g arborio rice 2 c
  • 200 g dry white wine ¾ c
  • 75 g parmesan cheese, grated ¾ c
  • 50 g unsalted butter 3 ½ T
  • Olive oil as needed
Instructions
  1. Tie each veal shank with kitchen twine to keep the meat compact. Generously season with salt and let sit for 15 minutes (or refrigerate for a few hours).
  2. Preheat oven to 500°F (260°C). Place shanks on a wire rack over a foil-lined tray, drizzle with olive oil, and roast for 30 minutes until browned.
  3. Roughly chop onion, carrot, fennel, and garlic. Mince these in a food processor until finely chopped but not a paste.
  4. Heat olive oil in a Dutch oven over medium-high heat. Add the minced vegetables and a pinch of salt. Sweat for 10-15 minutes until softened.
  5. Add anchovies and tomato paste into the veggies, stirring and cooking for 2 minutes. Add butter and melt. Add a splash of water here if the bottom of the pot is starting to get brown.
  6. Stir in flour to form a weak roux. Cook for about 30 seconds.
  7. Add red wine, scraping the bottom to deglaze. Cook for 2-3 minutes.
  8. Add Better Than Bouillon and beef stock. Bring to a simmer then nestle the seared shanks into the liquid along with any veal cooking liquid from the roasting pan (if it’s not burnt). Cover and braise in a 300°F (150°C) oven for 2 hours, checking at 90 minutes. Cook uncovered for the last 30-40 minutes.
  9. Cool braise at room temp for 2 hours then refrigerate overnight for best flavor.
  10. To finish, reheat over medium-low heat, scraping the pot bottom. Reduce liquid by 30-40%.
  11. Heat chicken stock with saffron and salt until just below simmering.
  12. In a separate pot, sweat onion, garlic and a pinch of salt in olive oil over medium heat in a saucier for about 5min. Avoid caramelization.
  13. Add arborio rice to onion/garlic and stir to coat. Fry for 5 minutes until translucent.
  14. Add white wine to rice and reduce until mostly evaporated.
  15. Add about 2c of the warmed saffron stock into the rice, bring to a simmer and stir frequently (about every 30 secs) until absorbed. Repeat twice. After adding the third round of liquid, continue stirring every 30 seconds until about half of the liquid has absorbed. At this point, cover the pot and cook over low heat for 3-4 mins. Taste for doneness. The rice should have bite, but not be chalky or crunchy.
  16. To finish, gently stir in parmesan, butter, and a splash of water off heat. Adjust with salt if needed.
  17. Place a scoop of saffron risotto on a plate, nestle a veal shank on top, removing the string. Spoon reduced braising liquid and vegetables over the meat. Garnish with chopped parsley and serve immediately.

PBJ From Scratch

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • SANDWICH BREAD:
  • 130 g or 1/2c warm water 86F/30C
  • 5 g or 2tsp instant yeast
  • 1 egg
  • 45 g or 3 1/4Tbsp melted butter not hot
  • 45 g or 3 2/3Tbsp granulated sugar
  • 100 g roasted and riced russet potatoes about 1/2-2/3 of 1 potato, peel, wrap in foil and roast at 400F/205C for 1 hour
  • 430 g or 3 1/2c AP flour
  • 10 g or 1 3/4tsp kosher salt
  • RASPBERRY JAM makes about 2 pints:
  • 500 g or 4c fresh raspberries
  • 75 g or 1/3c lemon juice
  • 75 g or 1/3c white distilled vinegar
  • 700 g or 3.5c granulated sugar
  • 60 g or 1/3c + 2Tbsp regular fruit pectin
  • PEANUT BUTTER:
  • 500 g or 3.5c spanish or runner peanuts I’m using spanish
  • WHIPPED SALTED BUTTER:
  • 450 g/1lb room temp butter
  • 3-4 g/Pinch kosher salt
Instructions
  1. SANDWICH BREAD:
  2. Using dough hook attachment, mix on medium for 4 mins. Increase speed to high and mix for a additional 7-8min. Dough should be clearing the bowl and pass the tug test when done.
  3. Round dough into a taught ball (see video @__1:45__). Cover bowl & ferment at room temp for 2 hours.
  4. Prepare a 1.5lb loaf pan with oil or pan spray.
  5. Flip dough onto lightly floured surface and press to degas. Shape as shown in video @__6:52__ and place into pan, seam side down. Mist with water, cover with towel, & proof at room temp for 60-90min.
  6. Mist loaf well with water then score top.
  7. Load into preheated 400F/205C oven, spritz oven well with 12-15 spritzes of water, & bake for 30min.
  8. RASPBERRY JAM:
  9. Add all ingredients except pectin to a heavy bottomed pot over high heat. When mix reaches a simmer, stir to combine.
  10. Cook until tender for about 10min. When mixture begins to foam, reduce heat to med/med-high , stirring occasionally.
  11. Strain seeds out of ⅓-½ of the mixture, making sure to force all juice and pulp back into the mix. Discard strained seeds.
  12. Return pot to high heat and bring to a boil then whisk in fruit pectin and continue boiling and reducing until temp reaches 220F/105C.
  13. Ladle into jars with lids and cool to room temp then refrigerate for 12 hours to set.
  14. PEANUT BUTTER:
  15. Spread peanuts onto sheet tray and roast at 300F/150C for 40-50min. Add to food processor and process for 10-12mins or until smooth and creamy.
  16. WHIPPED SALTED BUTTER:
  17. Mix with whisk attachment on high for 3-5min until fluffy and lightened in color.
  18. TO BUILD THE PBJ:
  19. Cut thick slices of bread. Spread 2 large spoons of peanut butter on 1 slice. Top with a pinch of flaky salt. On the other slice of bread, spread 2 spoons of butter. Squeeze jam on top of peanut butter (rather than spreading) to maintain layers of each ingredient.

Perfect Carnitas Recipe

Serves 8
Ingredients
  • 5 lb pork shoulder cut into 2-3 inch cubes
  • 50 g salt
  • 2 g dried oregano
  • 10 g onion powder
  • 30 g garlic minced
  • 100 g water or stock
  • 1 cinnamon stick
  • 4 bay leaves
  • 1 orange quartered
  • 150 g rendered pork fat reserved from cooking
  • 150 g pork gold (cooking liquid) reserved from cooking
  • flour tortillas warmed
  • white onion chopped
  • cilantro chopped
  • salsa taquera or your favorite salsa or taco sauce
Instructions
  1. Start by cutting pork shoulder into 2 to 3 inch cubes. If there's a bone, try to remove it. No need for perfect butchery. Just cut the meat off in chunks.
  2. Combine salt, oregano, garlic, and onion powder in a bowl with the pork shoulder cubes and mix until every piece is evenly coated. Then cure in the fridge for 1 hour.
  3. Place the pork in a wide, heavy bottomed pot in a tight even layer.
  4. Preheat the oven to 250°F/120°C.
  5. Add 1 cinnamon stick, 1 orange (quartered and juiced into the pot), 3 bay leaves, and 100g water or stock. Cover with the lid and roast at 250°F/120°C for 3 hours, checking and flipping all pieces of pork over after 90 minutes.
  6. After 3 hours of total cook time, pull the pork and check it for doneness. A fork should slide in easily and the meat should shred, but it shouldn't fall apart. It should still hold its shape. If it's not there yet, give it another 30 minutes and check again.
  7. Once done, flip any pieces of pork that are out of the liquids so they're now submerged. Re-cover the pork, and rest at room temperature for 30 to 60 minutes then refrigerate covered overnight in its own liquid.
  8. The next day, or after 8-12 hours minimum chilling, remove the pieces of pork from the pot. Separate and set aside the rendered pork fat and the cooking liquid (aka pork gold) underneath it.
  9. Shred pork pieces into a mix of larger chunks and smaller threadier pieces. Discard any pieces that are mostly fat.
  10. Add about 150g rendered pork fat and 150g pork gold to the shredded meat, then mix until everything is evenly coated.
  11. Heat a nonstick sauté pan over medium-high. Add pork in a thin, even layer, then press it down and let it sizzle untouched for about 90 seconds. When the pork is golden and crispy on one side, flip and repeat.
  12. Once crisped, pile the carnitas onto warm flour tortillas and top with chopped onion, cilantro, and salsa taquera or roasted tomatillo salsa.

Peri Peri Chicken

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 2400 g whole chicken
  • 25 g kosher salt
  • 12 g sugar
  • 2400 g bone-in skin on chicken leg quarters 6 quarters
  • 20 g kosher salt
  • 10 g sugar
  • 150 g chopped onion
  • 250 g chopped red bell pepper
  • 30 g garlic
  • 15 g Thai bird’s eye chiles sub 2 small habaneros
  • 10 g smoked paprika
  • 15 g kosher salt
  • 30 g sugar
  • 200 g olive oil
  • zest of 2 lemons
  • 50 g lemon juice about 2 lemons
  • 20 g white distilled vinegar
  • olive oil or pan spray for grill grates
Instructions
  1. In a food processor, process onion, red bell pepper, garlic, chilis, paprika, salt, sugar, olive oil, lemon zest, lemon juice, and vinegar until smooth but still a little chunky.
  2. Blend until broken down and saucy, but not completely smooth. The texture should be loose and rustic.
  3. Reserve half of the marinade in the fridge for use during cooking and finishing.
  4. Flip the chicken breast-side down. Using kitchen shears, cut along both sides of the spine and remove it. Flip the chicken breast-side up and firmly press down to crack the breastbone so the bird lays flat.
  5. Rub chicken all over with the salt and sugar mixture. Let IT sit uncovered for 25 minutes at room temperature.
  6. Liberally brush both sides of the chicken with half of the prepared marinade. Refrigerate uncovered overnight. Reserve/refrigerate unused marinade.
  7. The next day, preheat the oven to 285°F (140°C). Place chicken on a rack-lined tray and roast for 1 hour and 15 minutes. The internal temperature should reach 135–140°F (57–60°C) in the breast.
  8. Increase oven temperature to 475°F (245°C). Brush the bird liberally with reserved marinade (about ¼ cup). Roast for 15 minutes, then brush again lightly and roast an additional 10–15 minutes until deeply browned. Rotate bird during the final 5 minutes for even caramelization.
  9. While the chicken finishes roasting, pour the remaining marinade into a saucepan. Bring to a boil and simmer for 10 minutes to reduce and sterilize. Stir frequently.
  10. Once chicken reaches 165°F (74°C) in the breast and 175°F (80°C) in the thigh, remove from oven. Brush one final time with the cooked sauce and let rest for 10–15 minutes. Carve and serve.
  11. Using a paring knife, debone the chicken leg quarters, keeping skin intact. You can also grill bone-in.
  12. Season both sides of deboned leg quarters with salt and sugar mixture. Let them sit for 25 minutes.
  13. Spoon approximately 3 tbsp of marinade per piece, coating thoroughly. Refrigerate overnight uncovered.
  14. Preheat grill to 375–400°F (190–205°C). Oil grates generously to prevent sticking.
  15. Place chicken skin-side up on the grill. Dab with more marinade. Close lid and cook 3–5 minutes.
  16. Reduce the heat on the grill to medium-low and carefully flip to skin-side down. Grill another 3–4 minutes. Sauce the exposed side again. Repeat flipping every 3–5 minutes, saucing and rotating as needed for even browning.
  17. Grill for a total of 25–30 minutes until skin is well rendered and internal temp reaches 180°F (82°C). Remove from grill and brush with cooked, reduced peri peri sauce while resting.

Pizza Bagels

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • FOR THE BAGELS:
  • 475 grams all-purpose flour 3 ¾ cups
  • 10 grams sugar 2 1/2 teaspoons
  • 12 grams salt 2 teaspoons
  • 265 grams 90°F beer 1 1/2 cup, 32°C
  • 6 grams instant yeast 2 teaspoons
  • A drizzle of molasses
  • FOR THE PIZZA SAUCE:
  • Long drizzle of olive oil
  • ½ red onion small diced
  • 3 cloves garlic minced
  • Pinch of salt
  • 28 ounce can crushed tomatoes 800 grams
  • 7 grams salt 1 ½ teaspoons
  • 10 grams sugar 2 ½ teaspoons
  • Pinch of chili flakes
  • 1 gram dried oregano ½ teaspoon
  • 1 gram dried basil ½ teaspoon
  • 50 grams tomato paste 3 tablespoons
  • FOR TOPPINGS:
  • Mozzarella cheese full fat
  • Whole stick pepperoni the whole stick lets you cut it into tiny cubes
  • Parmesan cheese as desired
Instructions
  1. In a food processor, combine the all-purpose flour, sugar, and salt. Pulse briefly to combine.
  2. Pour yeast into the warm beer and whisk to combine, then add the beer/yeast mixture to the food processor while it is running. Process for 20-30 seconds or until the dough is fully combined.
  3. *If you prefer to mix by hand, click over to my one hour bagel recipe here for that process
  4. Transfer the dough to a cutting board and knead it by hand for 3 minutes until it becomes smooth and elastic. If the dough tears when you give it a tug, continue kneading until it becomes stronger.
  5. Place the dough in a bowl (this one’s my favorite for doughs), cover it with a lid (I use this cheap 12″ pizza pan), and let it ferment at room temperature for 20 minutes. The dough should have risen by about 20%.
  6. Divide the dough into 24 pieces, each weighing roughly 30 grams. Use a kitchen scale or eyeball it. Shape each piece into a mini bagel by degassing the dough with your fingertips (just press the down down a bit), rolling it into a tube shape and wrapping it around your pointer finger. Seal the seam by rolling the bagel back and forth on the worksurface and place on a parchment lined sheet tray.
  7. Proof the bagels by covering them with another sheet tray.
  8. This is a good point to preheat the oven to 475F/245C and bring a very large pot of water to a boil.
  9. Once the water is boiling, add a long drizzle of molasses to the water and carefully drop in 6 bagels. Boil them for 30 seconds on each side, then remove with a spider strainer or slotted spoon and place them on a parchment-lined sheet tray.
  10. Repeat the boiling process with the remaining bagels in batches of 6.
  11. Transfer the boiled bagels to the preheated oven and bake them for 13-16 minutes until they are crusty and browned.
  12. While the bagels are baking, prepare the pizza sauce. In a saucepan, heat olive oil over medium heat. Add diced red onion, minced garlic, and a pinch of salt. Stir and cook for 5 minutes until softened.
  13. Into the can of crushed tomatoes add salt, sugar, chili flakes, dried oregano, dried basil, and tomato paste and blend with an immersion blender until the herbs are well broken down. Alternatively, stir the ingredients together for a chunkier sauce. Add to the sauce pan with the onion and garlic.
  14. Bring the sauce to a simmer, reduce the heat to medium-low, and cook for 15-20 minutes to thicken it. It should be quite thick.
  15. Once the bagels are baked, allow them to cool slightly before cutting each bagel in half using a serrated knife. Increase oven temp to 500F (260C)
  16. Spread about 2 small spoons of the pizza sauce on each bagel half.
  17. Top with mozzarella cheese, tiny pepperoni cubes, and a sprinkle of parmesan
  18. Place the topped bagels on a parchment lined sheet tray and bake for 8-10 minutes or until the cheese is melted and bubbly.
  19. Remove the bagels from the oven and let them cool for a few minutes before serving.

Pot Roast

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 3-5 lb/1.5-2kg Beef chuck roast
  • 225 g 5 large Shallot or onion, chunky diced
  • 225 g 3 large Carrots, cut into obliques
  • 225 g 4-6 stalks Celery, cut into 1-2″/3-5cm pieces
  • 20 g 4-5 cloves Garlic, minced
  • Neutral high smokepoint oil (canola, vegetable, soy, avocado)
  • 50 g 3 1/2T unsalted butter
  • 25 g (1 1/2 tomato paste
  • 25 g 3T flour (all purpose or gluten free flour blend. I used bob’s red mill 1 to 1)
  • 150 g 2/3c red wine
  • 25 g 1 1/2T worcestershire
  • 15 g 1T better than bouillon beef base
  • 750 g 3c beef stock
  • 2 bay leaves
  • 3-4 springs fresh thyme
  • 6 russet potatoes
  • Salt
  • 115 g 8Tbsp unsalted butter
  • Sour cream
Instructions
  1. Liberally salt all sides of the chuck roast and allow it to sit at room temp for 20 minutes.
  2. While the beef brines, prep the veggies. Cut shallot into a chunky dice. Cut celery into 1-2″/3-5cm long planks. Cut carrots into obliques (see video @1:26) or a large chunky dice. Mince garlic.
  3. Before searing, dry beef very well on all sides with a towel.
  4. Preheat a dutch oven or large heavy-bottomed pot over high heat. Add a long squeeze of neutral oil. Drop in salted roast and press the meat down into the pan. Sear for 6-7 minutes until a brown crust has formed. Flip and sear on the second side for 5-6min. Transfer to a plate.
  5. Return the pot to medium heat and add 50g of butter, followed by veggies, garlic, and a large pinch of salt. Stir and cook to caramelize and slightly soften for about 3 min. Add tomato paste. Stir and saute for about 2 minutes until it has taken on a darker rusty color. Stir in flour for 30-60 sec.
  6. Add in wine, worcestershire and stir to deglaze solids on the bottom of the pot. When slightly thickened, add better than bouillon and stock. Bring to a simmer. When simmering, snuggle the seared beef into the pot so that it’s touching the bottom of the pot. Add bay and thyme.
  7. Add a probe thermometer if you have one, set the temp to 195F/90C. cover the pot leaving the lid slightly ajar, and load into a 275F/135C oven to cook for 4-5 hours. Checking on the beef in about 2 hours when the temp hits 195F. Check the meat at this point. It shouldn’t be shreddable yet. Return the lid to the pot, and return the pot to the oven to cook for about 2-3 more hours, checking the roast gain at the 4-4.5 hour mark. You’re now looking to maintain the temperature of about 190-205F/90C for a few more hours until the meat is fork tender and shreddable, but does not totally fall apart. If internal temp rises above this temp, turn down the oven to maintain 190-205F/90C. When done, you’ll be able to easily pull the meat apart with forks. Taste sauce for seasoning and add salt if needed.
  8. Allow beef to rest in the cooking liquids for about 30 minutes while you finish your potatoes.
  9. Serve a chunk of beef on top of baked bashed potatoes along with a few of the roasted veggies and extra sauce.
  10. Peel potatoes and warp each with foil. Bake on a sheet tray at 275F/135C for about 2 hours until they’re tender and easily mashable.
  11. Add potatoes to a tall sided pot along with a few large pinches of salt, the butter, and a large dollop (or more) of sour cream. Mash together until chunky but soft. Taste for seasoning.

Potato Gnocchi with Gorgonzola Sauce

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 1500 g russet potatoes or 3 large potatoes, 500g/1 lb needed after roasting
  • 2 large egg yolks
  • 8 g salt
  • 175 g all-purpose flour plus more for dusting
  • 275 g heavy cream
  • 65 g gorgonzola cheese
  • 20 g parmesan cheese finely grated, plus extra for garnish
  • 2 g kosher salt for sauce
  • 2 g black pepper freshly ground
  • olive oil for finishing
Instructions
  1. Poke holes up and down potatoes and bake at 350 until fork tender. Once out of the oven, cut the potatoes into quarters and leave the skin on.
  2. Carefully place the potatoes in the a potato ricer flesh side down and press through, discard the skin. When all potatoes have been passed through the ricer, combine with egg yolks and mix together fully before adding anything else.
  3. Using a bench scraper, cut the flour and salt into the potato until it begins to come together slightly and switch to your hand and knead until dough has come together. Cover with a towel or lid for 5 minutes.
  4. Divide dough into 4 sections and one at a time roll out with your hands going from the middle out until it is a uniform rope about ¾ in in diameter. Cut into ¾ in pieces and roll each piece onto a gnocchi board. Lightly dust with flour as needed. Repeat with all 4 sections.
  5. In boiling, salted water, boil gnocchis in two batches. As each batch is done (gnocchi is floating) remove from the water and toss in prepared sauce or place on a sheet tray and toss in olive oil if not cooking right away.
  6. In a sauce pan bring cream to a boil. Once boiling, whisk in remaining ingredients. Toss gnocchi in sauce. Add a splash of water if gnocchi soaks up too much sauce.

Prime Rib

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 7-10 pounds boneless prime rib roast 3-4.5 kg
  • Coarse kosher salt you’ll need a lot
  • Cracked black pepper approx. 6-9 T, depending on roast size
  • 10-12 T neutral oil e.g., peanut oil or beef fat
  • 900 g box beef stock 1 32oz
  • 50 g Worcestershire sauce 3 T
  • 25 g beef bouillon base (e.g., Better Than Bouillon) 1.5 T
  • 2 packets powdered gelatin 7g / 2 1/2t
  • 125 g sour cream or Greek yogurt ½ c
  • 75 g mayonnaise ⅓ c
  • 75 g prepared refrigerated horseradish ⅓ c
  • 10 g water 2 t
  • 3 g salt ½ t
Instructions
  1. Score the fat cap of the prime rib about ⅛” (0.5 cm) deep in a crosshatch pattern.
  2. Season all sides of the roast very generously with coarse kosher salt and cracked black pepper. Use at least 2-3 four-finger pinches of salt and pepper per side.
  3. Place the seasoned roast on a wire rack over a baking tray and refrigerate uncovered for at least 4 hours, preferably overnight. This dry brine enhances flavor and helps dry the exterior for better browning.
  4. Remove the roast from the fridge about 4 hours before cooking to bring it to room temperature.
  5. Preheat the oven to 500°F (260°C).
  6. Drizzle the roast with neutral oil and rub it evenly over the surface, ensuring you don’t disturb the salt and pepper crust.
  7. Place the roast on a foil-lined baking tray with a wire rack and sear in the oven for 15-20 minutes, or until the exterior is golden brown.
  8. Reduce the oven temperature to 250°F (120°C). Insert a probe thermometer into the thickest part of the roast. Roast for 2.5-3 hours, or until the internal temperature reaches 125°F (52°C) for medium.
  9. Remove the roast from the oven, tent loosely with foil, and let rest for 30 minutes before slicing.
  10. Combine all ingredients in a saucepan. Bring to a simmer over medium heat and reduce by 40-50%, about 15 minutes.
  11. Add rendered beef drippings from the roast (about 2-3 T) for extra flavor. Skim off excess fat if needed.
  12. Taste and adjust salt as needed. Serve warm.
  13. Combine all ingredients in a bowl and whisk until smooth and well blended.
  14. Taste and adjust seasoning if necessary.

Quick Korean BBQ

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 500 grams/1 pound ribeye steak or substitute with sirloin flap or skirt steak
  • 30 g or 2Tbsp soy sauce
  • 15 g or 1Tbsp brown sugar
  • 10 g or 2tsp Mirin
  • 3 g or 1/2tsp black pepper
  • 3 cloves of garlic grated or pressed
  • 10 g or 2tsp sesame oil
  • 10 g honey 1 long drizzle
  • 25 g or 1/2 Asian pear or substitute with Bosc or Bartlett pear, grated
  • 75 g thinly sliced white onion ½ an onion
  • 20 g or 2 bunches of scallion tops sliced into 2″ pieces
  • 400 grams or 3-4 Persian cucumbers sliced into coins (sub english cucumbers half peeled with seeds removed)
  • 20 g or 1.5Tbsp kosher salt
  • 30 grams or 1/3c sliced scallions
  • 10 grams or 3 cloves fresh garlic grated
  • 5 g or 2tsp sesame seeds
  • 10 g or 2 1/2tsp granulated sugar
  • 10 g or 2tsp rice vinegar
  • 7-8 g or 1 1/2tsp sesame oil
  • 25 g or 2Tbsp Gochugaru or substitute with a blend of 20g sweet paprika and 5g chili flakes
  • 225 g or 1c water
  • 75 g or 1/3c rice vinegar
  • 100 g or 1/3c + 1Tbsp white distilled vinegar
  • 40 g or 3Tbsp sugar
  • 4 g or 1t kosher salt
  • 150 grams 1 large carrot, julienned
  • 150 grams ¼ to ½ of 1 daikon radish (or substitute with jicama or white turnip), grated
  • Green leaf lettuce leaves
  • Samjang sub with equal parts miso and sriracha
  • Cooked white rice
  • Sesame seeds for garnish
Instructions
  1. Trim excess fat from the ribeye steak and slice it thinly, about 1/8 inch thick or thinner.
  2. Cut the slices in half into long thin strips and remove any internal hard fat.
  3. In a bowl, combine the beef strips, soy sauce, brown sugar, Mirin, black pepper, grated garlic, sesame oil, honey, and pear pulp. Mix well.
  4. Add the thinly sliced white onion and toss everything to combine.
  5. Marinate the beef in the fridge for about 20 minutes.
  6. Slice the kimchi into thin strips for easy serving
  7. In a bowl, combine the sliced cucumbers and salt, and toss to combine. Let it sit for 10 minutes.
  8. Drain off the excess water from the cucumbers.
  9. Add the sliced scallions, garlic, sesame seeds, sugar, rice vinegar, sesame oil, and Gochugaru. Stir to combine and let it marinate until ready to serve.
  10. In a saucepan, combine water, rice vinegar, white distilled vinegar, sugar, and salt. Bring it to a simmer.
  11. Use a julienne peeler to shred the carrot and grate the daikon radish.
  12. Drop the shredded carrot and grated daikon radish into the simmering brine. Stir and let it sit off the heat for about 10 minutes.
  13. Once cooled, the pickled daikon radish and carrot are ready to serve.
  14. Clean and prepare the green leaf lettuce leaves by removing the top and base.
  15. Wash the lettuce leaves and spin dry.
  16. Cover the lettuce leaves with a damp paper towel until ready to serve.
  17. Heat a non-stick pan or cast iron over medium-high heat.
  18. Once the pan is hot, add the marinated beef. It will steam initially before searing.
  19. Spread the beef evenly in the pan and let it sit undisturbed for at least 2 minutes.
  20. Flip the beef to cook the other side. Continue cooking for another 1-2 minutes, stirring every 30 seconds, until the beef is well browned, the cooking liquid is evaporated, and the onions are softened.
  21. Add the tops of two bunches of scallions (about 20 grams) and toss to wilt them.
  22. Sprinkle the cooked bulgogi with sesame seeds.
  23. To assemble the lettuce wraps, smear some ssamjang on the inside of each lettuce leaf.
  24. Add a scoop of cooked rice, a few ounces of bulgogi, and a couple of the crunchy toppings (kimchi, oi muchim, or pickled daikon radish and carrot).

Roast Beef N Cheddar

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 100 g or 1/2c warm water 86F/30C
  • 6 g or 1 1/2t instant yeast
  • 35 g or 3T sugar
  • 1 egg yolk
  • 35 g or 2 1/2T melted but not hot unsalted butter
  • 75 g or about ½ of a medium mashed riced potato
  • 7 g or 1 1/2t dough conditioner
  • 315 g or 2 1/2c all purpose flour
  • 6 g or 1t salt
  • Egg wash 1 egg whisked with a splash of water
  • 1 medium sweet/vidalia onion
  • Olive oil
  • Salt
  • Sugar
  • 50 g or 1/4c of water
  • Poppy seeds
  • Flaky salt
  • 8 lb/3.5kb top round beef roast
  • Olive oil
  • salt
  • pepper
  • 100 g or 1/2c water
  • 125 g or 1/2c ketchup
  • 15 g or 1T steak sauce
  • 10 g or 1T brown sugar
  • 10 g or 1T hot sauce
  • 15 g or 1T white vinegar
  • 2 g or 1/2t onion powder
  • 2 g or 1/2t garlic powder
  • 2 g or 3/4t black pepper
  • 3 g or 1/2t salt
  • 225 g or 3c medium cheddar cheese shredded
  • 8 g or 2 1/2t corn starch
  • 250 g or 1c evaporated milk
Instructions
  1. Add 100g warm water, yeast, sugar, egg yolk, melted butter, mashed potatoes (I press mine through a potato ricer to eliminate lumps), dough conditioner, flour, and 6g of salt to a stand mixer with dough hook attachment and mix on medium speed for about 8 minutes. When the dough has formed a smooth mass that is clearing the sides of the bowl, it's ready.
  2. Transfer the dough into a bowl and round it into a ball by rounding and tucking it up under itself to create tension across the top as shown in the video @0:54.
  3. Cover the bowl with a lid or plastic wrap and ferment for 90 minutes in a warm environment. To create that “warm environment” I turn my oven on at 350F/175C for about 2 minutes, then turn off the heat, creating a nice warm 90F/32C box where the dough can ferment.
  4. After 90 minutes, the size of the dough should have about doubled. If it hasn’t yet doubled, continue to ferment for a bit longer.
  5. Divide dough into 6 equal pieces that are about 100g each. Degas each piece with your fingertips and shape into balls, using your fingertips and palms while rolling against your work surface to create tension across the top of the balls as shown in the video @1:24.
  6. Place shaped balls onto a parchment lined sheet tray. Cover the tray and proof again at 90F/32C for 45-60 minutes. The individual buns should have doubled in size (if not, keep proofing until they have).
  7. Brush each bun with egg wash and top with a spoonful of the cooled caramelized onions (recipe below), spread as evenly as possible over the top of the bun. Follow the onions with a pinch of poppy seeds and flaky salt.
  8. Bake at 400F/205C for 15-17 min until golden brown, but still soft.
  9. To make the onions to top the bun, very small dice 1 medium vidalia onion.
  10. Add a bit of olive oil into a saute pan over medium heat and add in the onions, a pinch of salt, a pinch of sugar, and the 1/4c or 50g of water. Simmer to soften the onions and once the water has simmered off (about 2-3 min) then continue to saute for another 4-5 minutes or until the onions have taken on some light browning. Deglaze the pan with a few more spoonfuls of water. When the water has evaporated, saute for another 4-5 minutes while stirring constantly. When done, the onions should be a nice golden brown and have almost a sticky consistency. Spread the onions out on a plate to cool.
  11. Preheat oven to 550F/285C (or as high as your oven will go).
  12. Rub olive oil liberally onto all sides of the beef.
  13. Place the roast on a wire rack on a sheet tray. I also like to put a piece of foil underneath the wire rack to make clean up easier.
  14. Very liberally salt and pepper all sides of the roast.
  15. Load the roast into your hot oven to “sear” the outside for 15-20 minutes (use convection if you have it). Reduce the oven temp to 275F/135C and after about 10 minutes or so when the oven temp has cooled to 275F/135C, load the roast back into the oven to roast for 2 hours.
  16. After 90 min to 2 hours, check the internal temperature of the meat with a meat thermometer. You’re looking for around 130F/55C.
  17. Remove beef from the oven and allow it to rest for at least 30 minutes before slicing.
  18. Mix with an immersion blender
  19. Add corn starch to cheddar and mix well to coat. Use the largest holes on a box grater to grate your own. Packaged pre-shredded cheeses often include anti-caking agents that make them melt in a weird way.
  20. Add the cheese corn starch mixture into a medium sized saute pan then add in evaporated milk. Heat over low heat and stir frequently to melt for about 5 minutes.
  21. When mixture reaches a low simmer it should start to thicken. Be sure to scrape the bottom of the pan well with a spatula or wooden spoon to prevent scorching. If the mixture gets too thick, stir in a splash more evaporated milk.
  22. Slice an onion roll across, and toast in a well buttered pan until toasty and golden brown.
  23. Slicing the meat: If you don’t have access to a meat slicer, you can use a “slicer” knife to slice the meat as thinly as possible. We’re going for shaved pieces here. Thicker pieces will give you a more steaky, chewy texture and that’s not what we’re going for. If you DO have a meat slicer, use that to shave the beef very thin.
  24. Add a generous amount of tangy sauce to the bottom bun, followed by a handful of your shaved beef slices. Top with a few large spoonfuls of the cheese sauce and the toasted top bun. Squish and enjoy.

80m

Roman Gnocchi

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 1200 g whole milk
  • 115 g butter 1 stick
  • 12 g salt
  • 275 g semolina flour
  • 125 g parmesan grated
  • 5 egg yolks
  • 1/4 tsp nutmeg less than 1g
  • FRESH TOMATO SAUCE:
  • 3 lbs in-season tomatoes 5-8 med-lrg, cored and skin removed
  • 75 g olive oil
  • 150 g yellow onion 1 medium, small diced
  • 25 g sliced garlic about 5 cloves
  • 25 g calabrian chilis chopped
  • Salt
  • 20 g tomato paste
Instructions
  1. Combine milk, butter, and salt in a large heavy-bottomed pot and bring to simmer over low heat. While whisking, add semolina flour. When semolina is thick and has absorbed all of the liquid, continue cooking for another 15 minutes over low heat, stirring every 5 minutes.
  2. Off heat, stir in parm, egg yolks, and nutmeg. Spread doughy mixture evenly onto an oiled half sheet tray. Use a second sheet tray (oiled on bottom) to press down on the mixture to flatten and even it out. Refrigerate for 30 min.
  3. Cut chilled gnocchi into squares or use a large ring mold to cut out circles.
  4. Heat a nonstick saute pan over medium-high. Add squeeze of olive oil followed by gnocchi squares or rounds. Fry on each side for about 2 minutes or until golden and crisp.
  5. FRESH TOMATO SAUCE:
  6. Dice tomatoes.Strain off tomato water and reserve for later.
  7. Preheat large heavy bottom pot over medium. Add olive oil, onions, garlic, chilis, and a large pinch of salt. Stir to combine. Reduce heat to low and gently fry for 15min.
  8. Add in tomatoes. If mixture is too dry, add a splash of the tomato water. Stir and bring to a simmer over medium and cook for 15 minutes until thickened.
  9. Add tomato paste, stir in and cook for another 5 minutes. Taste for seasoning and adjust with additional salt if needed.

Rueben with Marble Rye

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 175 g salt
  • 50 g sugar
  • 15 g pink salt not himalayan
  • 10 g black peppercorns
  • 5-6 dried bay leaves
  • 4-5 whole cloves
  • 10 g mustard seed
  • 4 garlic cloves smashed
  • 1000 g cold water mL
  • 8-10 lb beef brisket 4kg
  • 1000 g ice cubes
  • 275 g warm water
  • 4 g instant yeast
  • 15 g or 2 1/2tsp salt
  • 330 g bread flour
  • 100 g dark rye flour
  • 300 g poolish or sourdough starter see below for poolish recipe if using poolish
  • 15 g caraway seeds
  • 15 g molasses
  • 10 g cocoa powder
  • 150 g water
  • Pinch instant yeast
  • 150 g bread flour
  • 1 egg yolk
  • 20 g red onion diced and rinsed
  • 5 g or ½ lrg clove garlic
  • 30 g bread and butter pickles
  • 5 g salt
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 50 g ketchup
  • 20 g sriracha
  • 275 g neutral oil avocado, light olive, canola, etc
  • Swiss cheese slices
  • Sauerkraut i recommend bubbies brand or another simple, naturally fermented brand
Instructions
  1. Trim excess fat from beef and remove any silver skin. Cut in half.
  2. Into a large (4qt) pot, combine all ingredients except beef and ice and bring to boil.
  3. Remove from heat and allow to infuse for another 20min. Add ice. When melted, pour into a deep 6qt container, then submerge beef and refrigerate to cure for 10 days.
  4. After the 10-day cure, rinse brine from beef.
  5. Place in cooking vessel (either slow cooker or dutch oven), add water to just cover. If using dutch oven, bring to simmer on the stove, then cover and cook at 250F/120C for 4-5hrs. If using slow cooker, cook for 4-5hrs.
  6. Add water, yeast, flours, and poolish or starter to stand mixer & mix on low with dough hook for 3min or until combined. Increase to high and continue mixing for 4min. When dough has fully come together and is slapping around the bowl, add caraway. Mix for 30sec on medium to incorporate.
  7. Remove 60% of dough and load into a well oiled container and cover. Add molasses, and cocoa to the dough that’s still in the mixer bowl and mix on low for 30 seconds then increase speed to high for another 30 seconds or until well incorporated. Place into a second well oiled container and cover. Allow both doughs to ferment at room temp for 30min.
  8. Perform strength-building fold to both doughs as shown in video @5:07. Cover again and allow to rise at room temp for 90min.
  9. Prepare a 1lb loaf pan with oil or pan spray.
  10. Flip both doughs onto a floured work surface. Degas each piece of dough into rough rectangles. Place brown dough on top of lighter dough and press to seal together. Roll into a loaf shape as shown @8:17 and place in loaf pan, cover, and proof at room temp for 60-90min.
  11. Score top of loaf and place preheated 460F/235C oven. Spray loaf and oven well with water, and bake for 15 min.
  12. Reduce heat to 400F/205C and continue to bake for an additional 20min.
  13. Stir, cover, and allow to ferment for 12-24 hours.
  14. Blend all ingredients except oil until broken down, then with blender running slowly stream in oil until the oil is well emulsified.
  15. Onto a slice of marble rye, layer a slice of swiss followed by beef (cut to desired thickness), and generous portion of kraut.
  16. Spoon & spread russian dressing on the second slice of bread. Lightly toast sandwich to melt cheese.
  17. How to Make SOURDOUGH BREAD WITH SEEDS (My New Favorite Loaf)
  18. CAST IRON FOCACCIA 3 Ways
  19. CIABATTA (Crispy Crust✔️ Silky Open Crumb✔️ Biga ✔️)

Schnitzel and Spaetzle Feast

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 1 lb or .5 pork tenderloins
  • Salt
  • 3-4 c or 400-500g all purpose flour
  • 6 eggs 1 per cutlet, whisked
  • 6 c or 750-1000g panko breadcrumbs broken down into a finer texture in a food processor
  • Canola oil or vegetable, grapeseed, avocado oil
  • 350 g or 1.5c milk
  • 1 egg + 4 egg yolks
  • 7 g or 1teas salt
  • 450 g or 3 1/3c all purpose flour
  • Butter
  • 500 g or 1 medium head purple cabbage sliced thin into long strips
  • 100 g or half of a white or red onion sliced thin
  • 400 g or 1 2/3c white vinegar
  • 400 g or 1 2/3c water
  • 80 g or 1/4c sugar
  • 8 g or 1 1/4teas salt
  • 50 g or 4Tbsp unsalted butter
  • 50 g shallot finely minced
  • Salt
  • 15 g or 2Tbsp all purpose flour
  • 125 g or 1/2c white wine or sherry
  • 250 g or 1c chicken stock
  • 100 g or 1/2c heavy cream
  • 75 g or 1/3c whole grain mustard
  • 50 g or 1/3c capers chopped
Instructions
  1. Cut tenderloins into thirds (about 175g/6oz each).
  2. Place each piece in a plastic bag and pound until flat and about ⅛-¼”/.3-.6cm thick with a heavy pot or meat mallet.
  3. Salt each piece of meat on both sides.
  4. To set up breading station, you’ll need 3 bowls: Place flour in one, whisked eggs in the sedond, and breadcrumbs + a pinch of salt in the third.
  5. One at a time, coat pork cutlets well in the AP flour, shake off excess then transfer to the eggs to coat. Drain off excess then transfer to breadcrumbs, coating well.
  6. Add 3”/7-8cm oil to a large heavy bottomed pot over medium heat until temperature reaches 350-360F/175-180C.
  7. Fry breaded cutlets one at a time, cooking for 3 minutes total, flipping halfway through. When done, iternal temp should read 150F/65C.
  8. Transfer to a wire rack lined sheet tray in warm oven (at lowest setting) to keep warm while you cook the remaining schnitzel.
  9. Bring a medium sized pot of water filled about halfway to a boil. Pot should be small enough that a metal colander can sit on top.
  10. Mix milk, egg, and salt until combined. Add flour and mix until a thick but wet, sticky paste forms. Place a metal colander on top of pot of boiling water and spoon in about a cup of the spaetzle batter. Use a dough scraper to push batter through holes of colander and into the boiling water.
  11. Boil spaetzle for 45-60 seconds then transfer to a tray or plate and drizzle with olive oil to prevent sticking.
  12. Continue to cook remaining spaetzle batter in batches.
  13. When finished boiling spaetzle, add about a Tbsp/15g butter to a large fry pan over high heat, then in batches (of 3-4) add spaetzle and a pinch of salt. Toss to coat then fry without moving for 2-3 minutes until warmed through and beginning to brown.
  14. Add vinegar, water, salt, and sugar to a sauce pan over high and bring to a boil. Add onions and cabbage and push down to fully submerge.
  15. 30sec-1min later when veggies have softened, remove from heat, cover with a lid and allow to cool and pickle for 15 minutes.
  16. Transfer to a container and refrigerate until ready to serve.
  17. Add butter, minced shallot, and a pinch of salt to small sauce pan over med-high. Saute until shallots are softened and beginning to take on color or about 30 seconds.
  18. Add flour, stir and fry for 30 seconds to 1 minute.
  19. Add wine.
  20. When mixture is thickened, add stock and bring to a simmer.
  21. Add cream, mustard, capers, and a pinch of salt. Stir to combine, bring to simmer and reduce by half, about 15 minutes.

Sheet Pan Chicken and Vegetables

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 6 skin-on bone-in chicken thighs
  • 3 large russet potatoes peeled & cut into chunks
  • 40 g olive oil 3T
  • 20 g salt 1T+1t
  • 2 g black pepper 1t
  • 1-2 g chili flake (crushed red pepper) 1t
  • 5 g garlic powder 1t
  • 2 g dried thyme 1.5t
  • 2 g dried oregano 1t
  • 3 medium zucchinis quartered & deseeded (450g/1lb total)
  • 250 g 1 medium onion, roughly chopped
  • 25 g 5-6 cloves garlic, smashed
  • 1 can 28 oz/800g whole peeled tomatoes, chopped
  • 20 g tbsp olive oil 1.5
  • 100 g Greek olives 3/4c
  • 2 g dried oregano 1t
  • 2 g black pepper 1t
  • 10 g salt 2t
  • 200 g feta cheese, crumbled 7oz
Instructions
  1. Preheat oven to 450°F (232°C).
  2. In a mixing bowl, combine chicken and potato chunks. Add olive, salt, pepper, chili flake, garlic powder, thyme, and oregano. Toss to coat.
  3. Arrange chicken and potatoes on a sheet tray (potatoes cut side down).
  4. Roast for about 40 minutes. While the chicken roasts, prep the veggie bake. In a large bowl, combine veggie bake ingredients, careful to not crumble too much. Spread the mixture on a second sheet tray and roast in the oven for approx 30 minutes.
  5. Check the chicken and potatoes after 40 mins of baking.. The chicken skin should be crispy, and the potatoes golden brown.

Sheet Pan Sweet Potato Aloo Gobi

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2 large sweet potatoes peeled and cubed (750g/1.5lb)
  • 1 head cauliflower cut into 1” florets
  • 1 large onion roughly chopped
  • 1 large poblano pepper small diced
  • 25 g 5-6 cloves garlic, smashed
  • 15 g tomato paste 1T
  • 5 g ground cumin 1 3/4t
  • 5 g garam masala 2t
  • 15 g curry powder 2T
  • 15 g salt 1T
  • 20 g olive oil 1 1/2T
  • 1 15 oz can garbanzo beans 430g, drained
  • 2 small cans coconut cream plus 2 cans of water, about 250 grams
  • Cilantro
  • Lime juice
Instructions
  1. Preheat oven and sheet tray to 450°F (230 °C).
  2. In a large mixing bowl, combine potatoes, cauliflower, onion, poblano, garlic, tomato paste, cumin, garam masala, curry powder, salt, olive oil, beans, & 1 can of coconut cream plus 2 coconut cream size cans of water.
  3. Toss to combine & spread onto preheated sheet tray. Roast for 35-45 minutes, checking for tenderness.
  4. After roasting, add 3-4 dollops of additional coconut cream, chopped cilantro, and lime juice to taste.
  5. Serve over rice.

Shepherd’s Pie

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2.5 lb peeled russet potatoes 1200g
  • 350 g or about 2med onions chopped
  • 200 g or 2-3 large carrots medium diced
  • 200 g or 3-4 ribs celery medium diced
  • 200 g or about ½ bulb fennel medium diced
  • 20 g or 4-5 cloves garlic chopped
  • 40 g or 3Tbsp olive oil
  • 3 lbs ground lamb 1.5kg , sub lean beef if you can't find lamb
  • salt
  • 250 g or 1c dry red wine pinot noir, chianti, cab, etc…
  • 50 g or 3 ½ Tbsp tomato paste
  • 50 g or 2 ¾ Tbsp beef paste better than bouillon if you can find it
  • 50 g or 3 ¾ Tbsp worcestershire
  • 2 g or 1t black pepper
  • 2 g or 2t fresh thyme chopped
  • 25 g or 3 ½ Tbsp all purpose flour sub gf flour or corn starch
  • 450 g or 1 3/4c beef stock sub chicken
  • 15 g or 1/4c parsley chopped
  • 150 g or 1 1/4c frozen peas
  • 115 g unsalted butter 1 stick, melted
  • 125 g or 3/4c heavy cream
  • 125 g or 1/2c sour cream
  • 125 g or 1c grated parmesan
  • 3 egg yolks
Instructions
  1. Add potatoes to a stock pot, cover with water and bring to a boil. Once boiling, reduce heat to medium low and cover. Cook for 30-40min or until a knife or cake tester doesn’t meet resistance when inserted.
  2. Drain and pass through a potato ricer. Stir in melted butter, cream, sour cream, parm, and two very large pinches of salt. Taste for seasoning. Add more salt if needed. Stir in egg yolks.
  3. Click here for my full recipe for creamy potato puree.
  4. Toss all cut veggies together in a bowl.
  5. Heat a large heavy bottomed pot over high. When hot, add olive oil and ground lamb and break apart while cooking. It should take about 5 minutes for the water to cook off then 5-10 more minutes to brown meat.
  6. When meat has begun to take on color and brown fond is forming in the bottom of the pot, add veggies and 1Tbsp 15g of salt. Stir to combine and cook for 3-4 more min until veg has begun to soften.
  7. Add wine to deglaze pot, scraping up fond on the bottom. Stir in tomato paste, bouillon, worcestershire, and black pepper.
  8. Once wine is mostly reduced, stir in thyme and flour. Cook until bottom of the pot glazes up with fond again, then add stock. Bring to a simmer then cover and lower heat to medium low to simmer until sauce is reduced by half and veggies are tender, about 10 min.
  9. Taste for seasoning and adjust with salt if needed.
  10. Off heat, stir in parsley and peas.
  11. Scoop filling into a 9″x13″ (23cm x 33cm) baking dish. Press into dish and smooth to create an even layer.
  12. Spoon mashed potato mixture over the top and spread into even layer. Use a spoon or knife to create a rough texture on top of the potatoes. You’re creating texture that will brown and get crisp here. Feel free to be creative.
  13. Top with parmesan cheese.
  14. Place baking dish on a sheet tray and bake in a preheated 425F/220C oven for 25min.
  15. After 25 min, move dish under a high broiler for a minute or so until top is golden and crusty – keep an eye during broiling because potatoes can burn quickly.

Shredded Beef Enchiladas

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 3-4 lb or 1.5-2kg Chuck roast cut into large chunks
  • 950 g or 32oz stock
  • 150 g or 1 white onion med-small diced
  • 150 g or 1 Poblano pepper
  • 5-6 cloves garlic minced or pressed
  • Oil
  • Salt
  • 5 g or 1 3/4tsp chili powder
  • 5 g or 2.5tsp cumin
  • 6 g or 1Tbsp black pepper
  • 2 g or 2tsp dried oregano
  • lime
  • Olive oil
  • 75 g or 2/3c chili powder
  • 50 g or 1/2c smoked paprika sweet works too
  • 1 qt or ltr beef cooking liquid from recipe above
  • 450 g or 2 8oz cans tomato sauce
  • 15 g or 1 1/4Tbsp brown sugar
  • 25 g or 1 3/4Tbsp apple cider vinegar
  • 5 g or 3/4tsp salt
  • 350 g or 12oz medium-sharp cheddar grated
  • 350 g or 12oz pepperjack grated
  • 12 corn tortillas + a few extras just in case
  • 200 g or 3/4c sour cream
  • 50 g or 1/4c milk
  • Zest of 1 lime + juice of ½ lime
  • Pinch of salt
Instructions
  1. Cut roast into large chunks and salt both sides.
  2. Sear in preheated, oiled heavy bottomed pan over high heat. Flip chunks over after about 3 minutes or when the first side is becoming nicely browned. Continue to cook for another 4-5 minutes on side 2.
  3. Transfer beef chunks and any pan fond into pressure cooker, add stock and cook on high for 90 minutes and allow pressure to release (another 30 min or so).
  4. Remove beef from cooker and transfer cooking liquid into a container – skim and discard as much fat as possible off of cooking liquid. Reserve liquid for later – there should be about 1qt/ltr left over.
  5. Preheat large saute and add a few glugs of oil, then diced onions, poblano, minced garlic, and a pinch of salt.
  6. When veg is softened, add in cooked beef and break down slightly to shred, leaving plenty of chunks. When beef has cooked for a few minutes and has begun to take on color, add chili powder, cumin, black pepper, oregano, and the juice from a lime. Stir and allow spices to bloom. Fry for about another minute or so. Season with a pinch of salt and remove from heat.
  7. Preheat large heavy bottomed pot over medium. Add a few glugs of olive oil followed by chili powder and paprika. Stir & allow to toast until fragrant.
  8. Add ⅓ of the beef cooking liquid. Stir. Add in another third of the liquid and stir again. Once incorporated, add in most of the rest of your liquid. Save a splash in case you need to thin the sauce later.
  9. Once the liquid is incorporated & smooth, add in tomato paste, brown sugar, cider vinegar, and salt. Stir & bring to a simmer then reduce heat to med-low & cook to reduce sauce for 15-20 minutes. When ready, it should have reduced by about 30%.
  10. Remove from heat and taste for seasoning.
  11. Grate cheeses and mix them together. Wet each tortilla slightly with water then heat in a skillet over medium until soft and pliable. Place warmed tortillas in a tortilla warmer (or thick container with lid to trap steam if you don’t have one) until you’re ready to assemble the enchiladas.
  12. Place each warmed tortilla into sauce to coat both sides then transfer to a bowl, shaking off excess sauce as you go. When all tortillas are coated Spread enough roja sauce into a 9”x13”/22x33cm baking pan to lightly coat the bottom (about ¾-1c). Place 3-4oz of the meat in a line on a sauced tortilla followed by a pinch of the cheese blend. Roll tightly then place in one corner of the baking dish, seam side down. Continue filling and rolling the remaining 11 tortillas, placing each snugly against the last. If you’re using a 9”x13” pan, the last 4 can be placed perpendicular to the first 8 as shown in video @10:14.
  13. Finish by spreading 5-6 more spoonfuls of the sauce over the enchiladas and top with cheese. Bake at 375F/190C oven for about 20 minutes until cheese is well melted. Plate up and top with a drizzle of lime crema.
  14. Stir to combine.

Sloppy Joseph

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 1 lb 80/20 ground beef .5kg
  • 1 g or 1/4tsp baking soda
  • 100 g or 1 large or 2 small poblano peppers small diced
  • 150 g or 1 large yellow or white onion small diced
  • Olive oil
  • 5 g or 1tsp salt
  • 5 g or 1 1/2tsp onion powder
  • 5 g or 1tsp garlic powder
  • 2 g or 1tsp black pepper
  • 5 g or 1 3/4tsp paprika
  • Pinch of Chile flakes
  • 10 g or 1 1/2Tbsp flour all purpose or gluten free blend
  • 50 g or 3Tbsp tomato paste
  • 50 g or 3Tbsp ketchup
  • 15 g or 2Tbsp yellow mustard
  • 15 g or 2 1/2-3tsp brown sugar
  • 50 g or 3 1/2Tbsp worcestershire
  • 20 g or 1 1/2Tbsp red wine vinegar
  • 10 g or 1 ½tsp better than bouillon beef paste
  • 350 g or 1 1/2c stock chicken or beef
Instructions
  1. Add beef to a bowl with baking soda and mix thoroughly to incorporate.
  2. Preheat a large nonstick pan over medium high then add a squeeze (Tbsp or so) of olive oil followed by diced onions, poblanos, and a pinch of salt. Saute for 3-4 mins, stirring often.
  3. When veg has softened a bit and has started to brown, add in beef/baking soda mix, using a meat musher or wooden spoon to break up meat into small pieces.
  4. When beef is broken down well and is beginning to brown, add salt (5g), onion powder, garlic powder, pepper, paprika, and chile flake. Stir to combine and cook for about 30 seconds.
  5. Add flour and stir to combine and cook for a few seconds.
  6. Add tomato products, mustard, brown sugar, worchestershire, vinegar, bouillon, and stock. Stir to combine. Bring to a simmer then reduce heat to medium low to reduce for about 10-12 minutes, continuing to stir and break up meat as sauce reduces.
  7. Taste for seasoning and add salt if needed.
  8. Serve on toasted brioche bun.

Smash Burgers

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 125 g or 1/2 c mayo
  • 15 g or about 3Tbsp chopped onion
  • 60 g or 1/4 c ketchup
  • 25 g or 1 2/3 Tbsp sriracha
  • 15 g or 1 Tbsp mustard
  • 60 g or about 1/2 cup bread and butt pickles
  • 1/2 clove garlic sliced
  • 2 g or 1/2 teas salt and 2g or 3/4 teas pepper
  • Spin sauce ingredients up with an immersion blender and refrigerate until ready to use
  • 1 lb boneless short rib, cut into 1"x1" pieces and frozen for 15-20min
  • burger sauce recipe above
  • green leaf lettuce
  • shallot thinly sliced and rinsed
  • dill pickle sliced (i recommend claussen)
  • kraft singles. don’t @ me.
Instructions
  1. Load 1/2 of the 15-20min frozen beef chunks into a food processor and process on high for approx 20 seconds.
  2. Place on tray or in bowl and process the other half of meat. once finished, toss both rounds of ground meat gently to combine
  3. Measure meat out into six 3oz portions. gently squeeze each into a ball, put between 2 pieces of parchment, and smash down with a flat bottomed pot. When finished smashing all 6 balls, refrigerate while you prep your toppings
  4. preheat cast iron skillet on high
  5. using a pad of butter, toast your burger buns in skillet until golden brown
  6. preheat 2 tbs of high smoke point neutral oil (canola, grapeseed, avocado) in the cast iron
  7. Season one side of patties with salt and pepper and place s&p side down in cast iron. Now season the side facing up with salt and pepper then press down with a spatula. cook on side 1 for about 90 seconds total, then flip.
  8. add the cheese to each patty, and cook on 2nd side for another 30 sec. once finished, assemble your burgers, 2 patties per burger

Smoked Beef (Easier Than Brisket)

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2 lbs hickory wood 1kg or other hard wood chips, soaked in water
  • 10 lb Beef Chuck/Shoulder 4.5kg
  • Salt
  • pepper
  • Squishy toasted bun
  • BBQ sauce
  • Creamy slaw
Instructions
  1. If using your grill as a smoker, set the far left burner to medium low, remove grill grate, and place the smoker box with with soaked chips directly onto the flavorizer bars. If your grill doesn’t have these, put the smoker box directly on the grill grates over the heat. Close the grill lid and allow to pre-heat for about 20 minutes.
  2. Coat all sides of meat in kosher salt and pepper and load them onto the right side of the grill (no heat under them). Add more chips to the smoker box, close the grill lid, and lower the heat under the chips to low. You’ll need to shoot to keep the internal temp of the grill at 250-275F/120-135C for the next 3-4 hours. Check the grill and top off the wood chips about every 30 minutes to keep the smoke rolling. You want the chips to be smoking, not burning.
  3. After the first 3 rounds of chips, or 90 minutes-2 hours of smoking, flip the beefs to the second side. Continue to smoke and change out chips every 30 minutes.
  4. After about 4 hours of total smoke time, or when the internal temp reaches 145-150F/65C remove from smoker. Wrap pieces of meat well in foil to seal (i use 3 layers) and return to the grill. Remove the smoker box and turn down heat as low as it will go. You’ll want to keep the internal temp of the grill/smoker at 225F/110C for the next 3-4 hours to finish. Internal temp should read 205-210F/96-99C at this point.
  5. To set the outside of the beef and get a crusty bark on the outside, unwrap the beefs (saving the drippings) and cook in a 225F/110C oven for 30 min. Tent with foil and allow to rest for at least 20 minutes before cutting.
  6. Cut beef into cubes and toss with enough the reserved beef drippings to moisten.
  7. Load onto toasted bun.
  8. Top with bbq sauce of your choice and creamy slaw
  9. This Burger Bun Took Me 10 Years to Figure Out
  10. BBQ SIDES 3 Ways

Spaghetti and Meatballs

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 4 lrg eggs
  • 250 g or 2c ap flour
  • 210 g or 1 1/4c semolina flour
  • 5 g or 1tsp salt
  • 20 g or 1 1/4Tbsp water
  • 250 g or 1c buttermilk
  • 1 egg
  • 150 g or about 4.5c bread cubed (i find that regular white bread works well)
  • 125 g or 1 1/2c parmesan grated
  • 15 g or 1/3c parsley chopped
  • 15 g or 2Tbsp garlic minced
  • 18 g or 1Tbsp salt
  • 3 g or 2tsp black pepper
  • 15 g or 1Tbsp lemon juice
  • Pinch chile flake
  • 450 g or 1lb 80/20 ground beef
  • 450 g or 1lb 80/20 ground pork
  • 100 g or about 1/2c olive oil
  • 150 g or 1.5c yellow onion small diced
  • 15 g or 2Tbsp garlic minced
  • Salt
  • Chile flake
  • 1 28 oz can whole tomatoes pureed until broken down
  • 1 can crushed tomatoes 28 oz
  • 5 g or 1 1/4tsp sugar
Instructions
  1. Add eggs, flour, semolina, and salt to mixer with paddle attachment. Mix on low for 60sec until dough begins to hydrate then add in water if needed.
  2. When dough is coming together into a mass, switch to dough hook and continue to mix on low for about 2 more mins.
  3. Flip dough onto work surface and continue to knead by hand for about 60 seconds or until it’s formed into a ball. Place in the fridge covered for about 30min.
  4. Remove dough from fridge and pinch off small pieces (about the size of a large walnut). Warm slightly and roll into a ball then drop into extruder (since im using my kitchen aid, the mixer is running on high speed).
  5. Cut at desired length then place on a floured tray and dust with flour.
  6. Cook in well salted boiling water for 3-4 min (or per package instructions if using dried pasta).
  7. Reserve some pasta water to finish pasta.
  8. Add buttermilk, egg, and bread into a bowl and mix to hydrate.
  9. Add parm, parsley, garlic, salt, pepper, lemon juice, chile flake, and meats. Mix together until just combined. Try not to over mix.
  10. Divide meat into 15 100-105g pieces, roll into balls, and place on a parchment lined sheet tray.
  11. Bake at 475F/250C for 20 min until beginning to brown. Allow to rest for 10 min.
  12. Reduce oven heat to 325F/160C.
  13. Heat large pot or dutch oven over medium.
  14. Add very long squeeze olive oil, onion, garlic, and generous pinch of salt. Stir to combine and sweat over medium for 10min or until onions are translucent and softened.
  15. Add pinch of chile flake, stir. Add in both cans of tomatoes. Stir, reduce heat to medium low, cover and simmer for about 15min.
  16. Taste for seasoning and add salt and 5g sugar if needed. Stir.
  17. Gently lower cooked meatballs into the sauce and stir to coat. Return pot of sauce/meatballs into 325F/160C oven and cook for 20-30min.
  18. When finished, the sauce should be nicely concentrated with browned edges.
  19. Add portion of pasta into a saute pan over low heat with 2 small pads of butter and a spoonful of pasta water.
  20. Swirl until combined and emulsify.
  21. Add pasta to plate, top with 2 meatballs, sauce, grated parm, chopped parsley, and a drizzle of nice olive oil.

Stuffed Crust Pizza

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 270 g warm water 86F/30C
  • 5 g instant yeast
  • 35 g sugar
  • 30 g olive oil
  • 450 g AP flour 11.7% protein
  • 12 g kosher salt
  • 150 g tomato paste
  • 2 g kosher salt
  • 10 g sugar
  • 1 g black pepper
  • 1 pinch chili flake
  • 1 g garlic powder
  • 1 g onion powder
  • 1 g dried basil
  • 1 g dried oregano
  • 10 g olive oil
  • 150 g water
  • 150 g full fat mozzarella shredded
  • 50 g provolone shredded
  • 340 g provolone sliced into 2″/5cm long batons
  • pepperonis thinly sliced
  • 115 g unsalted butter
  • 5-6 smashed garlic cloves
Instructions
  1. Add ingredients to stand mixer with dough hook and mix on low for 3 minutes or until a dough ball forms. Increase speed to high and continue to mix for 3 more minutes or until dough is slapping around the bowl and doesn’t tear when you tug it.
  2. Place dough in a bowl, cover, and allow to ferment at room temp for 30min.
  3. Perform a strength building fold. Cover and allow to rise at room temp for another 30min.
  4. Divide dough into 2 equal (approx 375g) pieces. De-gas dough well by pressing it down then shape into balls.
  5. Place each dough ball onto a well oiled tray, cover, and refrigerate for 4-48 hours.
  6. Use an immersion blender to spin all ingredients until broken down and combined.
  7. Pull out dough to come to room temp about for about 90min.
  8. Preheat oven to 550F or highest setting with pizza steel on the bottom 3rd of oven.
  9. To make garlic butter, melt butter and garlic together over low heat and allow to infuse for about 1min. Allow to sit off heat while you shape the pizza.
  10. Dust dough ball with finely ground cornmeal then place seam side down onto a cornmeal dusted work surface. De-gas dough ball again, then roll out dough into about a 14"/35cm diameter round.
  11. Place provolone batons near the edge, all around the outside of the dough. Fold the dough to cover the cheese all around the edge of the pizza. Be sure to seal the dough well to prevent cheese from escaping.
  12. Shred and combine the 150g mozzarella and 50g of the provolone.
  13. Scoot the shaped dough onto a pizza peel (well-dusted with cornmeal or semolina) then sauce the pizza with about 4 big spoonfuls of sauce spread edge to edge. Use more than you would put on a typical pizza.
  14. Sprinkle with about 1c or 100g total of the shredded cheese blend. Top with thin sliced pepperonis (or toppings of your choice).
  15. Bake on pizza steel for about 6-7min, keeping an eye on the bottom for burning since this crust has a higher sugar content.
  16. Brush crust with garlic butter.

Swedish Meatballs with Cranberry Jelly, Mashed Potatoes and Gravy

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 150 g or 1 large white onion small diced
  • Olive oil
  • salt
  • 200 g or 3/4c+1Tbsp buttermilk
  • 1 egg
  • 200 g or 5 slices soft white bread cut into cubes
  • 1 lb or .5kg 90/10 ground beef
  • 1 lb or .5kg 90/10 ground pork
  • 15 g or 3 cloves garlic minced
  • 18 g or 1Tbsp salt
  • 1 g or 3/4tsp ground allspice
  • 3 g or 1.5tsp white pepper or black pepper
  • 1-2 g or 1/2tsp nutmeg
  • 340 g or 12oz cranberries
  • 100 g or 1/4c water
  • 125 g or 1/2c granulated sugar
  • 3 g or 1/2tsp salt
  • 115 g or 1 stick unsalted butter
  • 75 g or 1/2c all purpose flour
  • 100 g or 1/2c dry white wine
  • 1 L or 1 box beef stock whisked with 14g gelatin and a spoon/15-20g better than bouillon or nice homemade beef stock
  • 15 g or 3tsp dijon mustard
  • 25 g or 1.5 worcestershire
  • 100 g or 1/2c heavy cream
  • 1000 g or 5-6 medium yukon gold potatoes, rough chopped
  • Water to cover
  • 1 stick or 115g butter
  • Salt
Instructions
  1. Add a squeeze of olive oil followed by onions and a pinch of salt into a preheated pan over medium heat. Saute for 4-5 minutes or until softened.
  2. Transfer to a bowl and add buttermilk, egg, bread, beef, pork, garlic, salt, allspice, pepper, and nutmeg. Mix until well combined.
  3. Divide and roll into 24 2oz/60g meatballs.
  4. Place meatballs on a parchment lined sheet tray and seer into 500F/260C oven for 20-25 minutes. Flip meatballs over and return to oven for 5-8 minutes to brown on the bottom.
  5. Add ingredients to a small saucepan and heat over medium high.
  6. When boiling, stir and cook down for 10-15min.
  7. Add butter into heavy-bottomed pot over medium.
  8. When butter is melted, add flour and stir until combined and no lumps remain, about 30 seconds. Continue to cook for about 30 more seconds.
  9. Add wine and stir. Cook for 30 seconds while stirring.
  10. Add beef stock and cook for 1-2 minutes until boiling and thickened.
  11. Add dijon, worcestershire, and cream and stir. Reduce heat to low and simmer for 10 minutes.
  12. Taste for seasoning and adjust with salt if needed.
  13. Bring potatoes and water to a boil and cook until tender, about 15-20min.
  14. When done, drain off water, add butter and stir. I like to keep mine a little chunky for this dish.
  15. Add salt to season.

Taco Pizza

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 900 g 90/10 ground beef
  • kosher salt
  • 150 g onion 1 small onion
  • 15 g garlic minced, 5-6 cloves
  • 25 g chili powder
  • 15 g cumin
  • 10 g paprika
  • 2 g cayenne
  • 2 g oregano
  • 425 g tomato sauce
  • 400 g chicken stock
  • 10 g brown sugar
  • 10 g cider vinegar
  • 175 g all purpose flour
  • 5 g baking powder
  • 3 g instant yeast
  • 3 g kosher salt
  • 50 g instant masa flour
  • 35 g olive oil
  • 140 g warm water 86℉/30℃
  • 425 g tomato sauce
  • 20 g tomato paste
  • 40 g chipotle chiles in adobo about 2 chiles
  • 10 g chile powder
  • 2 g cumin
  • 1 g garlic powder
  • 1 g onion powder
  • 1 g oregano
  • 15 g brown sugar
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • block of mild cheddar shredded
  • block of pepper jack shredded
  • fresh tomato medium diced
  • green onions middle section only, diced
  • pickled jalapenos diced
  • sliced black olives
Instructions
  1. Heat a large saute pan over medium-high. Add in the ground beef and break apart as much as possible. Sprinkle a large pinch of salt over the meat and continue to it break apart as it browns for 5-6min.
  2. Stir onion and garlic and continue to cook with the beef until it has browned and onions are becoming translucent.
  3. Add in the spices and herbs. Cook until fragrant, about 1-2 minutes.
  4. Pour in tomato sauce, stock, brown sugar, vinegar, and pinch of salt. Stir together and bring to a spirited simmer, then reduce heat to low. Continue to cook for about 20 minutes or until the liquid has reduced and there's no excess liquid left.
  5. Taste for seasoning. Add a bit more salt, brown, sugar and cider vinegar if desired.
  6. Combine the dry ingredients and olive oil into a food processor and spin, streaming in the water until it is incorporated and begins to form a ball.
  7. Transfer to work surface and knead by hand for about 2 minutes. You can also mix by hand or with a mixer if you don't have a blender.
  8. Cut dough ball into 2 equal pieces (about 200g each). Roll each portion of dough into a ball by folding all its sides into the center and the flipping the dough over onto the counter so that the seam side is down. Then using the side of your hand against the table, push the dough along the table to create surface tension on the ball of dough. When you have two nice balls of dough, cover and allow to proof at room temperature for about 30 minutes.
  9. After 30 minutes it is time to roll out the dough. On your table, set down a piece of parchment paper and spray it down with pan spray. Next, place your down ball in the center along with another piece of parchment paper over top of that. Take a sheet tray or a pizza pan and use it to press down onto the ball of dough until it is about ½ inch thick. Using a rolling pin, finish rolling the dough out into roughly an 11-12 inch (28-30cm) circle.
  10. Add ingredients to a high sided container and spin with an immersion blender until incorporated.
  11. Preheat oven to 550℉/290℃
  12. Spread 3 spoonfuls of sauce onto the crust, edge to edge. Follow with a generous amount of the shredded cheeses, the ground beef taco meat, black olives, green onions, diced tomatoes, and chopped jalapenos.
  13. Load pizza (with parchment underneath unless you're a complete pizza jedi) onto a preheated oven pizza steel or stone for 6-8 minutes. Remove the parchment from pizza after about 6 minutes and allow to cook directly on steel for another 1-2 minutes.
  14. Remove pizza from the oven and allow to cool on a wire rack for 2-5 minutes. Slice the pizza and enjoy.

Tandoori Chicken with Garlic Naan

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 2.5 lbs chicken drumsticks skin removed, about 8
  • 225 g plain Greek yogurt 1c
  • 75 g oil 1/3c, I’m using Avocado
  • 8 g salt 1 1/2t
  • 5 g turmeric 1 1/4t
  • 15 g garam masala 2T
  • 3 g cayenne pepper or Chile flakes 1t
  • 25 g smoked paprika 3T
  • 45 g garlic grated or pressed, 8 cloves
  • 30 g grated ginger 2T, about half a knob
  • 75 g lime juice 5T, juice from roughly 3 limes
  • 10 drops of red food coloring optional
  • 170 g beer slightly warmed to 90°F, 3/4c
  • 350 g all-purpose flour 3c
  • 5 g coarse salt 1t
  • 8 g granulated sugar 2t
  • 7 g instant yeast 2t
  • 15 g olive oil 1T
  • 75 g yogurt 1/3c
  • 125 g ghee or butter 2/3c
  • 30 g garlic minced, 6 cloves
  • 5-8 g fresh cilantro chopped, 2-3T
Instructions
  1. Remove the skin from drumsticks. Once skinned, score each drumstick 4 times on each side, cutting down to the bone.
  2. Generously season each drumstick with salt on both sides. Set aside.
  3. In a large mixing bowl, combine the yogurt, oil, 8g salt, turmeric, garam masala, cayenne, smoked paprika, grated garlic, grated ginger, lime juice, and optional red food coloring. Stir until smooth and lump-free.
  4. Add the seasoned drumsticks to the marinade, ensuring they are completely coated, especially into the slits. Cover the bowl and refrigerate for 30 minutes or up to 24 hours for deeper flavor.
  5. Preheat your grill to high heat. Brush the grates with an oil-soaked paper towel and spray with pan spray to create a non-stick surface.
  6. Place the marinated drumsticks onto the grill. Cook for 6-8 minutes on one side with the lid down. Lower the heat to medium, flip the drumsticks, and cook for another 12-15 minutes, until fully cooked through.
  7. During the last 5 minutes of cooking, brush the drumsticks with melted ghee or butter for extra flavor.
  8. In a small saucepan, heat the beer to about 90°F. Don’t overheat it.
  9. In the warmed beer, stir in the yeast, olive oil, and yogurt until combined. Some lumps of yogurt are fine.
  10. In a food processor, add the flour, salt, and sugar. Pulse until evenly combined.
  11. With the food processor running, drizzle in the beer-yogurt mixture. Continue processing until the dough forms a cohesive ball. NOTE: If mixing by hand, combine the ingredients in a bowl and knead.
  12. Transfer the dough to a floured surface and knead for about 2 minutes until it becomes elastic and smooth.
  13. Place the dough in a bowl, cover with a lid or damp towel, and let rest for 20 minutes.
  14. Divide the rested dough into 4 equal portions (about 150g each). Pre-shape each portion by folding the sides in and rounding into a ball. Let the dough relax for 10 minutes.
  15. On a floured surface, roll out each ball into a naan shape, about 10″ (25cm) in diameter and 1/8″ (.3cm) thick.
  16. In a small pan, melt the ghee or butter over medium-low heat. Add minced garlic and cook for about 30 seconds until fragrant, being careful not to burn. Stir in the chopped cilantro. Remove from heat.
  17. Heat a cast-iron skillet over medium-high heat. Place one rolled-out naan in the skillet and cook for about 90 seconds, until bubbles form and golden spots appear. Flip and cook for another 90 seconds to 2 minutes.
  18. Immediately after cooking, brush the naan with the garlic butter mixture. Repeat the process with the remaining naans.
  19. Store the cooked naan in a 200°F/90C oven, covered, to keep warm until serving with the tandoori chicken.

Tavern Style Chicago Thin Crust Pizza

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 360 g bread flour
  • 40 g cold butter
  • 180 g ice cold water divided in half
  • 5 g yeast
  • 7 g salt
  • 800 g can crushed tomatoes
  • 100 g tomato paste
  • 12 g salt
  • 20 g granulated sugar
  • 1 g chili flakes
  • 2 g dried basil
  • 2 g dried oregano
  • 2 g onion powder
  • 2 g garlic powder
  • 450 g 80/20 ground pork
  • 7 g salt
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 2 g chili flakes
  • 5 g red wine vinegar
  • 2 g ground fennel seed
  • 5 g fresh garlic minced
  • 450 g full-fat aged mozzarella cheese shredded
  • 1 lb italian sausage preferably homemade, recipe above
  • pizza sauce recipe above
  • dried oregano
  • dried basil
Instructions
  1. In a food processor, combine the bread flour and cold butter. Pulse until the butter is broken down into small, gravel-like bits.
  2. Dissolve the yeast in half of ice cold water and the salt in the other half of ice cold water.
  3. Add the yeast water and salt water to the flour-butter mixture in the food processor. Pulse for 20-30 seconds until the dough is evenly hydrated. Be careful not to overmix.
  4. Transfer the dough onto a work surface and knead for 2 minutes until it forms a homogeneous ball.
  5. Round the dough into a ball, place it in a bowl, cover, and ferment for 2 hours.
  6. After 2 hours, divide the dough into two equal pieces. Perform a preshape by folding in the sides and rounding off the dough into a ball. Cover the dough balls and let them rest for 20 minutes to relax the gluten.
  7. Press the dough ball flat with a pizza pan or large pot then roll out each dough ball into a 12″ (30cm) round
  8. Use a pizza docking tool or a fork to pierce the dough to prevent large bubbles from forming during baking.
  9. Spray 2 pieces of parchment paper with olive oil pan spray and a generous amount of fine grind cornmeal and place rolled out pizza doughs on top.
  10. Place both doughs stacked on a sheet tray and refrigerate, uncovered, for 4-24 hours to cold ferment and develop a skin for a crispier crust. Preferably 24 hours.
  11. Scoop out half a cup of crushed tomatoes from the can.
  12. Add the tomato paste, salt, sugar, chili flakes, basil, oregano, onion powder, and garlic powder to the remaining crushed tomatoes.
  13. Blend the sauce with immersion blender until the dried herbs are broken down and everything is well combined.
  14. Mix together all ingredients until well combined.
  15. Preheat the oven to 550°F (285°C) with a pizza steel or an upside-down sheet tray.
  16. Spread prepared pizza sauce evenly over the rolled dough.
  17. Distribute half of the Italian sausage in rough balls evenly on top of each pizza (1/2lb per pizza)
  18. Sprinkle half the shredded mozzarella cheese generously over the sausage of each pizza, spreading edge to edge (½ lb per pizza).
  19. Sprinkle each pizza with a pinch of dried oregano, dried basil, and a pinch of salt.
  20. Slide assembled pizza onto the preheated pizza steel or sheet tray in the oven.
  21. Bake for about 10 minutes until the crust is deeply baked and the cheese is bubbly and golden brown.
