The Best Classic Lasagna Recipe

Serves 8
Ingredients
  • 455 g fresh ricotta EASY recipe below or use nice store bought
  • 75 g grated parm
  • 100 g heavy cream
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 2 g black pepper
  • 1 egg
  • 2 g nutmeg
  • 2000 g whole milk
  • 200 g heavy cream
  • 15 g kosher salt
  • 100 g white distilled vinegar
  • 450 g 80/20 ground beef
  • 225 g Italian sausage
  • olive oil
  • 25-30 g unsalted butter
  • 200 g yellow onion diced
  • kosher salt
  • 20 g garlic minced
  • chile flakes
  • 1500 g crushed tomatoes canned
  • 5-7 g sugar
  • Meat sauce
  • 450 g no boil lasagna noodles or reg lasagna noodles soaked for 5 min in water before building
  • 450 g full fat mozzarella shredded
  • 100 g parmesan grated
  • 6-9 slices fresh mozzarella
Instructions
  1. Stir ingredients together.
  2. Prepare your “cheesecloth setup” by placeing 4 layers of cheesecloth in a colander with a bowl underneath (i use a salad spinner for this).
  3. Add milk, cream, and salt to a sauce pan and heat over medium for 10 minutes, whisking frequently until the temperature reaches 190°F/88°C. At this point, add vinegar. Very gently stir with spatula or whisk to combine. Allow milk/vinegar mixture to sit for 15 minutes to allow milk to break.
  4. At this point, the curds and whey should have separated. Gently pour this mixture into the cheesecloth setup then lift out cheesecloth and allow to drain for about 20 minutes. Now you have fresh ricotta.
  5. Spread ground beef and sausage meat flat onto a sheet tray and cook under the oven broiler for 15 minutes (or roast in oven at highest temp).
  6. While meat cooks, add 25-30g olive oil and butter into large heavy bottomed pot over medium high heat. When butter has melted, add the onion and a pinch of salt. Stir and cook onions until softened and translucent.
  7. Add garlic and chile flakes. Cook for about a minute until fragrant. Add crushed tomatoes and 10g salt and stir to combine. Simmer for about 5 minutes.
  8. Check broiling meat. When done, it should be browned and cooked through. Crumble into small pieces then add to tomato sauce along with any drippings. Stir and continue to cook for 20-30 minutes.
  9. When ready, sauce should have reduced by about half.
  10. To finish, taste for seasoning. Add a pinch more salt, if needed, and sugar to balance. Stir and taste again.
  11. Layer ingredients as follows into a 9×13 baking dish:
  12. ½ cup meat sauce spread, a single layer of lasagna noodles, 9 spoonfuls of the ricotta mixture, 1 cup shredded mozzarella, ¾ cup meat sauce, a layer of noodles, 9 spoons ricotta mix, 1 cup shredded mozzarella, 1 cup meat sauce, layer of noodles, 9 spoons ricotta mix, 1 cup shredded mozzarella, 1 cup meat sauce, layer of noodles, 1 cup meat sauce, 1 cup shredded mozzarella, grated parm, 6-9 slices of fresh mozzarella.
  13. Cover the baking dish tightly with oiled piece of foil and bake in preheated 375°F/190℃ oven for 25minutes.
  14. Remove foil and return to bake uncovered for 25 minutes. Finish by broiling for 2-3 minutes on high to brown the top. Allow to cool for 30 minutes before cutting and serving.

The Easiest Actually Good Pizza Dough – No Mixer

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 400 g warm water 86F/30C
  • 20 g extra virgin olive oil
  • 7 g instant yeast
  • 15 g salt
  • 20 g granulated sugar
  • 680 g all-purpose flour
  • 800 g crushed tomatoes canned
  • 100 g tomato paste
  • 12 g salt
  • 20 g sugar
  • 2 g dried basil
  • 2 g dried oregano
  • 1 g chili flake
  • 2 g garlic powder
  • 2 g onion powder
Instructions
  1. Mix warm water with olive oil, yeast, salt, and sugar then use a sturdy spoon to gradually add flour to the bowl and combine until flour has hydrated and has formed into a clump of dough.
  2. At this point, wet your hand and use it to continue mixing the dough. My preferred method is to grab and squeeze the dough repeatedly until ingredients are fully incorporated and dough is homogenous.
  3. Cover the bowl with a lid and let it ferment at room temperature for 30 minutes.
  4. Wet your hand again and perform a series of strength-building folds. To do this, grab a corner of the dough, stretch it out gently, and fold it back over itself. Repeat this process 4-5 times around the perimeter of the dough.
  5. After folding, flip the dough so the seams are underneath. Shape the dough into a tight ball by rounding and tucking the dough underneath to create surface tension on top. Cover and let sit at room temperature for 60 minutes.
  6. After the total fermentation time of 90 minutes, turn the dough out onto a lightly floured surface.
  7. Divide into four equal pieces, each weighing about 280g.
  8. Shape by folding the right and left sides into the center and pressing to create strength. Turn 90 degrees and repeat. Flip over onto those folds and roll into a ball to strengthen further.
  9. Spray a baking sheet tray with olive oil and place the pre-shaped dough balls on it. Cover and let them rest for 15 minutes.
  10. Preheat and pizza steel to 550℉ (285℃)
  11. To stretch the pizza, turn a dough ball out onto a lightly floured surface and stretch into a 10" round. Use your fingertips to press from the center out toward the edge. Then pick up the dough, letting gravity pull it down, flip and turn it on the back of your knuckles until you reach 10 inches.
  12. Dust a pizza peel with semolina then transfer the dough round to the peel. Sauce with a ladle of pizza sauce (leaving border for the crust), full fat mozzarella and toppings of your choice (I like pepperoni).
  13. Before loading pizza into the oven, gently stretch the pizza into approximately 12" on the peel. Shimmy the pizza to be sure it's not stuck to the peel. If it is, lift the edge where it's stuck and add more semolina to that spot.
  14. Bake on a pizza stone or steel in a preheated oven at 550°F (285°C) 9-10 minutes. Alternatively, bake for 10-12 minutes if baking in the cast iron.
  15. Pull pizza from the oven when the crust is golden brown and cheese is melted, bubbling, and beginning to brown. Cool on a wire rack for at least 60 seconds before cutting.
  16. Into a high sided container add crushed tomatoes, tomato paste, salt, sugar, basil, oregano, chili flake, garlic powder, and onion powder. Spin with immersion blender to break down until it's smooth, but still has texture.

The Greatest Roast Turkey Recipe Of All Time

Serves 12
Ingredients
  • 8 kg whole turkey about 17 lbs, adjust for larger or smaller birds
  • 4,000 g water
  • 400 g kosher salt
  • 100 g granulated sugar
  • 4-5 bay leaves
  • 3-4 heads of garlic halved
  • 1 clamshell poultry herb blend sage, rosemary, thyme
  • 4,000 g ice
  • 200 g water for roasting pan
  • olive oil for brushing
  • turkey neck,wings, and other meaty bones
  • 125 g yellow onion roughly chopped, ½ large
  • 75 g celery roughly chopped, 2 stalks
  • 75 g carrot roughly chopped, 1 large
  • 225 g water for deglazing
  • 1500 g chicken stock
  • 3 g fresh thyme
  • 2-3 bay leaves
  • 115 g butter
  • 115 g all-purpose flour
Instructions
  1. Thaw turkey in the fridge 3-4 days, or in cold water for about 8 hours. Once thawed, remove anything from the inside of the turkey. Detach the wings. Save wings and neck for the gravy.
  2. In a large pot, combine water, salt, sugar, bay, garlic, & herbs. Bring to a boil, simmer for 10 minutes, then pour over ice to cool.
  3. Use a brine injector to inject brine into the breast (5-6 injections per side) and each leg (2-3 injections).
  4. Next use the injector tip of the brine injector to pierce the turkey 40-50 times per breast and 20-30 times per leg. You’re only piercing to create channels for brine during the soak. Submerge turkey, breast down, in a brining bucket. I like using this 12qt cambro container. Refrigerate 3 days.
  5. Preheat oven to 425°F/218°C. In a roasting pan with rack, lay down a few sheets of folded tin foil and grease or spray foil with pan spray. Pour a cup of water into the bottom of the roasting pan (to prevent burning). Lay turkey breast side down on top of foil. If using wireless probe thermometer, insert 1 into breast & 1 into leg thigh, careful not to touch bone. Cover with 2 layers of foil.
  6. Roast in the oven for 1 hour. Remove foil cover, reduce oven to 325°F/163°C and continue roasting for 90-120 minutes or until the breast reaches 150°F/65°C and the thigh is 165°F/74°C
  7. Tent the turkey with foil & rest for 1 hour. Pour off drippings to use for gravy then flip the turkey on the roasting rack to face breast side up. Truss drumsticks with kitchen twine. Brush the breast skin with olive oil. Roast again, uncovered, at 500°F/260°C for 20 minutes to brown the skin.
  8. Break down wings into 3 pieces. Cut the neck into similar size pieces
  9. Preheat oven to 500°F/260°C, place turkey bones, onion, celery, and carrot on a sheet tray. Roast for 20 minutes until browned.
  10. Move roasted bones & veggies to a saucepan. Deglaze sheet tray with water, scraping up fond, then add fond liquid to the sauce pan. Add chicken stock, thyme, and bay leaves. Simmer for 45 minutes. Strain and set aside.
  11. Melt butter in a saucepan, then whisk in flour over medium heat until smooth, cooking for 1 minute.
  12. Gradually whisk in the stock you just made. Simmer, stirring, for 5-10 minutes until thickened to coat the back of a spoon. Don't salt until after you add the turkey drippings since they are likely already salty.
  13. Reheat the gravy base (if needed), add turkey drippings to taste. Careful, drippings can be salty! Simmer over medium to medium high heat, stirring often, to thicken if needed.
  14. Man’s Pro Tip: Strain finished gravy through a fine mesh strainer for a smoother finish.
Full recipe →

The Party Sub

Serves 8
Ingredients
  • Mortadella
  • Salami
  • Prosciutto or thin ham if you can’t find prosciutto
  • 15 g whole grain mustard
  • 50 g red wine vinegar
  • 5 g honey
  • 2 g salt
  • 1 g black pepper
  • 1 g dried basil
  • 1 g dried oregano
  • 150 g extra virgin olive oil
  • 150 mayo
  • 50 g roasted red bell pepps
  • 30 g calabrian chilis
  • 5 g garlic
  • 240 g warm water 86F/30C
  • 9 g instant yeast
  • 10 g sugar
  • 20 g olive oil
  • 360 g bread flour
  • 9 g salt
Instructions
  1. Combine all ingredients except oil, then slowly stream in oil while stirring. At this point i like to add the dressing to a lidded container and give it a good shake to emulsify.
  2. Blend ingredients with immersion or standard blender. Alternately, hand mince all ingredients then stir into mayo.
  3. Add ingredients to a mixer with dough hook and mix on low for about 3 minutes or until the dough is combined into a mass. Increase speed to high and mix for another 4-5 minutes. The dough is fully mixed when you tug it and it doesn’t shear or tear.
  4. Alternatively If mixing by hand, add ingredients to a bowl and stir with a sturdy spoon for as long as the dough will allow you to mix it with a spoon. From there, switch to a soaking wet hand and squeeze and turn the dough with your hand then knead dough until it forms into a smooth ball.
  5. Transfer dough to a bowl and round into a taught ball. Cover and ferment at room temp for 2 hours.
  6. After the 2 hours, the dough should have risen by about double. Transfer dough to a well floured work surface then degas to remove large air pockets. Fold and preshape dough. Cover dough with bowl or damp towel to rest for 25 minutes.
  7. Shape dough into a "party" sized loaf. Transfer to a parchment lined sheet tray, seam side down. Cover with another sheet tray or damp towel to proof for 60-75mins.
  8. After proofing, the loaf should have risen by about 2x. A properly proofed dough will slowly spring back when poked.
  9. Score loaf longways down the center of the loaf. Spray loaf generously with water then transfer to a preheated 375F/190C oven. Spray loaf once more then quickly close oven door and bake for 30-35 mins.
  10. After baking, brush the top of the loaf with melted butter. If you don’t build the sub immediately after baking, refresh bread in a 375F/190C oven for 5-8mins to crisp the crust before making the sandwich.
  11. To build, slice sub horizontally (parallel to the cutting board) then layer as follows:
  12. 5-6 spoonfuls of chopped giardiniera – press it lightly into the bread
  13. Mortadella – ribboned onto the sandwich not tightly stacked
  14. Salami – again, ribboned
  15. Prosciutto – ribboned!
  16. Shredded lettuce – about 3 large handfuls. Press down lightly
  17. Shaved red onions
  18. Vinaigrette, several spoonfuls drizzled all over the sandwich
  19. A layer of thinly sliced provolone
  20. On the top of the sub roll, spread a generous amount of calabrian mayo Cut and serve. I like to use long picks to keep the masterpiece held together.

Tlyuda

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 300 g all-purpose flour
  • 6 g kosher salt
  • 50 g olive oil
  • 180 g cool water
  • 425 g canned black beans (2 15oz cans, one drained, one undrained)
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 10 g cumin
  • 10 g onion powder
  • 5 g garlic powder
  • 450 g Mexican chorizo
  • 12-15 cherry tomatoes halved
  • 2-3 radishes thinly sliced
  • ¼ head red cabbage shredded
  • 1-2 avocados thinly sliced
  • 200 g sour cream thinned with a little water
  • Chihuahua cheese shredded
  • taco or hot sauce
  • fresh cilantro chopped
Instructions
  1. In a food processor, combine the flour, salt, and olive oil. Pulse about 10 times until the mixture is gravelly.
  2. With the processor running, slowly add the cool water through the top until the dough begins to form a clump.
  3. Turn the dough out onto a lightly floured surface and knead for about 3 minutes until it’s elastic and can withstand a firm tug without tearing.
  4. Divide the dough into 6 equal pieces, roughly 80-90 grams each, and roll them into balls. Cover with a damp towel and let rest for 15 minutes.
  5. In the same food processor (no need to clean after the dough), add in the canned black beans, salt, cumin, onion powder, and garlic powder.
  6. Process until the mixture is smooth and spreadable. Adjust seasoning to taste after testing.
  7. In a medium heat pan, crumble the chorizo and cook for 8-10 minutes until it is slightly dry and crumbly, with some browning occurring. Set aside to cool.
  8. On a floured surface, take one dough ball and flatten it with your hands. Use a rolling pin to roll the dough into a 10-12 inch circle, making sure to keep the surface and dough well-floured to prevent sticking.
  9. Transfer the rolled-out dough onto parchment paper lightly sprayed with olive oil.
  10. Spread three large spoonfuls of bean sauce over the dough, leaving a small border around the edge.
  11. Evenly distribute a portion of the chorizo and arrange the cherry tomato halves on top.
  12. Preheat your oven to 500°F (260°C). Slide the parchment paper with the tlyuda onto a pizza peel and then onto your oven’s baking steel/stone or just bake on a baking sheet.
  13. Bake for about 10 minutes until the edges are crisp and the toppings are nicely browned.
  14. After baking, top with shredded cheese, shredded cabbage, avocado slices, radish slices, and drizzles of thinned sour cream and taco sauce.
  15. Finish with a sprinkle of chopped cilantro

Tonkotsu Ramen

Serves 4
Ingredients
  • 2250 g pork neck bones 2 1/4kg
  • piece of pork trotter about the size of a fist
  • piece of pork fat back or pork belly about the size of a fist
  • water
  • 30 g kombu
  • 30 g bonito flake
  • 30 g salted dried anchovies whole
  • 30 g dried shitake whole
  • 500 g light soy sauce
  • 5 g sugar
  • 1500 g water
  • 1500 g boneless pork shoulder
  • cooking twine optional
  • 3000 g water
  • 1 knob ginger 2 inches/5cm, sliced
  • 500 g soy sauce
  • 450 g bread flour
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 8 g baked soda spread on parchment'd sheet tray, baked 225F/105c, 1 hour
  • tiny pinch of egg yellow food coloring
  • 160 g water
  • 6 eggs
  • 50 g water
  • 50 g mirin
  • 50 g soy sauce
  • 75 g canola
  • 10 g sesame oil
  • 10 g garlic minced
  • 10 g ginger grated
  • 30 g gochujang
  • 5 g paprika
  • 50 g mirin
  • 10 g honey
  • 200 g tare
  • 350 g pork broth
  • 1 portion of ramen noodles
  • Marinated egg
  • Sliced scallions
  • Aroma oil
Instructions
  1. Add pork parts to large stock pot, cover with water, bring to a boil then drain water from pork & rinse bones. Add rinsed bones to pressure cooker & top with 5000g/5L water. Cook, high pressure for 80 mins.
  2. Return broth & bones to large stock pot & bring to boil for 30 min until broth has taken on an opaque quality & has reduced by 25-30%. Strain out solids.
  3. Combine the tare ingredients in a stock pot and bring to a simmer. Once simmering, remove from the heat and steep for 1 hour. Strain the liquid and set aside.
  4. Tie pork into a roast. Sear meat on all sides in heavy bottomed pot or dutch oven over med-high heat. When roast has been seared, add water, sliced ginger, & soy sauce. Bring to simmer, reduce heat to low, cover, & continue to cook for 90-100 min.
  5. When finished, texture should be soft, but not shreddable. Allow to cool in liquid then wrap & refrigerate.
  6. Add flour, salt, & baked soda to food processor. In separate container, mix food coloring into water. With processor running, stream in yellow water. Dough will be dry & pebbly. Allow ito sit for 30 min to hydrate. Press together into a mass. Cut into 4 strips then link together into 1 long belt. Roll flat & wide into a shape that fits through a pasta roller. I’m using the kitchen aid pasta attachment.
  7. Feed dough through on the widest setting, fold in half & repeat. Reduce roller thickness, feed pasta through, & repeat several times until the dough turns into a more smooth, pasta looking sheet. Fold dough in half, increase roller thickness, & repeat several more times until you get a smooth sheet on #6 on the roller.
  8. Cut this long sheet into 12″/30cm sheets then drop each through a spaghetti cutter (or hand cut). Measure into four, 5oz portions. Cover & refrigerate until ramen time.
  9. Boil eggs 7 minutes then drain & transfer to ice bath. Once cooled, peel & move to a food storage bag with water, mirin, & soy sauce. Press out air & marinate for 4-24 hours.
  10. Combine oils, garlic, ginger in sauce pot over med heat until sizzling & fragrant. Stir in gochujang & paprika. Cook for 30 seconds and add in the mirin and the honey. Transfer the oil to a high sided container & spin with an immersion blender until smooth.
  11. Bring a pot of salted water to a boil.
  12. In a separate sauce pot, add the tare and broth and bring them to a boil. Remove the broth from the heat and blend with an immersion blender until creamy. Add in two slices of the pork chashu to heat through.
  13. Cook the noodles in the boiling water for 90 seconds.
  14. Pour the hot, creamy broth into a warm bowl. Add your cooked noodles, top with warmed pork, a halved egg, scallions and a dab of aroma oil.

Tuna Noodle Casserole

Serves 6
Ingredients
  • 350 g egg noodles
  • 150 g yellow onion diced, ½ of 1 large
  • 175 g sliced mushrooms
  • 145 g unsalted butter
  • 15 g garlic minced
  • 75 g all-purpose flour
  • 600 g chicken stock
  • 15 g Better Than Bouillon chicken paste or sub with chicken bouillon powder
  • 10 g kosher salt
  • 15 g Dijon mustard
  • 150 g heavy cream
  • 100 g cream cheese
  • 450 g tuna 4 cans, preferably chunkier varieties like Wild Planet or Starkist
  • 200 g frozen peas
  • 100 g shredded medium cheddar cheese
  • 50 g grated parmesan cheese
  • Black pepper about 20 cranks
  • 40-50 g olive oil for fried panko topping
  • 60 g panko bread crumbs
  • 50 g olive oil
  • panko breadcrumbs pinch of salt
Instructions
  1. Bring 4 quarts of water to a boil. Salt the water and cook the egg noodles until slightly al dente (about 1 minute less than package instructions). Drain off the noodles, coat them with a little olive oil, and set aside.
  2. In a large pot, melt your butter. Add in the diced onion, garlic, and a pinch of salt. Cook until the onions soften, about 3-4 minutes.
  3. Add mushrooms with another pinch of salt. Sauté until mushrooms are softened and lightly browned, about 4 minutes.
  4. Stir in flour and cook for 1 minute. Stir in chicken stock, then add bouillon, salt, Dijon, heavy cream, and cream cheese. Stir until smooth and velvety.
  5. Add cooked noodles, tuna (crumbled), frozen peas, cheddar, parmesan, and black pepper. Fold together, adding 30-40g (2-3tbsp) of water if it looks too dry.
  6. Transfer to a 9×13 casserole dish. Top with fried panko and additional grated parmesan. See instructions for fried panko below.
  7. Bake at 425°F/200°C for 20-25 minutes until golden and bubbly.
  8. Allow the casserole to sit for 10 minutes before eating.
  9. Heat saute pan over medium high and add 50g or 4tbsp olive oil.
  10. Once shimmering, add breadcrumbs. Toss to combine. Add pinch of salt.
  11. Continue stirring and tossing while cooking to a nice golden brown for about 60 seconds. Keep a close eye on these. They brown very quickly.
  12. Remove from pan and allow to dry and cool on a towel.

Turkey Club Sandwich

Serves 10
Ingredients
  • 2.5 kg turkey breast
  • 4000 g water
  • 400 g kosher salt
  • 40 g sugar
  • 40 g white distilled vinegar
  • 40 g sugar
  • 5 g kosher salt
  • 10 g mustard powder
  • 2 g sweet paprika
  • 150 g mayo
  • 150-160 g cooked egg yolks 12 large egg yolks
  • 500 g bacon
  • ripe tomato thinly sliced
  • green leaf lettuce
  • bread slices homemade or store-bought, sliced to 1/2-inch thickness
  • 130 g warm water 86°F/30°C
  • 5 g instant yeast
  • 1 egg
  • 45 g melted butter not hot
  • 45 g granulated sugar
  • 100 g roasted and riced russet potatoes about 1/2-2/3 of 1 potato, peel, wrap in foil and roast at 400°F/205°C for 1 hour
  • 430 g ap flour
  • 10 g kosher salt
Instructions
  1. In a large container, combine 4000/4L grams of water, 400 grams of salt, and 40 grams of sugar.
  2. Whisk until the salt and sugar are dissolved.
  3. Submerge the turkey breast in the brine and refrigerate for 12 to 48 hours.
  4. Preheat the oven to 275°F (135°C).
  5. Remove the turkey breast from the brine and place it on a wire rack set on a half sheet tray.
  6. Roast the turkey breast in the preheated oven for approximately 2 hours or until an instant read thermometer reads an internal temp of 147-150°F (64-66°C).
  7. Remove from the oven and let it rest.
  8. Remove the skin and slice as thinly as possible with a slicer knife before layering onto your sandwich
  9. Boil 12 eggs in a pot of gently boiling water for 12 minutes.
  10. Pour off hot water and add a lot of ice to cool eggs down for about 5 minutes
  11. Peel the eggs and separate the yolks.
  12. In a tall container, combine cooked yolks, vinegar, sugar, salt, mustard powder, paprika, and mayonnaise.
  13. Use an immersion blender to blend the ingredients until smooth and well combined. Use a blender if you don’t have an immersion/hand blender.
  14. Lay out a pound of bacon on a wire rack placed on a half sheet tray.
  15. Bake in a 375°F (190°C) oven for 25-30 minutes or until the bacon is crispy and fully rendered.
  16. Remove from the oven and set aside.
  17. Lightly toast bread slices in a toaster for 45 to 60 seconds (homemade bread recipe below)
  18. Spread a tablespoon of Famous Sauce on the bottom slice of bread.
  19. Layer green leaf lettuce and a thin tomato slice on top.
  20. Season tomato with salt and pepper.
  21. Add shingled bacon slices and approximately 3 ounces of sliced turkey breast.
  22. Spread plain mayonnaise on the middle slice of bread and place it on top.
  23. Repeat the layers of Famous Sauce, lettuce, tomato, salt, pepper, bacon, and turkey on the middle slice.
  24. Generously spread plain mayo on the top slice of bread to act as a glue and provide extra moisture.
  25. Press the sandwich firmly together and secure it with sandwich picks.
  26. Cut the sandwich diagonally with a sharp knife, ensuring the picks hold it together. I recommend serving with ruffles and a crunchy dill pickle on the side
  27. Combine ingredients in the bowl of a stand mixer using the dough hook attachment, mix on medium for 4 minutes. Increase speed to high and mix for an additional 7-8 minutes. Dough should be clearing the bowl and pass the tug test when done.
  28. Round dough into a taught ball and place in a bowl. Cover bowl and ferment at room temp for 2 hours.
  29. Prepare a 1 ½ lb loaf pan with oil or pan spray.
  30. Flip dough onto lightly floured surface and press to De-gas. Fold each side into the center of the dough to create a square shape and then roll it into itself to create a tube shape and place it into pan, seam side down. Mist it with water cover with a towel. Proof at room temperature for 60-90minutes.
  31. Mist loaf well with water then score top with bread lame or sharp knife.
  32. Load into preheated 400°F/205°C oven, spritz oven well with 12-15 spritzes of water, and bake for 30 minutes.

Vegetarian Chili

Serves 8
Ingredients
  • 250 g white onion medium diced
  • 100 g poblano medium diced
  • 15 g garlic minced/pressed
  • Olive oil
  • Salt
  • 25 g tomato paste
  • 40 g chipotle peppers in adobo small diced, about 2
  • 20 g chili powder
  • 15 g smoked paprika
  • 15 g cumin
  • 10 g cocoa
  • 500 g veggie stock + extra to thin chili if needed
  • 800 g diced, canned tomatoes
  • 800 g crushed, canned tomatoes
  • 200 g red lentils
  • 850 g canned black beans drained and rinsed
  • 850 g canned kidney beans with juices
  • 225 g frozen corn
  • 15 g soy sauce
  • 15 g brown sugar
  • 15 g apple cider vinegar
  • 15 g hot sauce
Instructions
  1. Heat large heavy bottomed pot over med high with a long squeeze of olive oil followed by onion, poblano, and a pinch of salt. Stir and saute until softened and onions are becoming translucent (2-3min).
  2. Add garlic, stir and cook until fragrant. Add tomato paste, chipotle peppers, and spices. Stir and cook for about 30sec.
  3. Add 500g veggie stock and tomatoes and stir well to combine (including scraping the bottom to prevent burning).
  4. Add lentils, stir, and bring to a boil. Reduce heat to medium low, cover, and simmer for 15-20min, stirring occasionally, until lentils are cooked through.
  5. Add beans and corn, stir. Add a bit more veggie stock if needed to thin the chili. Cover (lid cracked) and bring to a simmer for 10 min.
  6. Off heat, add soy sauce, brown sugar, vinegar, hot sauce, and pinch of salt to taste. Stir, taste, and adjust for seasoning (i typically add a little more salt).

Weeknight Steak Fajitas

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 1.5 lbs Top Sirloin Roast .7kg
  • 250 g red or orange bell peppers 2 med-lrg, julienned
  • 125 g poblano 1lrg, julienned
  • 250 g white onion 1 , sliced pole to pole
  • 10-15 g garlic 4 cloves
  • 2 g or 1tsp ground cumin
  • 2 g or 1tsp chili powder
  • 2 g 1tsp paprika
  • 1 g or 1tsp dried oregano
  • Lime halved
  • 1 bunch of fresh cilantro chopped
  • High smoke point oil avocado, canola, etc
  • Salt
  • Pepper
Instructions
  1. Cut roast in half, then cut pieces in half horizontally so they’re thin (see video @:51). Salt generously with salt and pepper on both sides.
  2. Preheat cast iron over high and add a lot of oil (4Tbsp or so). Dab any excess moisture from meat then place into hot pan. Press each steak into pan and allow to cook for 90 sec total on side 1.
  3. Flip and cook for an additional 60-90 sec. Remove beef from pan to rest.
  4. Wipe out excess oil in pan and add peppers and onion followed by large pinch of salt. Stir to combine then cook for about 45-60 seconds, undisturbed. Stir and repeat 2-3 times until you’ve cooked the veg for about 5 min until softened slightly and beginning to char.
  5. Add spices and garlic and stir for a few seconds to take the raw edge off the garlic. Transfer fajita veg mix to a bowl.
  6. Slice beef against the grain then add to fajita veg mix along with any resting juices, juice of ½ lime, and 1-2 pinches fresh chopped cilantro.
  7. Reheat beef and veg in a pan over medium just to heat through.
  8. Place on fresh warmed tortilla and top with whatever you like. I use fresh tomatillo salsa, queso fresco, and fresh cilantro.

“Fancy” Baked Mac & Cheese

Serves 0
Ingredients
  • 150 g or 1 1/4c Pecorino grated + extra for topping
  • 250 g or 2 1/4c Aged gruyere grated + extra for topping
  • 250 g or about a cup Taleggio cut into chunks (sub brie or camembert for less stinky cheese option)
  • 80 g or about 6Tbsp butter
  • 60 g or 1/2c ap flour
  • 1200 g or 5c whole milk
  • 225 g or 1/2c mascarpone
  • 2 g or 1tsp black pepper
  • 5 g or 1tsp salt
  • Pinch of nutmeg less than a tsp
  • 1 lb or 450g macaroni
Instructions
  1. Melt butter into a sauce pan over high heat. Whisk in flour.
  2. When mixture (roux) begins to turn golden, whisk in the milk. Bring to a simmer, whisking often.
  3. Once simmering, reduce heat to low and whisk in pecorino, followed by gruyere, then taleggio.
  4. When all cheeses are melted stir in mascarpone until combined.
  5. Remove from heat and whisk in pepper, salt, and nutmeg. Add a lid to the melted cheese mixture to prevent a skin from forming.
  6. Add macaroni into well salted boiling water and cook per package instructions or for 5min until al dente.
  7. Drain off water, add macaroni into a large bowl and stir in cheese mixture.
  8. Transfer macaroni cheese mix into a 9”x13” (23x33cm) baking dish, smooth down the top, and cover with additional grated pecorino and gruyere.
  9. Bake at 375F/190C for 35-40 minutes until cheeses are melted and top is toasty and golden. Allow to rest for a few minutes before serving.

65m

“Spicy Tuna Roll” Chicken Salad

Serves 2
Ingredients
  • 450 g chicken breast
  • 50 g cucumbers small diced
  • kosher salt
  • sugar
  • 400 g cooked chicken cubed
  • 175 g Kewpie mayonnaise
  • 30 g scallions chopped
  • 40 g sushi ginger chopped, squeeze out excess liquid
  • 30 g white miso
  • 5 g soy sauce
  • 20 g furikake
  • 30 g sriracha
  • 10 g sugar
  • 10 g rice vinegar
  • 2-3 g sesame oil
Instructions
  1. To cook the chicken, salt well on both sides then allow to sit and dry brine on a wire rack for around 15-20 minutes.
  2. Boil a pot of water, then reduce the heat to low until water temp reaches 180°F/82°C (to test water temp, I just use my meat thermometer)
  3. Stir a very large handful of salt into the water then gently drop in the chicken breasts. This should drop the water temp to about 165°F/75°C. Poach for 20 minutes at a temp of 165°F/75°C.
  4. When cooked, the internal temp of the chicken should read 150-155°F/65-68°C.
  5. Season the diced cucumbers with a pinch of salt and a pinch of sugar and let them cure and release their liquid for 15 minutes. Drain off the liquid.
  6. In a stand mixer bowl, combine the drained cucumbers, chopped chicken breast, mayo, scallions, sushi ginger (chopped with excess liquid squeezed off well), white miso, soy sauce, furikake, sriracha, sugar, rice vinegar, and sesame oil.
  7. Mix on high speed for about 30-45 seconds until ingredients are combined and chicken is somewhat shredded.
  8. If you don’t have a stand mixer, chop the chicken down well by hand, then add to a bowl and mix/whip well by hand, making sure to shred some of the chicken as you mix.
  9. Serve on a squishy toasted brioche bun
